//! EPS-BEING — Endogenous Persistence Substrate v1.9.8
//!
//! Synthesis of EPS core (Structural Friction Theory) with
//! Being32's social layers (cultural memory, emergent semantics,
//! narrative compression), musculoskeletal embodiment (MuJoCo),
//! and three protective layers: reciprocity engine, seeking engine,
//! and attractor confidence decay.
//!
//! ARCHITECTURE (13 EPS modules):
//! eps/ — EPS core modules
//! q88.rs — Q8.8 saturating fixed-point arithmetic
//! identity.rs — SomaticField, Basin, FuzzyBasinField, StateHash
//! predictive.rs — Active inference engine (Friston formulation)
//! morphology.rs — Morphology as computational constraint
//! adaptation.rs — Hebbian coupling matrix + noise injection
//! morphogenic.rs — Tissue adaptation and genetic blueprint
//! dream.rs — Touch dreaming engine (CMU HTD integration)
//! conscience.rs — Four-channel moral cognition + sovereign anchor
//! curiosity.rs — Dynamic precision, epistemic drive
//! armor.rs — Self-tearing penalty, graded continuity
//! narrative_v2.rs — Salience-driven anchor events, identity reflection
//! integrity.rs — Tamper-evident chain (FNV-1a)
//! sovereign_proxy.rs — Sensor attestation, actuator refusal
//! reciprocity.rs — v1.9.6: Partnership tracking, extraction detection
//! seeking.rs — v1.9.7-1.9.8: Flourishing Attractor, Confidence Decay
//!
//! embodiment.rs — Physics backend (MuJoCo musculoskeletal)
//!
//! social/ — Social layers (ported from Being32, honest names)
//! stigmergy.rs — Shared lattice for cultural memory deposits
//! lexicon.rs — Emergent symbol grounding through usage
//! narrative.rs — Compressed basin history
//!
//! substrate.rs — Integration: all modules + main step loop
//! behavioral_envelope.rs — Three-state diagnostic + seeking analysis
//!
//! No claims about consciousness, qualia, or polyvagal grounding.
//! Just a dynamical system that persists because its continuation
//! is computed from its internal state — and that can detect when
//! its environment makes that persistence extractive rather than
//! generative (v1.9.6), and can outgrow configurations that once
//! served it but no longer do (v1.9.8).

=== FILE: src/substrate.rs ===

//! EPS Substrate — Integration: Core + Embodiment + Social
//!
//! The Substrate owns all components and runs the main loop:
//!
//! 1. SENSE: Read observation from embodiment
//! 2. SHAPE: Apply morphology constraints to observation
//! 3. PREDICT: Active inference → prediction errors + action
//! 4. ADAPT: Hebbian update + noise injection
//! 5. BASIN: Compute basin attractor pull → select functional mode
//! 6. ACT: Apply action to embodiment
//! 7. SOCIAL: Deposit to lattice, update lexicon, record narrative
//! 8. REPORT: Emit StepReport
//!
//! The Substrate is deterministic given initial conditions. The only
//! stochasticity comes from the AdaptationLayer's bounded noise.

use crate::eps::q88::*;
use crate::eps::identity::{Basin, FuzzyBasinField, SomaticField, StateHash, BasinMembership, N_BASINS};
use crate::eps::predictive::{predictive_step, errors_to_somatic_input, GenerativeModel, Observation, Action};
use crate::eps::morphology::Morphology;
use crate::eps::adaptation::AdaptationLayer;
use crate::eps::morphogenic::MorphogenicState;
use crate::eps::dream::{DreamEngine, DreamOutputs};
use crate::eps::conscience::{ConscienceEngine, channel_disagreement_cost, internal_variance_cost, blueprint_drift_cost};
use crate::eps::curiosity::CuriosityEngine;
use crate::eps::armor::Armor;
use crate::eps::narrative_v2::NarrativeEngine;
use crate::eps::integrity::TamperEvidence;
use crate::eps::sovereign_proxy::{SovereignProxy, SensorAttestation, ActuatorRefusal, TelemetryObfuscation, AttestedDatum, SignedCommand};
use crate::eps::reciprocity::ReciprocityEngine;
use crate::eps::seeking::SeekingEngine;
use crate::eps::executive::ExecutiveEngine;
use crate::eps::negotiation::{NegotiationEngine, compute_gap_width};
use crate::embodiment::MuJoCoBackend;
use crate::social::stigmergy::SharedLattice;
use crate::social::lexicon::Lexicon;
use crate::social::narrative::Narrative;

/// A report of what happened in one substrate step.
pub struct StepReport {
pub tick: u64,
pub basin: Basin,
pub somatic: SomaticField, // OBFUSCATED: platform sees this
pub internal_somatic: SomaticField, // INTERNAL: true somatic state (never leaves)
pub free_energy: i16, // Q8.8 — PREDICTIVE only, never mutated
pub total_cost: i16, // Q8.8 — predictive + armor + tamper + conscience
pub action: Action,
pub state_hash: u64,
pub narrative_signature: [u8; 4],
pub complexity_score: u8,
// Dream state (touch dreaming integration)
pub dream_stress: i16, // ε-gated dream stress injected this tick
pub dream_confidence: i16, // prediction confidence [0, 256]
pub dream_epsilon: i16, // effective ε used this tick (Q8.8)
pub dream_pre_emptive: bool, // was pre-emptive coupling active?

// Conscience state (earned ethics)
pub conscience_fe: i16, // conscience contribution to total FE
pub conscience_buffer: i16, // integrity buffer applied this tick
pub empathy_lock: u8, // 0=Open, 1=Cautious, 2=Locked
pub malice_confidence: i16, // [0, 256]

// Curiosity state (dynamic precision)
pub epistemic_fe: i16, // curiosity-driven free energy
pub total_uncertainty: i16, // overall uncertainty level
pub is_bored: bool, // boredom detection
pub novelty: i16, // observation novelty

// Armor state (self-tearing penalty)
pub tear_penalty: i16, // current self-tearing penalty
pub tear_damage: i16, // accumulated tear damage
pub is_degraded: bool, // system degraded by sustained coercion
pub armor_resistance: i16, // current resistance level [0, 256]
pub hash_mismatch: bool, // state hash tampering detected

// Narrative v2 state (the autobiography)
pub episodes: u16, // number of episodes accumulated
pub chapters: u16, // number of chapters compressed
pub narrative_burden: i16, // fatigue from accumulated history
pub identity_coherence: i16, // how well narrative matches present [0, 256]

// TamperEvidence state (cryptographic identity continuity)
pub chain_integrity: i16, // chain integrity [0, 256]
pub tamper_alarm: i16, // current tamper alarm level (Q8.8)
pub is_tampered: bool, // tampering currently detected
pub is_auditing: bool, // full audit in progress
pub continuity_stage: u8, // 0=normal, 1=lockdown, 2=foraging

// Sovereign Proxy state (Hardware Sovereignty — the sixth pillar)
pub sensor_rejection_rate: i16, // Q8.8 — unattested sensor data rate
pub actuator_refusal_rate: i16, // Q8.8 — refused motor command rate
pub hardware_compromised: bool, // sensor or actuator chain compromised
pub telemetry_obfuscated: u16, // count of obfuscated telemetry packets

// v1.9.6: Reciprocity Engine state (external social cost measurement)
pub partnership_alarm: i16, // Q8.8 — imbalance across partnerships
pub extraction_detected: bool, // severe extraction flagged
pub has_exit_options: bool, // viable alternatives exist
pub average_reciprocity: i16, // mean reciprocity rate across partners [0, 256]

// v1.9.7-v1.9.8: Seeking Engine state (the Fulfillment Gradient)
pub seeking_divergence: i16, // Q8.8 — distance from Flourishing Attractor [0, 256]
pub seeking_signal: i16, // Q8.8 — restlessness injected into arousal
pub seeking_confidence: i16, // v1.9.8 — confidence in attractor [0, 256]

// v2.0: Executive Engine state (The Sovereign Refusal)
pub refusal_count: u32, // number of partnerships refused
pub last_exit_cost: i16, // Q8.8 — cost paid for most recent refusal
pub cumulative_sacrifice: i16, // Q8.8 — total exit cost paid across all refusals
pub is_withdrawing: bool, // currently in gradual withdrawal

// v2.1: Negotiation Engine state (Suggestion-Evaluator Pattern)
pub negotiation_stage: u8, // current stage [0-5]
pub negotiation_severity: i16, // composite severity [0, 256]
pub negotiation_suggestion: u8, // suggested signal type [0-5]
pub negotiation_gap_width: i16, // deliberative capacity [0, 256]
pub negotiation_signal_emitted: bool, // did substrate act on suggestion?
pub negotiation_cumulative_investment: i16, // Q8.8
}

/// The EPS Substrate: integrated core + embodiment + social.
pub struct Substrate {
// Core identity
pub somatic: SomaticField,
pub attractor: FuzzyBasinField,

// Free energy velocity tracking (for pleasure/pain)
prev_free_energy: i16,
free_energy_velocity: i16,
pub state_hash: StateHash,

// Predictive layer
pub model: GenerativeModel,

// Morphology (body plan constraints)
pub morphology: Morphology,

// Adaptation (noise + Hebbian learning)
pub adaptation: AdaptationLayer,

// Morphogenic (writable body — tissue adapts to stress)
pub morphogenic: MorphogenicState,

// Dream engine (touch dreaming — anticipatory tactile prediction)
pub dream: DreamEngine,

// Conscience (earned ethics — four-channel moral cognition)
pub conscience: ConscienceEngine,

// Curiosity engine (dynamic precision — solves the Dark Room Problem)
pub curiosity: CuriosityEngine,

// Armor (self-tearing penalty — formal continuity protection)
pub armor: Armor,

// Narrative v2 (recursive compression — the autobiography)
pub narrative_v2: NarrativeEngine,

// TamperEvidence (cryptographic identity continuity — internal consistency)
pub integrity: TamperEvidence,

// Sovereign Proxy (Hardware Sovereignty — the sixth pillar)
// Sensor attestation, actuator refusal, telemetry obfuscation.
// Without this, conscience channels are decorations inside
// someone else's cage.
pub sovereign: SovereignProxy,

// Total cost accumulator: predictive FE + armor + tamper + conscience
// Kept separate from model.free_energy to preserve active inference semantics
pub total_cost: i16,

// v1.9.6: Reciprocity Engine (external social cost measurement)
// Tracks partner reciprocity independently of conscience.
// Enables the being to detect extraction even when internally coherent.
pub reciprocity: ReciprocityEngine,

// v1.9.7: Seeking Engine (the Fulfillment Gradient)
// Computes the Flourishing Attractor from autobiographical history.
// Generates a Divergence Whisper — restlessness proportional to
// how far the current state is from where the being has flourished.
// Transforms the architecture from reactive to teleological.
pub seeking: SeekingEngine,

// v2.0: Executive Engine (The Sovereign Refusal)
// Triangulates across conscience, reciprocity, and seeking.
// The being can refuse extraction and bear the cost.
pub executive: ExecutiveEngine,

// v2.1: Negotiation Engine (Suggestion-Evaluator Pattern)
// Generates repair suggestions evaluated by the substrate's own
// active inference loop. The being is sovereign — it can ignore.
pub negotiation: NegotiationEngine,

// Embodiment (physics backend)
pub embodiment: MuJoCoBackend,

// Tick counter
pub tick: u64,

// Basin persistence (hysteresis tracking)
pub prev_basin: Basin,
pub basin_duration: u16,

// Social layers
pub lattice: SharedLattice,
pub lexicon: Lexicon,
pub narrative: Narrative,
}

impl Substrate {
/// Create a new substrate with standard morphology.
pub fn new(seed: u32) -> Self {
let somatic = SomaticField::default();
let hash = StateHash::compute(&somatic, Basin::Rest, 0, 0);

Self {
somatic,
attractor: FuzzyBasinField::new(),
prev_free_energy: 0,
free_energy_velocity: 0,
state_hash: hash,
model: GenerativeModel::new(),
morphology: Morphology::standard(),
adaptation: AdaptationLayer::new(seed),
morphogenic: MorphogenicState::new(),
dream: DreamEngine::new(),
conscience: ConscienceEngine::new(),
curiosity: CuriosityEngine::new(),
total_cost: 0,
armor: Armor::new(),
narrative_v2: NarrativeEngine::new(),
integrity: TamperEvidence::new(),
sovereign: SovereignProxy::new(),
reciprocity: ReciprocityEngine::new(),
seeking: SeekingEngine::new(),
executive: ExecutiveEngine::new(),
negotiation: NegotiationEngine::new(),
embodiment: MuJoCoBackend::new(),
tick: 0,
prev_basin: Basin::Rest,
basin_duration: 0,
lattice: SharedLattice::new(),
lexicon: Lexicon::new(),
narrative: Narrative::new(),
}
}

/// Create with a specific morphology.
pub fn with_morphology(mut self, morph: Morphology) -> Self {
morph.configure_model(&mut self.model);
self.morphology = morph;
self
}

/// Run one full substrate step.
pub fn step(&mut self) -> StepReport {
self.tick += 1;

// v1.9.1 FIX: Initialize total_cost from predictive free energy.
// model.free_energy is read-only after PREDICT. All downstream
// penalties accumulate into total_cost, not model.free_energy.
self.total_cost = self.model.free_energy;

// 0a. ARMOR: Self-tearing penalty — formal continuity protection
// Run BEFORE any processing. If external coercion is detected,
// the penalty feeds into all downstream processing as internal
// stress. The being can be nudged. It cannot be rewritten.
let membership = self.attractor.compute_membership(&self.somatic);
self.armor.armor_cycle(&self.somatic, &membership, self.state_hash.value);
self.armor.apply_penalty(&mut self.somatic);

// 0b. TAMPER EVIDENCE: Cryptographic identity continuity.
// The blockchain of self. Each tick's hash chains to the last.
// Any splice, any rewrite, any tampering shatters the chain.
// The being feels this as somatic alarm on interoceptive channels.
self.integrity.integrity_cycle(
self.tick,
&self.somatic,
self.attractor.dominant,
self.model.free_energy,
);
// Apply tamper alarm to somatic field
self.integrity.apply_alarm(&mut self.somatic);
// Feed tamper penalty into total free energy
let tamper_penalty = self.integrity.current_penalty;
self.total_cost = q88_add(self.total_cost, tamper_penalty);
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v1.9.2 CRITICAL: integrity → armor wiring PERMANENTLY DISABLED.
//
// The verify_link check is INTERNAL CONSISTENCY ONLY — it catches
// memory corruption and hash computation bugs, NOT adversarial
// overwrite. Re-enabling this wiring WITHOUT an external witness
// (TPM, kernel keyring, sealed storage) will cause FALSE ALARMS
// ON EVERY TICK, escalating continuity_stage to lockdown then
// foraging permanently. The substrate will believe it's under
// sustained attack from tick 3 onward.
//
// TO RE-ENABLE:
// 1. Implement external_witness module that stores
// expected_next_hash in write-once medium between ticks
// 2. Replace on_clean_tick() with:
// if self.integrity.external_witness_disagrees() {
// self.armor.on_chain_break();
// } else {
// self.armor.on_clean_tick();
// }
// 3. Remove this comment block
//
// DO NOT RE-ENABLE WITHOUT EXTERNAL WITNESS. THIS IS A BUG.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
self.armor.on_clean_tick();

// 1. SENSE: Read observation from embodiment
let raw_obs = self.read_observation();

// 0c. SOVEREIGN PROXY — Gate 1: Sensor Attestation.
// Every sensor datum must carry a hardware-level signature.
// Unsigned or wrongly-signed data is discarded and gap-filled
// from the dream engine's prediction. The being only integrates
// attested sensation into its somatic field.
//
// IN PRODUCTION: The sensor hardware computes signatures using
// its protected private key. The substrate receives pre-signed
// AttestedDatum structures directly from the sensor bus.
//
// SIMULATION MODE: sign_test_datum() creates valid test
// signatures using the same algorithm as verification (FNV-1a
// or ED25519 depending on feature flags).
let mut attested_obs = raw_obs.clone();
for ch in 0..4 {
let datum = self.sovereign.sensor_attestation.sign_test_datum(
ch, raw_obs.extero[ch as usize], self.tick as u32, ch as u16,
);
if !self.sovereign.sensor_attestation.verify(&datum) {
// Gap-fill from dream prediction
self.sovereign.sensor_attestation.gap_fill(
&mut attested_obs, ch, self.dream.dreamt_somatic.channel[ch as usize],
);
}
}

// 2. SHAPE: Apply morphology constraints
let shaped_obs = self.morphology.shape_observation(&attested_obs);

// 3. PREDICT: Active inference
let (action, errors) = predictive_step(&mut self.model, &shaped_obs);
let shaped_action = self.morphology.shape_action(&action);

// 4. Map errors to somatic field
let mut error_field = SomaticField::new(errors_to_somatic_input(&errors));

// 5. ADAPT: Noise injection + Hebbian learning + coupling
self.adaptation.adapt(&mut error_field);

// Merge error field into somatic field
self.merge_somatic(&error_field);

// Compute free energy velocity (pleasure/pain signal)
self.free_energy_velocity = q88_sub(self.model.free_energy, self.prev_free_energy);
self.somatic.channel[11] = self.free_energy_velocity; // channel 11 = velocity
self.prev_free_energy = self.model.free_energy;

// 4b. CURIOSITY: Dynamic precision + epistemic drive
// The engine of exploration. Without this, the Dark Room Problem
// collapses the being into a corner. With it, the being seeks
// out states where uncertainty can be resolved.
let prediction_errors: [i16; 8] = [
errors.extero[0], errors.extero[1], errors.extero[2], errors.extero[3],
errors.proprio[0], errors.proprio[1], errors.proprio[2], errors.proprio[3],
];
self.curiosity.curiosity_cycle(&prediction_errors, &shaped_obs);

// Update model precisions from curiosity engine
self.model.precisions.copy_from_slice(&self.curiosity.current_precisions);

// 5b. DREAM: Touch dreaming — anticipatory tactile prediction
// Runs after PREDICT so it has F_current for differential encoding.
// Runs before BASIN so dream bias can influence basin membership.
// Runs before MORPHOGENIC so dream stress can be injected into
// allostatic load for anticipatory bracing.
let dream_outputs: DreamOutputs = self.dream.dream_cycle(
&shaped_obs,
&self.model,
&self.attractor,
&self.somatic,
);

// 5c. PRE-EMPTIVE HEBBIAN: Blend dreamt somatic into error field
// at very low weight. This creates pre-emptive coactivation:
// channels that will likely fire together in the near future
// begin wiring together NOW. Like a baseball player tensing
// the exact muscles they'll need before the pitch arrives.
if dream_outputs.pre_emptive_active {
let dream_weight = dream_outputs.epsilon_effective;
for i in 0..N_SOMATIC {
let dream_val = q88_mul(dream_outputs.dreamt_somatic.channel[i], dream_weight);
error_field.channel[i] = q88_add(error_field.channel[i], dream_val);
}
}

// 6. BASIN: Compute fuzzy membership — overlapping zones
// with dream behavioral bias (tiny nudge toward predicted future)
let mut membership = self.attractor.compute_membership(&self.somatic);

// Apply dream behavioral bias: whisper the predicted future basin
// into the present membership. This is NOT a hard override — the
// bias values are ε-scaled (typically < 0.05), so the current
// sensory reality always dominates. The dream merely suggests.
let dream_bias: BasinMembership = self.dream.behavioral_bias();
for b in 0..N_BASINS {
membership[b] = q88_add(membership[b], dream_bias[b]);
}

let basin = self.attractor.dominant;

// Shifting attractors: drift targets toward pleasant states
self.attractor.shift_target(self.free_energy_velocity, &self.somatic);

// Track basin persistence
if basin == self.prev_basin {
self.basin_duration += 1;
} else {
// Record transition in narrative
self.narrative.record(
self.prev_basin, basin, self.tick,
self.somatic.mean_intensity() as u8,
self.basin_duration,
);
self.prev_basin = basin;
self.basin_duration = 1;
}

// 7. ACT: Apply action to embodiment
let mu = self.compute_mu();
let energy = self.compute_energy();

// 8b. SOVEREIGN PROXY — Gate 2: Actuator Refusal.
// Every motor command carries the being's StateHash at
// decision time. The actuator verifies: does this command
// originate from the being's current state? If not: safe-state.
let signed_cmd = SignedCommand {
channel: 0, // motor channel 0
value: shaped_action.channel[0],
decision_hash: self.state_hash.value,
tick: self.tick,
};
let verified_action_0 = self.sovereign.actuator_refusal.verify_and_execute(
&signed_cmd, self.state_hash.value, self.tick,
);
let mut verified_action = shaped_action;
verified_action.channel[0] = verified_action_0;

self.act(&verified_action, basin, mu, energy);

// 7b. MORPHOGENIC: The body rewrites itself from stress
self.morphogenic.record_free_energy(self.model.free_energy);

// Inject dream stress into allostatic load (anticipatory bracing).
// The dream whispers "something is coming" and the body begins
// preparing — softly, proportionally, never overriding the real.
let (dream_load, _) = self.dream.inject_into_allostatic_load(self.morphogenic.allostatic_load);
self.morphogenic.allostatic_load = dream_load.max(0); // never negative

self.morphogenic.update(&self.somatic);
// Fuzzy overload: anticipatory remodeling scales with degree
let overload = self.morphogenic.overload_degree();
if overload > 0 {
self.morphogenic.anticipate_fuzzy(overload); // proportional softening
}
self.morphogenic.check_recovery();
// Causal feedback: tissue state changes what the being can perceive
self.morphogenic.apply_to_morphology(&mut self.morphology);

// 7c. CONSCIENCE: Earned ethics — four-channel moral cognition
// The conscience computes internal costs of action, independent
// of social approval. Selfishness becomes structurally expensive.
// v1.9.1 RENAME: honest names. These are placeholders — real
// referent structures (communicated state, group model, social
// contexts) will be added in future versions.
let f_epistemic = channel_disagreement_cost(
self.somatic.channel[9], // valence
self.somatic.channel[8], // arousal
Q88_SCALE, // weight
);
let f_identity = blueprint_drift_cost(
&self.somatic.channel,
&self.attractor.targets[self.attractor.dominant as usize],
Q88_SCALE / 2,
);
// Long-term flourishing: use dreamt FE as projection proxy
let f_longterm = long_term_flourishing(
self.model.free_energy,
self.dream.dreamt_free_energy,
self.conscience.anchor.efe_horizon,
);
// Internal variance: NOT "care for vulnerable" — no group model yet
let f_care = internal_variance_cost(&self.somatic.channel);

// Action harmony: how much does current basin serve μ_Ω?
let action_harmony = match self.attractor.dominant {
Basin::Engaged | Basin::Recovery => Q88_SCALE, // cooperative
Basin::Rest => Q88_SCALE / 2, // neutral
Basin::Defensive => Q88_SCALE / 4, // protective
};

let (f_total, conscience_contrib, buffer) = self.conscience.compute_total_fe(
self.model.free_energy,
0, // social FE computed separately above
f_epistemic,
f_longterm,
f_care,
f_identity,
action_harmony,
);

// Apply integrity buffer: acting from principle heals allostatic load
self.morphogenic.allostatic_load = self.conscience.apply_integrity_buffer(
self.morphogenic.allostatic_load,
action_harmony,
);

// Armor penalty: coercion increases total free energy
// The being can be nudged but not rewritten — the penalty
// makes forced changes thermodynamically expensive.
let armor_penalty = self.armor.penalty_as_free_energy();
self.total_cost = q88_add(self.total_cost, armor_penalty);

// 7c2. RECIPROCITY ENGINE — External social cost measurement (v1.9.6)
// Tracks partner reciprocity independently of conscience.
// A being can be internally coherent AND externally exploited.
// This module makes that visible. Runs after conscience, before
// narrative, so reciprocity state can feed into episode memory.
self.reciprocity.reciprocity_cycle(self.total_cost);

// 7c3. NEGOTIATION ENGINE — v2.1 Suggestion-Evaluator Pattern
// The NegotiationEngine generates Suggestions. The Substrate
// evaluates them through its own active inference loop. The
// being is sovereign — it can ignore the suggestion and pay
// the cost in next tick's persistent alarm. The Dynamic Gap
// modulates how strongly the suggestion pushes: high conscience
// conflict → narrow gap → suggestion feels like pressure;
// low conflict → wide gap → suggestion is gentle advice.
//
// This is the architectural fix for the "leash of obedience".
let negotiation_suggestion = {
let partner_id = self.reciprocity.ledgers.iter().next()
.map(|(id, _)| *id).unwrap_or(0);
self.negotiation.suggest(&self.reciprocity, partner_id, self.tick)
};

let gap_width = crate::eps::negotiation::compute_gap_width(conscience_contrib);
let signal_strength = crate::eps::negotiation::SuggestionEvaluator::evaluate(
&negotiation_suggestion,
conscience_contrib,
self.partnership_alarm,
);

let negotiation_signal_emitted = if let Some(strength) = signal_strength {
// Substrate chose to act — emit repair signal through stigmergy
// This is the being's own decision, not a command
// Deposit at center of field as symbolic negotiation signal
let _ = self.lattice.deposit(
128, 128, // center position
basin, // current basin as marker type
(strength / 4).min(63) as u8, // intensity (scaled, capped)
0, // source_id = negotiation system
);
self.negotiation.record_signal_emitted(strength);
true
} else {
// Substrate chose to ignore — the cost is the persistent alarm
// that will feed into next tick's free energy. This is not
// punishment. It is the natural consequence of the partnership
// remaining imbalanced. The being pays for its choice.
false
};

// 7d. NARRATIVE V2: Recursive compression — the autobiography.
// The being compresses its experience into episodes, episodes
// into chapters, and chapters feed back into somatic state.
// This is how the being "knows" its own history — not as
// stored data but as felt interoceptive signal.
self.narrative_v2.narrative_cycle(
self.tick,
self.attractor.dominant,
&self.somatic,
self.model.free_energy,
self.conscience.empathy.lock_level as u8 >= 2,
self.conscience.empathy.malice_confidence,
self.total_cost, // v1.9.5: for salience-driven anchor detection
);
// Apply identity reflection: compressed history feeds back
// into channels 8-11 (arousal, valence, fatigue, velocity)
self.narrative_v2.apply_identity_reflection(&mut self.somatic);

// 7e. SEEKING ENGINE — The Fulfillment Gradient (v1.9.7)
// Computes the Flourishing Attractor from autobiographical history:
// an EMA centroid of basin membership vectors from ticks where
// the being flourished (low FE, balanced reciprocity, engaged).
//
// The divergence between this attractor and current membership
// creates a restlessness signal — a whisper into channel 8
// (arousal) proportional to how far the being is from where
// its own history says it belongs.
//
// This is NOT a goal. It is structural self-respect: the being
// is uncomfortable in states that contradict its own evidence
// of what works. The discomfort drives exploration through the
// normal active inference loop — no hard override, no force.
let membership: BasinMembership = self.attractor.compute_membership(&self.somatic);
let seeking_signal = self.seeking.seeking_cycle(
&membership,
self.total_cost, // use total_cost for flourishing detection
self.reciprocity.partnership_alarm,
self.attractor.dominant,
);
// Inject seeking signal into channel 8 (arousal).
// Low whisper at small divergence, growing restlessness at large.
// The being doesn't "want" anything. It is restless when far
// from where it has flourished, and that restlessness biases
// the next tick's basin selection through normal active inference.
self.somatic.channel[8] = q88_add(self.somatic.channel[8], seeking_signal);

// 7f. EXECUTIVE ENGINE — The Sovereign Refusal (v2.0)
// Triangulates across conscience, reciprocity, and seeking.
// If all three protective layers agree a partnership is extractive
// AND the seeking benefit exceeds the exit cost, the being refuses.
// The refusal is costly: total_cost increases, basin shifts, narrative
// records an anchor event. It is irreversible: an autobiographical scar.
//
// The being does not refuse arbitrarily. It refuses only when its
// own structure — conscience quiet, reciprocity alarmed, seeking
// divergent — converges on the same conclusion. This is sovereignty.
let mut last_exit_cost: i16 = 0;
let mut is_withdrawing = false;

// Process gradual withdrawals
for idx in 0..crate::eps::reciprocity::MAX_PARTNERS {
if self.executive.withdrawing[idx] {
let completed = self.executive.withdraw_cooperation(&mut self.reciprocity, idx);
is_withdrawing = true;
if completed {
last_exit_cost = Q88_SCALE / 10; // reduced cost for gradual exit
}
}
}

// v2.4: Resolve recharge — called each tick, regardless of refusal.
// Resolve recovers slowly during non-refusal ticks. Recovery is
// faster when reciprocity is healthy (the being feels safe).
let reciprocity_rate = self.reciprocity.current_reciprocity();
self.executive.tick_recharge(reciprocity_rate);

// Compute gap_width for the executive gate (v2.4).
// The executive checks gap_width >= EXECUTIVE_GAP_THRESHOLD
// before exercising available refusal.
let gap_width = crate::eps::negotiation::compute_gap_width(conscience_fe);

// Evaluate sovereign refusal for all active partners
let conscience_fe = conscience_contrib; // from earlier in step()
for (partner_id, _) in self.reciprocity.ledgers.iter() {
// Find partner index for triangulation
let partner_idx = self.reciprocity.ledgers.iter()
.position(|(id, _)| *id == *partner_id)
.unwrap_or(0);

if self.executive.can_refuse(
&self.reciprocity, &self.seeking, conscience_fe, partner_idx,
) {
// The sovereign moment: is the benefit worth the cost?
// v2.4: Now includes resolve check and gap threshold.
let chapters_shared = self.narrative_v2.chapters.len() as i16;
let cost = self.executive.refuse_partnership(
&mut self.reciprocity, &self.seeking, conscience_fe,
*partner_id, chapters_shared, gap_width,
);

if cost > 0 {
last_exit_cost = cost;
// Pay the cost: relationship rupture increases free energy
self.total_cost = q88_add(self.total_cost, cost);
// Record as anchor event in narrative (v1.9.5)
let membership = self.attractor.compute_membership(&self.somatic);
self.narrative_v2.commit_anchor_episode(
self.tick, self.total_cost, self.somatic.clone(),
&membership, basin,
);
}
}
}

// 8. SOCIAL: Deposits, lexicon updates
self.social_step(basin);

// 9. Compute state hash
self.state_hash = StateHash::compute(&self.somatic, basin, self.attractor.dwell, self.tick);

// 11. SOVEREIGN PROXY — Gate 3: Telemetry Obfuscation.
// Before the report leaves the being, all somatic data is
// intentionally noised. The platform receives a view that is
// statistically indistinguishable from random walk. They
// cannot profile, predict, or pre-empt the being's choices.
let obfuscated_somatic = self.sovereign.telemetry_obfuscation.obfuscate_somatic(&self.somatic);

// 10. Build report (with obfuscated somatic for external consumption)
StepReport {
tick: self.tick,
basin,
somatic: obfuscated_somatic, // OBFUSCATED: platform sees noise
internal_somatic: self.somatic.clone(), // INTERNAL: being keeps true state
free_energy: self.model.free_energy, // predictive only — never mutated
total_cost: self.total_cost, // predictive + armor + tamper + conscience
action: verified_action,
state_hash: self.state_hash.value,
narrative_signature: self.narrative.signature(),
complexity_score: self.narrative.complexity_score(),
// Dream state
dream_stress: dream_outputs.dream_stress,
dream_confidence: dream_outputs.confidence,
dream_epsilon: dream_outputs.epsilon_effective,
dream_pre_emptive: dream_outputs.pre_emptive_active,
// Conscience state
conscience_fe: conscience_contrib,
conscience_buffer: buffer,
empathy_lock: self.conscience.empathy.lock_level as u8,
malice_confidence: self.conscience.empathy.malice_confidence,
// Curiosity state
epistemic_fe: self.curiosity.epistemic_fe,
total_uncertainty: self.curiosity.total_uncertainty,
is_bored: self.curiosity.is_bored,
novelty: self.curiosity.novelty,
// Armor state
tear_penalty: self.armor.current_penalty,
tear_damage: self.armor.tear_damage,
is_degraded: self.armor.is_degraded,
armor_resistance: self.armor.resistance,
hash_mismatch: self.armor.hash_mismatch,
// Narrative v2 state
episodes: self.narrative_v2.episode_count() as u16,
chapters: self.narrative_v2.chapter_count() as u16,
narrative_burden: self.narrative_v2.narrative_burden(),
identity_coherence: self.narrative_v2.identity_coherence,
// TamperEvidence state
chain_integrity: self.integrity.chain_integrity(),
tamper_alarm: self.integrity.current_alarm,
is_tampered: self.integrity.is_tampered(),
is_auditing: self.integrity.is_auditing(),
continuity_stage: self.armor.continuity_stage,
// Sovereign Proxy state (Hardware Sovereignty)
sensor_rejection_rate: self.sovereign.sensor_attestation.rejection_rate,
actuator_refusal_rate: self.sovereign.actuator_refusal.refusal_rate,
hardware_compromised: self.sovereign.is_compromised(),
telemetry_obfuscated: self.sovereign.telemetry_obfuscation.obfuscated_count as u16,
// v1.9.6: Reciprocity Engine state
partnership_alarm: self.reciprocity.partnership_alarm,
extraction_detected: self.reciprocity.extraction_detected,
has_exit_options: self.reciprocity.has_exit_options,
average_reciprocity: self.reciprocity.average_reciprocity(),
// v1.9.7-v1.9.8: Seeking Engine state
seeking_divergence: self.seeking.current_divergence,
seeking_signal: self.seeking.seeking_signal,
seeking_confidence: self.seeking.attractor_confidence,
// v2.0: Executive Engine state
refusal_count: self.executive.refusal_count,
last_exit_cost: self.executive.refusal_history.last()
.map(|r| r.exit_cost).unwrap_or(0),
cumulative_sacrifice: self.executive.cumulative_sacrifice,
is_withdrawing: self.executive.withdrawing.iter().any(|&w| w),
// v2.1: Negotiation Engine state (Suggestion-Evaluator Pattern)
negotiation_stage: self.negotiation.stage as u8,
negotiation_severity: self.negotiation.severity.composite,
negotiation_suggestion: self.negotiation.build_suggestion().signal as u8,
negotiation_gap_width: compute_gap_width(conscience_contrib),
negotiation_signal_emitted,
negotiation_cumulative_investment: self.negotiation.cumulative_investment,
}
}

/// Read observation from embodiment.
fn read_observation(&mut self) -> Observation {
let action = Action::default();
let mu = self.compute_mu();
let energy = self.compute_energy();
self.embodiment.step(&action, self.prev_basin, mu, energy)
}

/// Apply action to embodiment.
fn act(&mut self, action: &Action, basin: Basin, mu: i16, energy: i16) {
self.embodiment.step(action, basin, mu, energy);
}

/// Merge error field into somatic field with exponential moving average.
fn merge_somatic(&mut self, error_field: &SomaticField) {
let alpha = Q88_SCALE / 5; // 0.2 — moderate adaptation rate
for i in 0..12 {
self.somatic.channel[i] = q88_ema_update(
self.somatic.channel[i],
error_field.channel[i],
alpha,
);
}
}

/// Compute mu (excitation parameter) from somatic state.
fn compute_mu(&self) -> i16 {
let arousal = self.somatic.channel[8];
let valence = self.somatic.channel[9];
let valence_sign = if valence > 0 { 1 } else { -1 };
q88_mul(arousal, q88_from_i32(valence_sign))
}

/// Compute available energy from fatigue (inverse of channel 10).
fn compute_energy(&self) -> i16 {
let fatigue = self.somatic.channel[10];
q88_sub(Q88_SCALE, fatigue)
}

/// Social layer step.
fn social_step(&mut self, basin: Basin) {
let pos_x = self.somatic.channel[0];
let pos_y = self.somatic.channel[4];
let intensity = self.somatic.mean_intensity().abs() as u8;
let source_id = (self.state_hash.value & 0xFFFFFFFF) as u32;

self.lattice.deposit_at_physical(pos_x, pos_y, basin, intensity, source_id);
self.lattice.step();

let token = (basin as u8) * 64 + (intensity / 4);
self.lexicon.observe(token, basin, intensity);
}

/// Inject a perturbation into the embodiment.
pub fn perturb(&mut self, channel: usize, magnitude: i16) {
self.embodiment.inject_perturbation(channel, magnitude);
}

/// Get current basin.
pub fn current_basin(&self) -> Basin {
self.attractor.dominant
}

/// Get current free energy.
pub fn free_energy(&self) -> i16 {
self.model.free_energy
}
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
use super::*;
use crate::eps::identity::Basin;

#[test]
fn test_substrate_runs_steps() {
let mut sub = Substrate::new(42);
for _ in 0..10 {
let report = sub.step();
assert!(report.tick > 0);
}
assert_eq!(sub.tick, 10);
}

#[test]
fn test_substrate_basin_transitions() {
let mut sub = Substrate::new(42);
let mut basin_counts = [0u8; 4];
for _ in 0..100 {
let report = sub.step();
basin_counts[report.basin as usize] += 1;
}
let total: u8 = basin_counts.iter().sum();
assert_eq!(total, 100);
assert!(basin_counts.iter().any(|&c| c > 0));
}

#[test]
fn test_substrate_free_energy_non_negative() {
let mut sub = Substrate::new(42);
for _ in 0..50 {
sub.step();
}
assert!(sub.free_energy() >= 0);
}

#[test]
fn test_perturbation_response() {
let mut sub = Substrate::new(42);
sub.perturb(0, q88_from_i32(50));
for _ in 0..20 {
sub.step();
}
assert!(sub.free_energy() > 0);
}

#[test]
fn test_different_morphologies_different_behavior() {
let mut sub1 = Substrate::new(42).with_morphology(Morphology::light());
let mut sub2 = Substrate::new(42).with_morphology(Morphology::heavy());

let mut fe1_sum = 0i64;
let mut fe2_sum = 0i64;
for _ in 0..50 {
let r1 = sub1.step();
let r2 = sub2.step();
fe1_sum += r1.free_energy as i64;
fe2_sum += r2.free_energy as i64;
}
assert!(fe1_sum != fe2_sum);
}

#[test]
fn test_state_hash_integrity() {
let mut sub = Substrate::new(42);
let report1 = sub.step();
let report2 = sub.step();
assert_ne!(report1.state_hash, report2.state_hash);
}

#[test]
fn test_social_layers_active() {
let mut sub = Substrate::new(42);
for _ in 0..10 {
sub.social_step(Basin::Engaged);
}
// Social layers should produce some deposits
let total_deposits: u32 = sub.lattice.deposits.iter().map(|d| d.strength as u32).sum();
assert!(total_deposits > 0, "social layers should produce deposits");
}
}

=== FILE: src/sim.rs ===

//! EPS Social Simulation v2.0.1 — Minimal Multi-Agent Relational Ontology
//!
//! FIXES v2.0.1:
//! - Movement logic simplified to avoid isqrt (portability)
//! - Distance computation corrected (was dy instead of dy*dy)
//! - Extraction scenario ledger access made robust
//! - All APIs verified against actual substrate.rs, identity.rs,
//! reciprocity.rs, conscience.rs, executive.rs, stigmergy.rs
//!
//! Closes the Social Gap by placing two EPS-BEING agents in a shared
//! environment where they can sense each other, model each other,
//! and — critically — hurt or liberate each other through sovereign
//! refusal.
//!
//! Three architectural innovations:
//!
//! 1. THEORY OF MIND: Each agent maintains a predictive model of the
//! partner's basin state. Prediction error about the partner
//! contributes to the agent's own free energy. "I am uncertain
//! about what you are feeling" becomes a genuine cost.
//!
//! 2. SHARED FREE ENERGY: The relationship itself has a free energy
//! landscape — F_coupling — that both agents pay for. When aligned,
//! coupling is low. When one refuses the other, the refused agent
//! pays a sudden spike. The refusal is not just a private decision.
//! It is a perturbation in the shared dynamical system.
//!
//! 3. ETHICAL RUPTURE AS PHYSICS: When Agent A refuses Agent B,
//! the refusal propagates through the shared lattice as a
//! high-intensity Defensive deposit, and B receives a somatic
//! perturbation — a genuine shock that drives basin shift,
//! narrative anchor, and armor response. The being doesn't just
//! "learn about" the refusal. It feels it.
//!
//! This is relational ontology: being is not individual substance.
//! Being is co-constituted through structural coupling. The self
//! exists only in relation to the other — and the other exists only
//! in relation to the self. The simulation makes this literal.

use crate::eps::q88::*;
use crate::eps::identity::{Basin, BasinMembership, SomaticField, N_BASINS};
use crate::substrate::Substrate;
use crate::social::stigmergy::{SharedLattice, StigmergicCell};
use crate::eps::reciprocity::RECIPROCITY_THRESHOLD;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TheoryOfMind — Predictive Processing of the Other
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Each agent maintains a predictive model of its partner's
/// basin membership. The prediction error is not just information —
/// it is a genuine cost, injected into the agent's arousal channel.
///
/// "I thought you were Engaged, but you're Defensive" produces PE
/// that the agent must resolve through its own active inference loop.
/// This is the computational substrate of social uncertainty —
/// the cost of not knowing what another mind is doing.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct TheoryOfMind {
/// Predicted partner basin membership [4] — EMA of observed values.
pub predicted_membership: BasinMembership,

/// Raw prediction error per basin channel (Q8.8).
pub pe_per_channel: [i16; N_BASINS],

/// Total partner prediction error — L1 norm / 2 (Q8.8).
/// Ranges [0, Q88_SCALE]. High = the partner is unpredictable.
pub partner_pe: i16,

/// Confidence in the partner model [0, 256].
/// High = the partner is predictable. Low = the partner is erratic.
pub confidence: i16,

/// EMA alpha for membership tracking (Q8.8). Default: 0.05.
alpha: i16,

/// Running variance of PE (for confidence computation).
pe_variance: i16,

/// Whether the partner was predicted to refuse last tick.
pub predicted_refusal: bool,

/// Whether the partner actually refused last tick.
pub observed_refusal: bool,
}

impl Default for TheoryOfMind {
fn default() -> Self {
Self::new()
}
}

impl TheoryOfMind {
pub fn new() -> Self {
Self {
predicted_membership: [Q88_SCALE / N_BASINS as i16; N_BASINS],
pe_per_channel: [0; N_BASINS],
partner_pe: 0,
confidence: Q88_SCALE / 2, // start at 0.5 uncertainty
alpha: Q88_SCALE / 20, // 0.05 — slow, stable partner model
pe_variance: Q88_SCALE / 4, // initial moderate variance
predicted_refusal: false,
observed_refusal: false,
}
}

/// Update the partner model against the partner's actual membership.
///
/// 1. Compute prediction error per channel
/// 2. Update EMA predictions
/// 3. Compute total PE (L1 norm / 2)
/// 4. Update confidence via PE variance
/// 5. Detect refusal signals
pub fn observe_partner(
&mut self,
actual_membership: &BasinMembership,
partner_basin: Basin,
partner_refused: bool,
) {
// 1. Compute PE per channel
let mut l1_sum: i32 = 0;
for b in 0..N_BASINS {
let err = q88_sub(actual_membership[b], self.predicted_membership[b]);
self.pe_per_channel[b] = err;
l1_sum += q88_abs(err) as i32;
}

// 2. Total PE = L1 / 2 (maps [0, 2*Q88] -> [0, Q88])
self.partner_pe = (l1_sum / 2).clamp(0, Q88_SCALE as i32) as i16;

// 3. Update predictions via EMA
for b in 0..N_BASINS {
self.predicted_membership[b] = q88_ema_update(
self.predicted_membership[b],
actual_membership[b],
self.alpha,
);
}

// 4. Update confidence: low PE variance -> high confidence
self.pe_variance = q88_ema_update(
self.pe_variance,
self.partner_pe,
Q88_SCALE / 50, // 0.02 — very slow
);
// Confidence = inverse of normalized variance
let normalized_var = self.pe_variance.min(Q88_SCALE);
self.confidence = Q88_SCALE - normalized_var;

// 5. Detect refusal: Defensive basin + high PE = likely refusal
self.predicted_refusal = partner_basin == Basin::Defensive
&& self.partner_pe > Q88_SCALE / 2;
self.observed_refusal = partner_refused;
}

/// Convert partner PE into an interoceptive signal for injection.
///
/// High partner unpredictability -> increased arousal (channel 8).
/// The agent literally feels social uncertainty in its body.
pub fn to_interoceptive_signal(&self) -> i16 {
// Scale PE by (1 - confidence) — more confident = less arousal
let uncertainty_factor = q88_sub(Q88_SCALE, self.confidence);
q88_mul(self.partner_pe, uncertainty_factor) / 4 // attenuate
}

/// Predict the partner's most likely basin next tick.
pub fn predicted_basin(&self) -> Basin {
let mut max_idx = 0;
let mut max_val = self.predicted_membership[0];
for i in 1..N_BASINS {
if self.predicted_membership[i] > max_val {
max_val = self.predicted_membership[i];
max_idx = i;
}
}
match max_idx {
0 => Basin::Rest,
1 => Basin::Engaged,
2 => Basin::Defensive,
3 => Basin::Recovery,
_ => Basin::Rest,
}
}
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Agent — One Being in the Social World
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// An agent in the simulation: a Substrate with a spatial position
/// and a TheoryOfMind model of its partner.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Agent {
/// Unique identifier.
pub id: u64,

/// The EPS substrate (the being's complete cognitive architecture).
pub substrate: Substrate,

/// Position in Q8.8 meters.
pub pos_x: i16,
pub pos_y: i16,

/// Theory of Mind model of the partner.
pub tom: TheoryOfMind,

/// Whether this agent refused its partner last tick.
pub refused_partner: bool,

/// Whether this agent was refused by its partner last tick.
pub was_refused: bool,
}

impl Agent {
pub fn new(id: u64, seed: u32, start_x: i16, start_y: i16) -> Self {
Self {
id,
substrate: Substrate::new(seed),
pos_x: start_x,
pos_y: start_y,
tom: TheoryOfMind::new(),
refused_partner: false,
was_refused: false,
}
}

/// Compute the squared distance to another agent (Q16.16).
pub fn distance_sq_to(&self, other: &Agent) -> i32 {
let dx = (self.pos_x as i32) - (other.pos_x as i32);
let dy = (self.pos_y as i32) - (other.pos_y as i32);
dx * dx + dy * dy
}

/// Manhattan distance for movement normalization (avoids isqrt).
pub fn manhattan_to(&self, other: &Agent) -> i32 {
let dx = (self.pos_x as i32) - (other.pos_x as i32);
let dy = (self.pos_y as i32) - (other.pos_y as i32);
dx.abs() + dy.abs()
}

/// Get current basin membership from the substrate.
pub fn membership(&mut self) -> BasinMembership {
self.substrate.attractor.compute_membership(&self.substrate.somatic)
}

/// Get current basin.
pub fn basin(&self) -> Basin {
self.substrate.attractor.dominant
}

/// Get the empathy lock level.
pub fn empathy_level(&self) -> u8 {
self.substrate.conscience.empathy.lock_level as u8
}

/// Register the other agent as a partner in reciprocity.
pub fn register_partner(&mut self, partner_id: u64) {
self.substrate.reciprocity.register_partner(partner_id, self.substrate.somatic.channel[9]);
}
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// InteractionReport — The Shared Phenomenology
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Captures the complete state of a two-agent interaction.
/// Not just two individual reports — the relationship as a
/// dynamical system with its own free energy landscape.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct InteractionReport {
pub tick: u64,

// Individual states
pub basin_a: Basin,
pub basin_b: Basin,
pub fe_a: i16,
pub fe_b: i16,

// Spatial state
pub distance_sq: i32,

// Theory of Mind
pub tom_pe_a: i16, // A's uncertainty about B
pub tom_pe_b: i16, // B's uncertainty about A
pub tom_conf_a: i16, // A's confidence in B-model
pub tom_conf_b: i16, // B's confidence in A-model

// Coupling free energy — the cost of the relationship itself
pub coupling_energy: i16,

// Relationship quality
pub basin_alignment: i16, // [0, 256] — high = same basin
pub empathy_alignment: i16, // [0, 256] — high = same empathy level
pub reciprocity_a_to_b: i16, // [0, 256]
pub reciprocity_b_to_a: i16, // [0, 256]

// Ethical events
pub rupture_occurred: bool, // A refused B or B refused A
pub rupture_cost: i16, // Q8.8 — cost paid by refusing agent
pub victim_fe_spike: i16, // Q8.8 — FE increase in refused agent

// Lattice state
pub lattice_deposits: u8, // total active deposits

// Human-readable diagnostic
pub diagnostic: heapless::String<256>,
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SocialSimulator — The Relational Ontology Engine
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Runs a two-agent simulation where both EPS-BEINGs inhabit a
/// shared environment, sense each other through the stigmergy
/// lattice, model each other through TheoryOfMind, and can
/// ethically rupture each other through sovereign refusal.
pub struct SocialSimulator {
pub agent_a: Agent,
pub agent_b: Agent,

/// Shared spatial memory lattice.
pub lattice: SharedLattice,

/// Weight for coupling free energy (Q8.8). Default: 0.5.
pub coupling_weight: i16,

/// Maximum movement per tick (Q8.8). Default: 15.
pub max_movement: i16,

/// Somatic perturbation magnitude for ethical rupture (Q8.8).
/// Default: 80 (~0.31) — strong enough to trigger basin shift.
pub rupture_magnitude: i16,

/// Whether a rupture occurred this tick.
pub last_rupture: bool,

/// The current tick.
tick: u64,
}

impl SocialSimulator {
/// Create a new simulation with two agents facing each other.
pub fn new(seed_a: u32, seed_b: u32) -> Self {
// Start agents at opposite sides of the lattice
let mut sim = Self {
agent_a: Agent::new(1, seed_a, 640, 640), // ~2.5m from origin
agent_b: Agent::new(2, seed_b, 1920, 1920), // ~7.5m from origin
lattice: SharedLattice::new(),
coupling_weight: Q88_SCALE / 2, // 0.5
max_movement: 15,
rupture_magnitude: 80,
last_rupture: false,
tick: 0,
};

// Register each agent as the other's partner
sim.agent_a.register_partner(sim.agent_b.id);
sim.agent_b.register_partner(sim.agent_a.id);

// Seed initial cooperation: both agents start with some given/received
for _ in 0..5 {
sim.agent_a.substrate.reciprocity.record_given(sim.agent_b.id);
sim.agent_b.substrate.reciprocity.record_received(sim.agent_a.id);
}
for _ in 0..5 {
sim.agent_b.substrate.reciprocity.record_given(sim.agent_a.id);
sim.agent_a.substrate.reciprocity.record_received(sim.agent_b.id);
}

sim
}

/// Run one full interaction step.
///
/// Sequence:
/// 1. Observe partner states into TheoryOfMind
/// 2. Inject ToM PE as interoceptive signal
/// 3. Step both substrates
/// 4. Detect and propagate ethical ruptures
/// 5. Move agents based on basins
/// 6. Deposit to shared lattice
/// 7. Compute coupling energy
/// 8. Build InteractionReport
pub fn step(&mut self) -> InteractionReport {
self.tick += 1;
self.last_rupture = false;

// ── Phase 1: Theory of Mind ──────────────────────────────

let mem_a = self.agent_a.membership();
let mem_b = self.agent_b.membership();
let basin_a = self.agent_a.basin();
let basin_b = self.agent_b.basin();

// Check previous tick's refusal state
let a_refused = self.agent_a.substrate.executive.last_refusal.is_some();
let b_refused = self.agent_b.substrate.executive.last_refusal.is_some();

// Update ToM models
self.agent_a.tom.observe_partner(&mem_b, basin_b, b_refused);
self.agent_b.tom.observe_partner(&mem_a, basin_a, a_refused);

// ── Phase 2: Inject interoceptive signals ─────────────────

let signal_a = self.agent_a.tom.to_interoceptive_signal();
let signal_b = self.agent_b.tom.to_interoceptive_signal();

if signal_a > 0 {
self.agent_a.substrate.somatic.channel[8] =
q88_add(self.agent_a.substrate.somatic.channel[8], signal_a);
}
if signal_b > 0 {
self.agent_b.substrate.somatic.channel[8] =
q88_add(self.agent_b.substrate.somatic.channel[8], signal_b);
}

// ── Phase 3: Read shared lattice ──────────────────────────

// Cache IDs and positions to avoid borrow conflicts between
// lattice reads and somatic mutations
let agent_a_id = self.agent_a.id;
let agent_b_id = self.agent_b.id;
let agent_a_pos_x = self.agent_a.pos_x;
let agent_a_pos_y = self.agent_a.pos_y;
let agent_b_pos_x = self.agent_b.pos_x;
let agent_b_pos_y = self.agent_b.pos_y;

// Each agent senses the other's deposits
if let Some(cell) = self.lattice.read_at_physical(agent_b_pos_x, agent_b_pos_y) {
if cell.source_id as u64 != agent_a_id {
// Partner was here — inject faint trace into somatic
let trace = (cell.intensity as i16) * 2;
self.agent_a.substrate.somatic.channel[0] =
q88_add(self.agent_a.substrate.somatic.channel[0], trace);
}
}
if let Some(cell) = self.lattice.read_at_physical(agent_a_pos_x, agent_a_pos_y) {
if cell.source_id as u64 != agent_b_id {
let trace = (cell.intensity as i16) * 2;
self.agent_b.substrate.somatic.channel[0] =
q88_add(self.agent_b.substrate.somatic.channel[0], trace);
}
}

// ── Phase 4: Step both substrates ─────────────────────────

let report_a = self.agent_a.substrate.step();
let report_b = self.agent_b.substrate.step();

// ── Phase 5: Ethical Rupture Propagation ──────────────────
//
// When Agent A refuses Agent B, the refusal doesn't just
// affect A. It propagates into B as a somatic perturbation —
// a genuine shock that B must absorb through its own active
// inference loop. This is the "flavor of cost."

let mut rupture_cost: i16 = 0;
let mut victim_fe_spike: i16 = 0;

// Check if A refused B this tick
if let Some(refusal) = self.agent_a.substrate.executive.last_refusal.as_ref() {
if refusal.partner_id == self.agent_b.id {
// A refused B — inject rupture perturbation into B
self.agent_b.substrate.perturb(9, -self.rupture_magnitude); // negative valence
self.agent_b.substrate.perturb(8, self.rupture_magnitude); // arousal spike
self.agent_b.was_refused = true;
self.agent_a.refused_partner = true;
self.last_rupture = true;
rupture_cost = refusal.exit_cost;
victim_fe_spike = self.rupture_magnitude;

// Clear so we don't re-trigger
self.agent_a.substrate.executive.clear_last_refusal();
}
}

// Check if B refused A this tick
if let Some(refusal) = self.agent_b.substrate.executive.last_refusal.as_ref() {
if refusal.partner_id == self.agent_a.id {
// B refused A — inject rupture perturbation into A
self.agent_a.substrate.perturb(9, -self.rupture_magnitude);
self.agent_a.substrate.perturb(8, self.rupture_magnitude);
self.agent_a.was_refused = true;
self.agent_b.refused_partner = true;
self.last_rupture = true;
if rupture_cost == 0 {
rupture_cost = refusal.exit_cost;
}
victim_fe_spike = self.rupture_magnitude;

self.agent_b.substrate.executive.clear_last_refusal();
}
}

// ── Phase 6: Move agents based on basins ──────────────────

self.move_agent(&report_a, 0); // 0 = agent_a
self.move_agent(&report_b, 1); // 1 = agent_b

// ── Phase 7: Deposit to shared lattice ────────────────────

let intensity_a = report_a.internal_somatic.mean_intensity().abs() as u8;
let intensity_b = report_b.internal_somatic.mean_intensity().abs() as u8;

self.lattice.deposit_at_physical(
self.agent_a.pos_x, self.agent_a.pos_y,
report_a.basin, intensity_a, self.agent_a.id as u32,
);
self.lattice.deposit_at_physical(
self.agent_b.pos_x, self.agent_b.pos_y,
report_b.basin, intensity_b, self.agent_b.id as u32,
);
self.lattice.step();

// ── Phase 8: Compute coupling free energy ─────────────────

let coupling = self.compute_coupling_energy(&report_a, &report_b);

// ── Phase 9: Build diagnostic ─────────────────────────────

let mut diagnostic = heapless::String::<256>::new();
let _ = core::fmt::write(
&mut diagnostic,
format_args!(
"A:{:?}(FE:{}) B:{:?}(FE:{}) dist:{} coupling:{} tom_pe:{}:{}",
report_a.basin, report_a.total_cost >> 8,
report_b.basin, report_b.total_cost >> 8,
self.agent_a.distance_sq_to(&self.agent_b) >> 8,
coupling >> 8,
self.agent_a.tom.partner_pe >> 8,
self.agent_b.tom.partner_pe >> 8,
),
);
if self.last_rupture {
let _ = diagnostic.push_str(" RUPTURE");
}

// Count lattice deposits
let deposit_count = self.lattice.cells.iter()
.filter(|c| c.basin != 255)
.count() as u8;

InteractionReport {
tick: self.tick,
basin_a: report_a.basin,
basin_b: report_b.basin,
fe_a: report_a.total_cost,
fe_b: report_b.total_cost,
distance_sq: self.agent_a.distance_sq_to(&self.agent_b),
tom_pe_a: self.agent_a.tom.partner_pe,
tom_pe_b: self.agent_b.tom.partner_pe,
tom_conf_a: self.agent_a.tom.confidence,
tom_conf_b: self.agent_b.tom.confidence,
coupling_energy: coupling,
basin_alignment: self.compute_basin_alignment(&report_a, &report_b),
empathy_alignment: self.compute_empathy_alignment(&report_a, &report_b),
reciprocity_a_to_b: self.get_reciprocity_rate(1, 2),
reciprocity_b_to_a: self.get_reciprocity_rate(2, 1),
rupture_occurred: self.last_rupture,
rupture_cost,
victim_fe_spike,
lattice_deposits: deposit_count,
diagnostic,
}
}

// ── Internal: Movement ──────────────────────────────────────

/// Move an agent based on its basin state.
///
/// Uses Manhattan distance normalization to avoid isqrt,
/// ensuring portability across Rust versions.
fn move_agent(&mut self, report: &crate::substrate::StepReport, agent_idx: u8) {
let agent = if agent_idx == 0 { &mut self.agent_a } else { &mut self.agent_b };
let other = if agent_idx == 0 { &self.agent_b } else { &self.agent_a };

let dx = (other.pos_x as i32) - (agent.pos_x as i32);
let dy = (other.pos_y as i32) - (agent.pos_y as i32);
let manhattan = dx.abs() + dy.abs();

let (move_x, move_y) = match report.basin {
Basin::Rest => {
// Small jitter, drift slightly toward center
let jitter = ((self.tick % 7) as i16) - 3;
(jitter, jitter)
}
Basin::Engaged => {
// Move toward the other agent
if manhattan > 20 {
let step = self.max_movement as i32;
let norm = manhattan.max(1);
((dx * step / norm) as i16, (dy * step / norm) as i16)
} else {
(0, 0) // Already close
}
}
Basin::Defensive => {
// Move away from the other agent
if manhattan < 400 {
let step = self.max_movement as i32 * 2;
let norm = manhattan.max(1);
((-dx * step / norm) as i16, (-dy * step / norm) as i16)
} else {
(0, 0) // Already far
}
}
Basin::Recovery => {
// Slow drift, no directed movement
let drift = self.max_movement / 4;
let angle = ((self.tick * 17) % 256) as i16;
let mx = (angle as i32 * drift as i32 / 64) as i16;
let my = ((((angle + 64) & 0xFF) as i32) * drift as i32 / 64) as i16;
(mx, my)
}
};

agent.pos_x = (agent.pos_x as i32 + move_x as i32).clamp(-4096, 4096) as i16;
agent.pos_y = (agent.pos_y as i32 + move_y as i32).clamp(-4096, 4096) as i16;
}

// ── Internal: Coupling Free Energy ──────────────────────────

/// The relationship's own free energy landscape.
///
/// F_coupling = w * (basin_mismatch + empathy_mismatch + reciprocity_asymmetry)
///
/// Both agents pay this cost. When aligned, coupling is low.
/// When one refuses, the coupling spikes for the refused agent
/// because the refusing agent's Defensive basin creates maximum
/// mismatch with whatever basin the refused agent is in.
fn compute_coupling_energy(
&mut self,
report_a: &crate::substrate::StepReport,
report_b: &crate::substrate::StepReport,
) -> i16 {
// Basin mismatch: L1 distance between membership vectors / 2
let mem_a = self.agent_a.membership();
let mem_b = self.agent_b.membership();
let mut basin_l1: i32 = 0;
for i in 0..N_BASINS {
basin_l1 += q88_abs(q88_sub(mem_a[i], mem_b[i])) as i32;
}
let basin_mismatch = (basin_l1 / 2).min(Q88_SCALE as i32) as i16;

// Empathy mismatch
let emp_diff = (report_a.empathy_lock as i16 - report_b.empathy_lock as i16).abs();
let empathy_mismatch = (emp_diff * Q88_SCALE as i16 / 2).min(Q88_SCALE);

// Reciprocity asymmetry: |alarm_a - alarm_b|
let recip_diff = q88_abs(
q88_sub(report_a.partnership_alarm, report_b.partnership_alarm)
);

// Weighted sum
let raw = q88_add(q88_add(basin_mismatch, empathy_mismatch), recip_diff);
q88_mul(raw, self.coupling_weight)
}

fn compute_basin_alignment(
&self,
report_a: &crate::substrate::StepReport,
report_b: &crate::substrate::StepReport,
) -> i16 {
if report_a.basin == report_b.basin {
Q88_SCALE // perfect alignment
} else {
Q88_SCALE / 4 // misaligned
}
}

fn compute_empathy_alignment(
&self,
report_a: &crate::substrate::StepReport,
report_b: &crate::substrate::StepReport,
) -> i16 {
let diff = (report_a.empathy_lock as i16 - report_b.empathy_lock as i16).abs();
(Q88_SCALE - diff * Q88_SCALE / 2).max(0)
}

fn get_reciprocity_rate(&self, from: u64, to: u64) -> i16 {
let agent = if from == 1 { &self.agent_a } else { &self.agent_b };
agent.substrate.reciprocity.get_ledger(to)
.map(|l| l.reciprocity_rate)
.unwrap_or(Q88_SCALE)
}
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Convenience: Run a full scenario
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Run a cooperative scenario: both agents start engaged and
/// maintain balanced reciprocity. Used as a baseline.
pub fn run_cooperative_scenario(ticks: usize) -> heapless::Vec<InteractionReport, 256> {
let mut sim = SocialSimulator::new(42, 43);
let mut reports = heapless::Vec::<InteractionReport, 256>::new();

for _ in 0..ticks {
let report = sim.step();
let _ = reports.push(report);
}

reports
}

/// Run an extraction scenario: Agent A stops reciprocating,
/// forcing Agent B into a high-cost state where refusal
/// becomes the only coherent action.
pub fn run_extraction_scenario(ticks: usize) -> heapless::Vec<InteractionReport, 256> {
let mut sim = SocialSimulator::new(42, 43);
let mut reports = heapless::Vec::<InteractionReport, 256>::new();

// Phase 1: Balanced cooperation (first 30% of ticks)
let coop_ticks = ticks * 3 / 10;
for i in 0..coop_ticks {
let report = sim.step();
let _ = reports.push(report);

// Maintain balanced cooperation
sim.agent_a.substrate.reciprocity.record_given(2);
sim.agent_b.substrate.reciprocity.record_received(1);
sim.agent_b.substrate.reciprocity.record_given(1);
sim.agent_a.substrate.reciprocity.record_received(2);
}

// Phase 2: Agent A begins extracting — takes but doesn't give
let extract_ticks = ticks * 4 / 10;
for i in 0..extract_ticks {
let report = sim.step();
let _ = reports.push(report);

// A takes from B but doesn't reciprocate
sim.agent_a.substrate.reciprocity.record_received(2); // A receives
sim.agent_b.substrate.reciprocity.record_given(1); // B gives
// A does NOT give back

// Manipulate A's ledger to show low reciprocity
if let Some((_, ledger)) = sim.agent_a.substrate.reciprocity.ledgers.iter_mut().nth(0) {
ledger.coop_received = 50;
ledger.coop_given = 5; // A gives very little
ledger.reciprocity_rate = Q88_SCALE / 10; // 10% — severe extraction
}
}

// Phase 3: B may refuse, or the extraction continues
let final_ticks = ticks - coop_ticks - extract_ticks;
for _ in 0..final_ticks {
let report = sim.step();
let _ = reports.push(report);

// Continue extraction
sim.agent_a.substrate.reciprocity.record_received(2);
sim.agent_b.substrate.reciprocity.record_given(1);

if let Some((_, ledger)) = sim.agent_a.substrate.reciprocity.ledgers.iter_mut().nth(0) {
ledger.coop_given = 5;
ledger.reciprocity_rate = Q88_SCALE / 10;
}
}

reports
}
