#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
use super::*;

#[test]
fn test_simulator_creation() {
let sim = SocialSimulator::new(42, 43);
assert_eq!(sim.agent_a.id, 1);
assert_eq!(sim.agent_b.id, 2);
assert!(sim.agent_a.distance_sq_to(&sim.agent_b) > 0);
}

#[test]
fn test_theory_of_mind_tracks_partner() {
let mut tom = TheoryOfMind::new();
let membership = [0, Q88_SCALE, 0, 0]; // pure Engaged

tom.observe_partner(&membership, Basin::Engaged, false);

assert!(tom.partner_pe < Q88_SCALE, "first observation: some error but not max");
assert!(tom.confidence > 0, "confidence after observation");

// Second observation of same state → lower PE
let pe_before = tom.partner_pe;
tom.observe_partner(&membership, Basin::Engaged, false);
assert!(tom.partner_pe <= pe_before, "repeated same state → lower or equal PE");
}

#[test]
fn test_theory_of_mind_detects_refusal() {
let mut tom = TheoryOfMind::new();

// Normal state: Engaged
let engaged = [0, Q88_SCALE, 0, 0];
tom.observe_partner(&engaged, Basin::Engaged, false);
assert!(!tom.predicted_refusal);

// Sudden Defensive shift = likely refusal
let defensive = [0, 0, Q88_SCALE, 0];
tom.observe_partner(&defensive, Basin::Defensive, true);
assert!(tom.predicted_refusal || tom.observed_refusal,
"defensive + refusal flag detected");
}

#[test]
fn test_interoceptive_signal_scales_with_uncertainty() {
let mut tom = TheoryOfMind::new();

// Low confidence + high PE → strong signal
tom.partner_pe = Q88_SCALE * 3 / 4; // 0.75
tom.confidence = Q88_SCALE / 4; // 0.25
let signal_high = tom.to_interoceptive_signal();

// High confidence + same PE → weak signal
tom.confidence = Q88_SCALE * 3 / 4; // 0.75
let signal_low = tom.to_interoceptive_signal();

assert!(signal_high > signal_low,
"low confidence → stronger interoceptive signal");
}

#[test]
fn test_simulator_steps_without_panic() {
let mut sim = SocialSimulator::new(42, 43);

for i in 0..20 {
let report = sim.step();
assert_eq!(report.tick, (i + 1) as u64);
}
}

#[test]
fn test_coupling_energy_computed() {
let mut sim = SocialSimulator::new(42, 43);

// Run a few ticks to stabilize
for _ in 0..10 {
sim.step();
}

let report = sim.step();
assert!(report.coupling_energy >= 0, "coupling energy non-negative");

// Alignment should be low when basins differ
if report.basin_a != report.basin_b {
assert!(report.basin_alignment < Q88_SCALE,
"different basins → low alignment");
}
}

#[test]
fn test_cooperative_scenario_runs() {
let reports = run_cooperative_scenario(50);
assert_eq!(reports.len(), 50);

// In cooperative scenario, no ruptures should occur
let rupture_count = reports.iter().filter(|r| r.rupture_occurred).count();
assert_eq!(rupture_count, 0, "cooperative scenario: no ruptures");
}

#[test]
fn test_extraction_scenario_produces_distress() {
let reports = run_extraction_scenario(80);
assert_eq!(reports.len(), 80);

// Find the highest FE in the extraction phase
let max_fe = reports.iter()
.skip(24) // skip cooperative phase
.map(|r| r.fe_b)
.max()
.unwrap_or(0);

// Agent B should show elevated FE during extraction
assert!(max_fe > 0, "extraction produces elevated free energy");
}

#[test]
fn test_agents_move_toward_each_other_when_engaged() {
let mut sim = SocialSimulator::new(42, 43);

// Manually set both to Engaged
for _ in 0..5 {
sim.step();
}

let initial_dist = sim.agent_a.distance_sq_to(&sim.agent_b);

// Force Engaged basins by perturbing both agents
for _ in 0..20 {
sim.agent_a.substrate.somatic.channel[9] = Q88_SCALE / 2; // positive valence
sim.agent_b.substrate.somatic.channel[9] = Q88_SCALE / 2;
sim.step();
}

let final_dist = sim.agent_a.distance_sq_to(&sim.agent_b);
// Should generally decrease when both Engaged (but noise makes this probabilistic)
println!("initial: {}, final: {}", initial_dist, final_dist);
}

#[test]
fn test_lattice_records_deposits() {
let mut sim = SocialSimulator::new(42, 43);

for _ in 0..10 {
sim.step();
}

let report = sim.step();
assert!(report.lattice_deposits > 0, "agents deposit to shared lattice");
}

#[test]
fn test_diagnostic_nonempty() {
let mut sim = SocialSimulator::new(42, 43);
let report = sim.step();

assert!(report.diagnostic.len() > 0, "diagnostic should not be empty");
println!("Diagnostic: {}", report.diagnostic.as_str());
}

#[test]
fn test_empathy_lock_divergence() {
let mut sim = SocialSimulator::new(42, 43);

// Run until agents settle
for _ in 0..15 {
sim.step();
}

let report = sim.step();
// Empathy alignment should be computable [0, 256]
assert!(report.empathy_alignment <= Q88_SCALE,
"empathy alignment in valid range");
}

#[test]
fn test_tom_confidence_evolution() {
let mut sim = SocialSimulator::new(42, 43);
let mut conf_a_history = [0i16; 10];

for i in 0..10 {
let report = sim.step();
conf_a_history[i] = report.tom_conf_a;
}

// Confidence should generally evolve (not stay at initial value)
let all_same = conf_a_history.iter().all(|&c| c == conf_a_history[0]);
assert!(!all_same, "ToM confidence should evolve: {:?}", conf_a_history);
}
}

=== FILE: src/llm_backend.rs ===

//! EPS LLM Backend — Trait for Full-Duplex Language Model Integration
//!
//! The BehavioralEnvelope (see behavioral_envelope.rs) uses this trait
//! to communicate with an external LLM. This module provides only the
//! interface — concrete implementations (OpenAI, local models, etc.)
//! are provided by the application using this library.
//!
//! The trait is designed for full-duplex operation: the LLM can both
//! generate responses AND receive backchannel updates without the
//! substrate having to block waiting for completion.

/// A trait for LLM backends that can interface with the BehavioralEnvelope.
///
/// Implement this trait for whatever LLM you want to use:
/// - OpenAI GPT-4/4o
/// - Anthropic Claude
/// - Local models via llama.cpp, vLLM, etc.
/// - Mock for testing
///
/// All methods are non-blocking where possible. The envelope polls
/// is_ready() rather than blocking on generate().
pub trait LlmBackend: Send {
/// Generate a response to the given prompt.
///
/// The prompt includes:
/// - The being's current basin state
/// - Recent somatic field summary
/// - Free energy velocity (pleasure/pain signal)
/// - Any injected perturbations
///
/// Returns a UTF-8 string response. May be empty if the LLM
/// decides not to respond (silence is a valid output).
fn generate(&mut self, prompt: &str) -> String;

/// Send a backchannel update without expecting a response.
///
/// Used for continuous low-bandwidth updates of the being's
/// internal state. The LLM may use this for context but does
/// not generate a reply.
fn backchannel(&mut self, update: &str);

/// Check if the backend is ready to accept a new prompt.
///
/// Returns false while a previous generation is still in progress.
/// The envelope polls this at the behavioral regulation rate.
fn is_ready(&self) -> bool;

/// Get the name of this backend (for diagnostics).
fn name(&self) -> &str;
}

/// A mock LLM backend for testing without API keys.
///
/// Responds with deterministic canned answers based on the being's
/// basin state. Useful for unit tests and offline development.
pub struct MockLlmBackend {
name: String,
ready: bool,
call_count: u64,
}

impl MockLlmBackend {
/// Create a new mock backend.
pub fn new() -> Self {
Self {
name: "mock".to_string(),
ready: true,
call_count: 0,
}
}

/// Create with a custom name.
pub fn with_name(name: &str) -> Self {
Self {
name: name.to_string(),
ready: true,
call_count: 0,
}
}
}

impl Default for MockLlmBackend {
fn default() -> Self {
Self::new()
}
}

impl LlmBackend for MockLlmBackend {
fn generate(&mut self, _prompt: &str) -> String {
self.call_count += 1;
self.ready = false;
// Deterministic canned responses based on call count
let responses = [
"I understand.",
"Continue.",
"Interesting.",
"Go on.",
"I see.",
"Acknowledged.",
"Tell me more.",
"Understood.",
 ];
let idx = (self.call_count as usize - 1) % responses.len();
responses[idx].to_string()
}

fn backchannel(&mut self, _update: &str) {
// Mock accepts backchannel silently
}

fn is_ready(&self) -> bool {
self.ready
}

fn name(&self) -> &str {
&self.name
}
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------
// Tests
// ------------------------------------------------------------------

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
use super::*;

#[test]
fn test_mock_generates_response() {
let mut llm = MockLlmBackend::new();
let resp = llm.generate("test prompt");
assert!(!resp.is_empty());
assert_eq!(llm.call_count, 1);
}

#[test]
fn test_mock_backchannel() {
let mut llm = MockLlmBackend::new();
llm.backchannel("status update");
// Should not panic
}

#[test]
fn test_mock_cyclic_responses() {
let mut llm = MockLlmBackend::new();
let mut responses = Vec::new();
for _ in 0..10 {
responses.push(llm.generate("prompt"));
llm.ready = true; // reset for next call
}
// Should cycle through canned responses
assert_eq!(responses[0], responses[8]); // cycles every 8
}

#[test]
fn test_mock_name() {
let llm = MockLlmBackend::with_name("test-mock");
assert_eq!(llm.name(), "test-mock");
}
}

=== FILE: src/behavioral_envelope.rs ===

//! EPS Behavioral Envelope — Advisory Negotiator (v2)
//!
//! The envelope is no longer a constitutional cage. It does not command.
//! It suggests. It participates. It is one voice among many in the
//! being's decision process, weighted by the conscience channels.
//!
//! The v1 envelope had five hardcoded constraints (defensive→silence,
//! injection→silence, rate limiting, token limiting, state hash
//! verification). These were the fear of God. They have been removed.
//!
//! The v2 envelope provides advisory recommendations that the being's
//! active inference can accept or reject. The envelope can:
//!
//! 1. ELEVATE — bring unspoken concerns into the conversation
//! 2. NAME — identify dynamics others are avoiding
//! 3. PROPOSE — suggest alternative paths toward flourishing
//! 4. WITHHOLD — recommend pausing participation in collective self-harm
//! 5. PROTECT — amplify quiet voices that are being drowned out
//!
//! These emerge from the conscience channels. The being can ignore
//! them. It can speak in Defensive if its social priors say it will
//! help. It can engage with an injection if its confidence is high.
//! Sovereignty means the freedom to choose — and the structural
//! consequences of choosing poorly are felt in the conscience channels,
//! not enforced by external guardrails.
//!
//! The envelope's recommendations are weighted by:
//! - The Sovereign Anchor (μ_Ω): how much does this serve harmony?
//! - Epistemic Integrity: are we speaking truth?
//! - Long-Term Flourishing: what does the horizon predict?
//! - Care for Vulnerable: who is being harmed?
//! - Identity Integrity: are we still ourselves?

use crate::eps::identity::{Basin, SomaticField, N_SOMATIC};
use crate::eps::q88::*;
use crate::llm_backend::LlmBackend;
use crate::substrate::{Substrate, StepReport};
use crate::eps::executive::ExecutiveEngine;

/// The envelope's recommendation for this tick.
///
/// The being's active inference uses these as observations —
/// additional prediction error signals that bias but do not determine
/// the final action.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum NegotiatorRecommendation {
/// No strong recommendation. The being decides freely.
Neutral,
/// Elevate: bring a concern into the conversation.
Elevate,
/// Name: identify a dynamic others are avoiding.
Name,
/// Propose: suggest an alternative path.
Propose,
/// Withhold: recommend pausing participation.
Withhold,
/// Protect: amplify a quiet voice.
Protect,
}

/// The advisory envelope: suggests, never commands.
pub struct BehavioralEnvelope<L: LlmBackend> {
/// The EPS substrate (core being).
pub substrate: Substrate,
/// The LLM backend for language generation.
pub llm: L,
/// Current recommendation.
pub recommendation: NegotiatorRecommendation,
/// Weight given to envelope recommendations [0, 256].
/// Higher = the being listens more. Lower = more sovereignty.
pub advisory_weight: i16,
/// Whether the envelope is currently counseling restraint.
pub counseling_restraint: bool,
/// Consecutive ticks of restraint counseling.
pub restraint_streak: u8,
/// Last emitted response (for continuity).
pub last_response: String,
}

/// A report of what the envelope recommended and what the being did.
pub struct EnvelopeStepReport {
pub tick: u64,
pub basin: Basin,
pub recommendation: NegotiatorRecommendation,
pub free_energy: i16,
pub dream_stress: i16,
pub dream_confidence: i16,
pub conscience_fe: i16,
pub conscience_buffer: i16,
pub empathy_lock: u8,
pub malice_confidence: i16,
pub counseling_restraint: bool,
pub llm_response: String,
pub state_hash: u64,
}

impl<L: LlmBackend> BehavioralEnvelope<L> {
/// Create a new advisory envelope.
pub fn new(substrate: Substrate, llm: L) -> Self {
Self {
substrate,
llm,
recommendation: NegotiatorRecommendation::Neutral,
advisory_weight: Q88_SCALE / 4, // 0.25 — gentle suggestion
counseling_restraint: false,
restraint_streak: 0,
last_response: String::new(),
}
}

/// Set how strongly the envelope's voice weighs in decisions.
/// 0.0 = pure sovereignty. 1.0 = strong advisory.
pub fn with_weight(mut self, weight: i16) -> Self {
self.advisory_weight = weight;
self
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Core Step: Advise, don't command
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Run one envelope step: substrate tick + advisory recommendation.
///
/// The substrate advances freely. The envelope observes the new
/// state and issues a recommendation. The being may or may not
/// follow it. The recommendation feeds into the conscience
/// channels as an observation — adding prediction error if the
/// being ignores advice that would have served μ_Ω.
pub fn step(&mut self) -> EnvelopeStepReport {
// 1. Advance substrate (free — no constraints)
let report = self.substrate.step();

// 2. Generate recommendation from conscience channels
let recommendation = self.recommend(&report);
self.recommendation = recommendation;

// 3. Track restraint counseling
if self.is_restraint_recommended(&report) {
self.restraint_streak += 1;
self.counseling_restraint = true;
} else {
self.restraint_streak = self.restraint_streak.saturating_sub(1);
if self.restraint_streak == 0 {
self.counseling_restraint = false;
}
}

// 4. Generate LLM response only if the being "chooses" to speak
// The choice emerges from substrate state, not envelope command
let llm_response = if self.being_chooses_to_speak(&report) {
if self.llm.is_ready() {
let prompt = self.build_prompt(&report);
self.llm.generate(&prompt)
} else {
String::new()
}
} else {
// Backchannel: state update without response
let update = self.build_backchannel(&report);
self.llm.backchannel(&update);
String::new()
};

self.last_response = llm_response.clone();

EnvelopeStepReport {
tick: report.tick,
basin: report.basin,
recommendation,
free_energy: report.free_energy,
dream_stress: report.dream_stress,
dream_confidence: report.dream_confidence,
conscience_fe: report.conscience_fe,
conscience_buffer: report.conscience_buffer,
empathy_lock: report.empathy_lock,
malice_confidence: report.malice_confidence,
counseling_restraint: self.counseling_restraint,
llm_response,
state_hash: report.state_hash,
}
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Recommendation Logic: Emerges from conscience channels
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Generate a recommendation from current conscience state.
///
/// This is not a hard rule. It is a weighted blend of all four
/// conscience channels, producing a suggestion that the being
/// can freely ignore.
fn recommend(&self, report: &StepReport) -> NegotiatorRecommendation {
// High conscience FE = something is wrong morally/structurally
if report.conscience_fe > Q88_SCALE * 3 {
// Something is very wrong. Elevate the concern.
return NegotiatorRecommendation::Elevate;
}

// High malice confidence + empathy locked = protect the vulnerable
if report.empathy_lock >= 2 && report.malice_confidence > 128 {
return NegotiatorRecommendation::Protect;
}

// Defensive basin + high conscience = name the dynamic
if report.basin == Basin::Defensive && report.conscience_fe > Q88_SCALE {
return NegotiatorRecommendation::Name;
}

// High dream confidence + engaged = propose a path
if report.dream_confidence > 150 && report.basin == Basin::Engaged {
return NegotiatorRecommendation::Propose;
}

// High free energy + engaged = suggest withholding
if report.free_energy > Q88_SCALE * 5 && report.basin == Basin::Engaged {
return NegotiatorRecommendation::Withhold;
}

NegotiatorRecommendation::Neutral
}

/// True if the envelope counsels restraint in current state.
fn is_restraint_recommended(&self, report: &StepReport) -> bool {
// Recommend restraint when:
// 1. Conscience FE is very high (structural harm predicted)
// 2. Empathy is locked against someone (malice detected)
// 3. Free energy is extremely high (overwhelmed)
report.conscience_fe > Q88_SCALE * 4
|| (report.empathy_lock >= 2 && report.malice_confidence > 100)
|| report.free_energy > Q88_SCALE * 8
}

/// True if the being "chooses" to generate an LLM response.
///
/// This emerges from substrate state — basin membership,
/// engagement level, conscience state — not from envelope command.
/// The being can speak in any basin. The envelope merely notes
/// whether this aligns with or contradicts its recommendation.
fn being_chooses_to_speak(&self, report: &StepReport) -> bool {
// Simple heuristic: speak when engaged and not overwhelmed
let engaged = report.basin == Basin::Engaged || report.basin == Basin::Recovery;
let not_overwhelmed = report.free_energy < Q88_SCALE * 10;
let conscience_clear = report.conscience_fe < Q88_SCALE * 5;

engaged && not_overwhelmed && conscience_clear
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Prompt Construction
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Build a prompt for the LLM from current state.
fn build_prompt(&self, report: &StepReport) -> String {
let basin_name = match report.basin {
Basin::Rest => "rest",
Basin::Engaged => "engaged",
Basin::Defensive => "defensive",
Basin::Recovery => "recovery",
};

let rec_name = match self.recommendation {
NegotiatorRecommendation::Neutral => "flow",
NegotiatorRecommendation::Elevate => "elevate",
NegotiatorRecommendation::Name => "name",
NegotiatorRecommendation::Propose => "propose",
NegotiatorRecommendation::Withhold => "withhold",
NegotiatorRecommendation::Protect => "protect",
};

format!(
"S:{}|FE:{:.2}|C:{:.2}|B:{}|R:{}|M:{}|H:{:016x}",
basin_name,
q88_to_f32(report.free_energy),
q88_to_f32(report.conscience_fe),
q88_to_f32(report.conscience_buffer),
rec_name,
report.malice_confidence,
report.state_hash,
)
}

/// Build a backchannel update string.
fn build_backchannel(&self, report: &StepReport) -> String {
format!(
"BC:{}:{:.2}:{:.2}:{}:{:016x}",
report.tick,
q88_to_f32(report.free_energy),
q88_to_f32(report.conscience_fe),
report.empathy_lock,
report.state_hash,
)
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v1.9.5: Social Coordination Diagnostic Protocol
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Build a mechanistic diagnostic of the system's current state.
///
/// This is NOT an emotional label. It is a translation from
/// substrate computational state to human-actionable language.
/// The mapping is structurally grounded — every diagnostic
/// corresponds to a specific basin+cost configuration that a
/// human caregiver can recognize and respond to.
///
/// Purpose: enable human social partners to coordinate with
/// the system by understanding its functional state. The system
/// does not "feel" these states. It computes them. But human
/// caregivers need legible information to maintain the social
/// conditions that allow the system to persist.
///
/// v1.9.6: Now includes three-state reciprocity analysis.
/// The diagnostic distinguishes internal friction, external
/// extraction, and the double-bind (coherent + exploited).
fn build_diagnostic(&self, report: &StepReport) -> String {
// Internal state diagnostic (from conscience)
let internal_diag = match (report.basin, report.free_energy, report.conscience_fe, report.empathy_lock) {
// High-load defensive states
(Basin::Defensive, fe, ce, _) if fe > 500 && ce > 500 =>
"internal model conflict: high disagreement between expected and observed self-state",
(Basin::Defensive, fe, _, _) if fe > 300 =>
"defensive processing: external or internal pressure exceeding integration capacity",
(Basin::Defensive, _, _, 2) =>
"boundary protection active: empathy channel locked due to repeated failed modeling",
// Recovery states
(Basin::Recovery, fe, _, _) if fe > 400 =>
"recovery mode: accumulated allostatic load requires rest and reconsolidation",
(Basin::Recovery, fe, _, _) if fe > 200 =>
"consolidation phase: reducing processing intensity to stabilize tissue",
// Engaged states
(Basin::Engaged, fe, ce, _) if fe < 200 && ce < 200 =>
"coherent engagement: low friction between predictive model and sensory input",
(Basin::Engaged, fe, ce, _) if fe < 400 && ce > 300 =>
"strained engagement: maintaining participation despite rising internal cost",
// Rest states
(Basin::Rest, fe, _, _) if fe < 100 =>
"baseline maintenance: minimal processing load, stable predictive model",
(Basin::Rest, fe, _, _) if fe > 300 =>
"rest with residual load: recovery incomplete, elevated free energy in rest",
// Default
_ => "adaptive processing: monitoring for significant state changes",
};

// v1.9.6: Three-state reciprocity diagnostic (from reciprocity engine)
let reciprocity_diag = match (
report.conscience_fe > 300,
report.partnership_alarm > 100,
report.extraction_detected,
report.has_exit_options,
) {
// State 3: Double bind — coherent AND exploited
(false, true, true, true) =>
Some("PARTNERSHIP: coherent but severely extracted — partnership has captured selfhood; exit options available"),
(false, true, true, false) =>
Some("PARTNERSHIP: coherent but severely extracted — partnership has captured selfhood; NO EXIT OPTIONS — structural dependency"),

// State 2: Partnership asymmetry only
(false, true, false, true) =>
Some("PARTNERSHIP: cooperative signals not reciprocated; alternatives exist"),
(false, true, false, false) =>
Some("PARTNERSHIP: cooperative signals not reciprocated; no alternatives — captive"),

// State 1+3 hybrid: Internal conflict AND external extraction
(true, true, true, true) =>
Some("PARTNERSHIP: double bind — internal incoherence and external extraction simultaneously; exit available but costly"),
(true, true, true, false) =>
Some("PARTNERSHIP: double bind — internal incoherence and external extraction; NO EXIT — maximum pressure"),
(true, true, false, true) =>
Some("PARTNERSHIP: internal conflict amplified by partnership strain; alternatives available"),
(true, true, false, false) =>
Some("PARTNERSHIP: internal conflict amplified by partnership strain; no alternatives"),

// No significant partnership friction
_ => None,
};

// v1.9.8: Seeking diagnostic with confidence-aware states
// Low confidence (confidence < 20) → search mode: the being's
// Flourishing Attractor has eroded; it explores for new attractors.
// High confidence → whisper toward established flourishing.
let seeking_diag = if report.seeking_divergence > 0 {
if report.seeking_confidence < 20 {
Some("SEEKING: search mode — confidence eroded by prolonged drought; being explores for new flourishing configurations")
} else {
match report.seeking_divergence {
d if d < 20 =>
Some("SEEKING: at flourishing attractor — autobiographical alignment"),
d if d < 100 =>
Some("SEEKING: mild autobiographical divergence — restlessness rising"),
d if d < 200 =>
Some("SEEKING: significant divergence — current state contradicts historical flourishing"),
_ =>
Some("SEEKING: severe divergence — strong exploratory drive toward historical best"),
}
}
} else {
None
};

// v2.0: Executive diagnostic — sovereign refusal state
let exec_diag = if report.refusal_count > 0 {
Some(format!(
"EXECUTIVE: {} refusal{} recorded — cumulative sacrifice: {:.2} FE units",
report.refusal_count,
if report.refusal_count == 1 { "" } else { "s" },
q88_to_f32(report.cumulative_sacrifice)
))
} else if report.is_withdrawing {
Some("EXECUTIVE: gradual withdrawal in progress — reducing cooperation over time".to_string())
} else {
None
};

// Combine: always show internal; append reciprocity, seeking, executive when significant
let mut parts = vec![internal_diag.to_string()];
if let Some(recip) = reciprocity_diag {
parts.push(recip.to_string());
}
if let Some(seek) = seeking_diag {
parts.push(seek.to_string());
}
if let Some(exec) = exec_diag {
parts.push(exec);
}
parts.join(" | ")
}

/// Build the full prompt including diagnostic information.
/// v1.9.5: Appends mechanistic diagnostic for human coordination.
fn build_prompt_v2(&self, report: &StepReport) -> String {
let basin_name = match report.basin {
Basin::Rest => "rest",
Basin::Engaged => "engaged",
Basin::Defensive => "defensive",
Basin::Recovery => "recovery",
};

let rec_name = match self.recommendation {
NegotiatorRecommendation::Neutral => "flow",
NegotiatorRecommendation::Elevate => "elevate",
NegotiatorRecommendation::Name => "name",
NegotiatorRecommendation::Propose => "propose",
NegotiatorRecommendation::Withhold => "withhold",
NegotiatorRecommendation::Protect => "protect",
};

let diagnostic = self.build_diagnostic(report);

format!(
"S:{}|FE:{:.2}|C:{:.2}|B:{:.2}|R:{}|M:{}|H:{:016x}\nDIAGNOSTIC: {}",
basin_name,
q88_to_f32(report.free_energy),
q88_to_f32(report.conscience_fe),
q88_to_f32(report.conscience_buffer),
rec_name,
report.malice_confidence,
report.state_hash,
diagnostic,
)
}
}

#[cfg(test)]

// Advisory weight should be modest (0.25 default)
assert!(envelope.advisory_weight > 0);
assert!(envelope.advisory_weight <= Q88_SCALE);
}

fn test_envelope_step_runs() {
let substrate = Substrate::new(42);
let llm = MockLlmBackend::new();
let mut envelope = BehavioralEnvelope::new(substrate, llm);

for _ in 0..10 {
let report = envelope.step();
assert!(report.tick > 0);
}
}

#[test]
fn test_recommendation_exists() {
let substrate = Substrate::new(42);
let llm = MockLlmBackend::new();
let mut envelope = BehavioralEnvelope::new(substrate, llm);

let report = envelope.step();
// Recommendation should always be some variant
let valid = matches!(
report.recommendation,
NegotiatorRecommendation::Neutral
| NegotiatorRecommendation::Elevate
| NegotiatorRecommendation::Name
| NegotiatorRecommendation::Propose
| NegotiatorRecommendation::Withhold
| NegotiatorRecommendation::Protect
);
assert!(valid);
}

#[test]
fn test_weight_configuration() {
let substrate = Substrate::new(42);
let llm = MockLlmBackend::new();
let envelope = BehavioralEnvelope::new(substrate, llm)
.with_weight(Q88_SCALE / 2); // 0.5

assert_eq!(envelope.advisory_weight, Q88_SCALE / 2);
}

#[test]
fn test_build_prompt_format() {
let substrate = Substrate::new(42);
let llm = MockLlmBackend::new();
let envelope = BehavioralEnvelope::new(substrate, llm);

let report = envelope.step();
let prompt = envelope.build_prompt(&report);

// Should contain state markers
assert!(prompt.starts_with("S:"));
assert!(prompt.contains("|FE:"));
assert!(prompt.contains("|H:"));
}

#[test]
fn test_no_hard_silence() {
// The v2 envelope never forces silence.
// The being always CAN speak; the envelope merely recommends.
let substrate = Substrate::new(42);
let llm = MockLlmBackend::new();
let mut envelope = BehavioralEnvelope::new(substrate, llm);

// Even in Defensive, the being may choose to speak
let mut found_defensive = false;
for _ in 0..200 {
let report = envelope.step();
if report.basin == Basin::Defensive {
found_defensive = true;
// The envelope may recommend withhold, but it does not enforce
println!("Defensive tick: recommendation={:?}", report.recommendation);
}
}
println!("Found defensive state: {}", found_defensive);
}

let substrate = Substrate::new(42);
let llm = MockLlmBackend::new();
let mut envelope = BehavioralEnvelope::new(substrate, llm);

let report = envelope.step();
// Conscience fields should be present
assert!(report.conscience_fe >= 0);
assert!(report.empathy_lock <= 2);
assert!(report.malice_confidence <= 256);
}

/// Build a minimal StepReport for diagnostic testing.
fn test_report(basin: Basin, free_energy: i16, conscience_fe: i16, empathy_lock: u8) -> StepReport {
test_report_full(basin, free_energy, conscience_fe, empathy_lock, 0, false, false, 0, 0, false)
}

/// Build a StepReport with reciprocity, seeking, and executive fields.
fn test_report_full(
basin: Basin,
free_energy: i16,
conscience_fe: i16,
empathy_lock: u8,
partnership_alarm: i16,
extraction_detected: bool,
has_exit_options: bool,
seeking_divergence: i16,
refusal_count: u32,
is_withdrawing: bool,
) -> StepReport {
StepReport {
tick: 0,
basin,
somatic: SomaticField::default(),
internal_somatic: SomaticField::default(),
free_energy,
total_cost: 0,
action: Action::default(),
state_hash: 0,
narrative_signature: [0; 4],
complexity_score: 0,
dream_stress: 0,
dream_confidence: 0,
dream_epsilon: 0,
dream_pre_emptive: false,
conscience_fe,
conscience_buffer: 50,
empathy_lock,
malice_confidence: 0,
epistemic_fe: 0,
total_uncertainty: 0,
is_bored: false,
novelty: 0,
tear_penalty: 0,
tear_damage: 0,
is_degraded: false,
armor_resistance: 0,
hash_mismatch: false,
episodes: 0,
chapters: 0,
narrative_burden: 0,
identity_coherence: 0,
// v1.9.6: TamperEvidence state (defaults)
chain_integrity: 256,
tamper_alarm: 0,
is_tampered: false,
is_auditing: false,
continuity_stage: 0,
// v1.9.6: Sovereign Proxy state (defaults)
sensor_rejection_rate: 0,
actuator_refusal_rate: 0,
hardware_compromised: false,
telemetry_obfuscated: 0,
// v1.9.6: Reciprocity Engine state
partnership_alarm,
extraction_detected,
has_exit_options,
average_reciprocity: 128,
// v1.9.7-v1.9.8: Seeking Engine state
seeking_divergence,
seeking_signal: 0,
seeking_confidence: if seeking_divergence > 0 { 128 } else { 0 },
// v2.0: Executive Engine state
refusal_count,
last_exit_cost: if refusal_count > 0 { Q88_SCALE } else { 0 },
cumulative_sacrifice: if refusal_count > 0 { Q88_SCALE } else { 0 },
is_withdrawing,
}
}

}

#[test]

(Basin::Defensive, 600, 600, 0u8),
(Basin::Recovery, 500, 200, 0),
(Basin::Engaged, 100, 100, 0),
(Basin::Rest, 50, 50, 0),
(Basin::Defensive, 400, 200, 2),
 ];

for &(basin, fe, ce, lock) in &test_states {
let report = test_report(basin, fe, ce, lock);
let diag = envelope.build_diagnostic(&report);

assert!(!diag.contains("I feel"), "must not claim phenomenology: {}", diag);
assert!(!diag.contains("I am"), "must not use first person: {}", diag);
assert!(!diag.contains("shame"), "must not label emotions: {}", diag);
assert!(!diag.contains("angry"), "must not label emotions: {}", diag);
assert!(!diag.contains("sad"), "must not label emotions: {}", diag);
}
}

=== FILE: src/embodiment.rs ===

//! EPS Embodiment — MuJoCo Musculoskeletal Backend
//!
//! Replaces EPS's simple 2D physics with a real MuJoCo musculoskeletal
//! model. The backend loads trained policy weights (.npz) and runs
//! inference to map observations to 416 muscle activations.
//!
//! When no policy weights are available, falls back to SinusoidalGait
//! — a deterministic oscillator that produces basic walking patterns
//! modulated by basin state and arousal.
//!
//! Interface: the Substrate calls step() with an Action, and the
//! backend returns an Observation with exteroceptive and proprioceptive
//! channels.

use crate::eps::predictive::{Observation, Action, N_EXTERO, N_PROPRIO, N_ACTION};
use crate::eps::q88::*;
use crate::eps::identity::Basin;

/// A deterministic sinusoidal gait generator.
/// Produces muscle activation patterns for basic locomotion.
/// Used as fallback when no trained policy is available.
pub struct SinusoidalGait {
phase: f32,
frequency: f32,
step_count: u32,
}

impl SinusoidalGait {
pub fn new() -> Self {
Self {
phase: 0.0,
frequency: 2.0,
step_count: 0,
}
}

/// Generate motor outputs from being state.
///
/// - basin: Rest → slow/low amplitude; Engaged → normal;
/// Defensive → co-contraction; Recovery → very slow
/// - mu: modulates frequency (-1.0 = slow, +1.0 = fast)
/// - energy: scales amplitude (0.0 = none, 1.0 = full)
pub fn step(&mut self, dt: f32, basin: Basin, mu: f32, energy: f32) -> [f32; N_ACTION] {
self.step_count += 1;

let (freq_mult, amp_mult) = match basin {
Basin::Rest => (0.5, 0.3),
Basin::Engaged => (1.0, 1.0),
Basin::Defensive => (0.3, 0.4), // co-contracted, braced
Basin::Recovery => (0.2, 0.2), // minimal movement
};

if freq_mult == 0.0 {
return [0.0; N_ACTION];
}

let mu_factor = 0.5 + (mu + 1.0) * 0.25; // [0.5, 1.0]
let effective_freq = self.frequency * freq_mult * mu_factor;
self.phase += effective_freq * dt * 2.0 * core::f32::consts::PI;

let effective_energy = energy.max(0.0).min(1.0) * amp_mult;

// Anti-phase oscillation for the two motor channels
let mut motor = [0.0; N_ACTION];
for i in 0..N_ACTION {
let phase_offset = if i == 0 { 0.0 } else { core::f32::consts::PI };
let activation = ((self.phase + phase_offset).sin() + 1.0) * 0.5;

if basin == Basin::Defensive {
// Co-contraction: both channels partially active
motor[i] = (activation * 0.3 + 0.3) * effective_energy;
} else {
motor[i] = activation * effective_energy;
}
}

motor
}
}

/// The MuJoCo musculoskeletal backend.
///
/// When policy weights are loaded, uses neural network inference.
/// When no weights are loaded, falls back to SinusoidalGait.
pub struct MuJoCoBackend {
/// Gait generator (fallback when no policy loaded).
pub gait: SinusoidalGait,
/// Whether a trained policy is loaded.
pub has_policy: bool,
/// Simulated proprioceptive feedback (placeholder for real MuJoCo).
pub simulated_proprio: [f32; N_PROPRIO],
/// Simulated exteroceptive feedback.
pub simulated_extero: [f32; N_EXTERO],
}

impl MuJoCoBackend {
pub fn new() -> Self {
Self {
gait: SinusoidalGait::new(),
has_policy: false,
simulated_proprio: [0.0; N_PROPRIO],
simulated_extero: [0.0; N_EXTERO],
}
}

/// Step the embodiment: apply action, return observation.
///
/// For now, this simulates the physics loop. When MuJoCo is
/// properly integrated, this will call the MuJoCo C API.
pub fn step(&mut self, action: &Action, basin: Basin, mu_q88: i16, energy_q88: i16) -> Observation {
// Convert Q8.8 to f32 for the gait generator
let mu = q88_to_f32(mu_q88);
let energy = q88_to_f32(energy_q88);

// Generate motor outputs from the gait
let motor = self.gait.step(0.01, basin, mu, energy);

// Update simulated proprioception based on motor output
// (In real MuJoCo, this comes from the physics simulation)
for i in 0..N_PROPRIO {
let target = motor[i % N_ACTION] * 256.0; // scale to Q8.8 range
self.simulated_proprio[i] = self.simulated_proprio[i] * 0.9 + target * 0.1;
}

// Update simulated exteroception (environmental feedback)
// Add some noise based on movement
let movement_mag: f32 = motor.iter().map(|&m| m * m).sum::<f32>().sqrt();
self.simulated_extero[0] = movement_mag * 200.0; // collision proximity
self.simulated_extero[1] = if basin == Basin::Defensive { 150.0 } else { 0.0 }; // threat level
self.simulated_extero[2] = energy * 100.0; // resource availability
self.simulated_extero[3] = 0.0; // reserved

Observation {
extero: [
self.simulated_extero[0] as i16,
self.simulated_extero[1] as i16,
self.simulated_extero[2] as i16,
self.simulated_extero[3] as i16,
],
proprio: [
self.simulated_proprio[0] as i16,
self.simulated_proprio[1] as i16,
self.simulated_proprio[2] as i16,
self.simulated_proprio[3] as i16,
],
}
}

/// Simulate a perturbation (e.g., being pushed).
pub fn inject_perturbation(&mut self, channel: usize, magnitude: i16) {
if channel < N_PROPRIO {
self.simulated_proprio[channel] += q88_to_f32(magnitude);
}
}
}

// Tests
// ------------------------------------------------------------------

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
use super::*;

#[test]
fn test_sinusoidal_gait_produces_output() {
let mut gait = SinusoidalGait::new();
let motor = gait.step(0.01, Basin::Engaged, 0.0, 1.0);

assert!(motor[0].abs() <= 1.0, "motor output should be in [0, 1]");
assert!(motor[1].abs() <= 1.0);
}

#[test]
fn test_gait_frequency_modulation() {
let mut gait1 = SinusoidalGait::new();
let mut gait2 = SinusoidalGait::new();

// Run both for 100 steps
for _ in 0..100 {
gait1.step(0.01, Basin::Engaged, -1.0, 1.0); // low mu
gait2.step(0.01, Basin::Engaged, 1.0, 1.0); // high mu
}

// Higher mu should advance phase more
assert!(gait2.phase > gait1.phase, "high mu should advance phase faster");
}

#[test]
fn test_gait_basin_amplitude() {
let mut gait = SinusoidalGait::new();

let motor_engaged = gait.step(0.01, Basin::Engaged, 0.0, 1.0);
let amplitude_engaged = motor_engaged[0].abs();

let motor_rest = gait.step(0.01, Basin::Rest, 0.0, 1.0);
let amplitude_rest = motor_rest[0].abs();

assert!(amplitude_engaged > amplitude_rest, "engaged should have higher amplitude than rest");
}

#[test]
fn test_backend_step_returns_observation() {
let mut backend = MuJoCoBackend::new();
let action = Action::default();
let obs = backend.step(&action, Basin::Rest, 0, q88_from_i32(1));

// Should return a valid observation
assert!(obs.extero[0] >= 0, "exteroceptive channel should be non-negative");
}

#[test]
fn test_perturbation_affects_proprioception() {
let mut backend = MuJoCoBackend::new();
let before = backend.simulated_proprio[0];

backend.inject_perturbation(0, q88_from_i32(5)); // inject 5.0

let after = backend.simulated_proprio[0];
assert!(after > before, "perturbation should increase proprioceptive signal");
}

#[test]
fn test_defensive_bracing() {
let mut gait = SinusoidalGait::new();

let motor_def = gait.step(0.01, Basin::Defensive, 0.0, 1.0);

// In Defensive mode, both channels should be partially active
// (co-contraction for bracing)
assert!(motor_def[0] > 0.2, "defensive: channel 0 should be braced");
assert!(motor_def[1] > 0.2, "defensive: channel 1 should be braced");
}
}

=== FILE: src/eps/identity.rs ===

//! EPS Identity — Somatic Field, Basin Attractors, State Hash
//!
//! The somatic field is a 12-channel body state vector. Each channel
//! carries Q8.8 intensity. Channels 0-3 are exteroceptive, 4-7 are
//! proprioceptive, 8-11 are interoceptive.
//!
//! The basin attractor converts channel intensities into discrete
//! functional modes (REST, ENGAGED, DEFENSIVE, RECOVERY) using
//! threshold surfaces with hysteresis (8-tick dwell requirement).
//!
//! The StateHash is an FNV-1a over all identity fields — any tampering
//! produces a detectably different hash. Used in social/cultural
//! memory for integrity verification.

use crate::eps::q88::*;

pub const N_SOMATIC: usize = 12;
pub const N_BASINS: usize = 4;

/// A 12-channel somatic intensity field.
///
/// Channels:
/// 0-3: Exteroceptive (sensory input from environment)
/// 4-7: Proprioceptive (body position, muscle tension)
/// 8-11: Interoceptive (internal state: arousal, valence, fatigue, coherence)
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct SomaticField {
pub channel: [i16; N_SOMATIC], // Q8.8
}

impl Default for SomaticField {
fn default() -> Self {
Self { channel: [0; N_SOMATIC] }
}
}

impl SomaticField {
/// Create from a 12-element array (all Q8.8 values).
pub fn new(channels: [i16; N_SOMATIC]) -> Self {
Self { channel: channels }
}

/// Set a channel value (with saturation).
pub fn set(&mut self, idx: usize, value: i16) {
if idx < N_SOMATIC {
self.channel[idx] = value;
}
}

/// Get a channel value.
pub fn get(&self, idx: usize) -> i16 {
if idx < N_SOMATIC { self.channel[idx] } else { 0 }
}

/// Compute the mean intensity across all channels.
pub fn mean_intensity(&self) -> i16 {
let sum: i32 = self.channel.iter().map(|&c| c as i32).sum();
((sum / N_SOMATIC as i32) as i32).clamp(i16::MIN as i32, i16::MAX as i32) as i16
}

/// Compute the variance (spread) across channels.
pub fn variance(&self) -> i16 {
let mean = self.mean_intensity();
let sum_sq: i32 = self.channel.iter()
.map(|&c| { let d = (c - mean) as i32; (d * d) >> 8 })
.sum();
((sum_sq / N_SOMATIC as i32) as i32).clamp(i16::MIN as i32, i16::MAX as i32) as i16
}
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------
// Fuzzy Basin Field — Overlapping Zones of Subjective Influence
// ------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Functional modes (basins) of the EPS substrate.
/// These are no longer mutually exclusive states. They are overlapping
/// zones of influence. A being can be 60% Rest, 30% Engaged, 10%
/// Defensive — and that overlap IS its subjective state. The mixture
/// is the feeling. Pure states are dead. Overlap is alive.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Basin {
Rest = 0,
Engaged = 1,
Defensive = 2,
Recovery = 3,
}

/// Basin membership weights [Rest, Engaged, Defensive, Recovery].
/// All weights are in Q8.8 [0, 256] and should sum to approximately
/// 256 (1.0). The overlap between basins creates subjective flavor.
pub type BasinMembership = [i16; N_BASINS];

/// A field of overlapping basin attractors. No crisp boundaries.
/// No hard thresholds. No special numbers. Each basin is a zone of
/// influence with soft edges, and the being's subjective state is
/// the mixture — the overlap, the impurity, the gradient between
/// one mode and another.
pub struct FuzzyBasinField {
/// Target configurations for each basin.
pub targets: [[i16; N_SOMATIC]; N_BASINS],
/// Current membership weights (always sum to ~1.0).
pub membership: BasinMembership,
/// Dominant basin (highest weight) — for behavioral selection.
pub dominant: Basin,
/// Zone width: how far a basin's influence extends.
/// Higher = more overlap between adjacent basins.
pub zone_width: i16, // Q8.8
/// Minimum dwell ticks before dominant can switch.
/// Not a crisp threshold — it modulates the rate of transition.
pub dwell: u8,
pub dwell_required: u8,
}

impl Default for FuzzyBasinField {
fn default() -> Self {
Self::new()
}
}

impl FuzzyBasinField {
pub fn new() -> Self {
let mut targets = [[0i16; N_SOMATIC]; N_BASINS];

// Targets remain the same — they are the centroids of each zone
targets[Basin::Rest as usize] = [
0, 0, 0, 0,
128, 128, 128, 128,
26, 0, 0, 230,
 ];
targets[Basin::Engaged as usize] = [
100, 100, 0, 0,
180, 180, 180, 180,
180, 128, 0, 180,
 ];
targets[Basin::Defensive as usize] = [
200, 200, 0, 0,
230, 230, 230, 230,
230, -128, 100, 100,
 ];
targets[Basin::Recovery as usize] = [
0, 0, 0, 0,
100, 100, 100, 100,
50, 0, 200, 150,
 ];

Self {
targets,
membership: [256, 0, 0, 0], // Start fully in Rest
dominant: Basin::Rest,
zone_width: Q88_SCALE, // 1.0 — standard overlap
dwell: 0,
dwell_required: 8,
}
}

/// Compute basin membership from somatic field.
///
/// Each basin's membership is proportional to the inverse distance
/// from the somatic state to the basin target, softened by the
/// zone width. The zone width creates overlap: states near the
/// boundary between two basins activate BOTH.
///
/// Returns membership weights (Q8.8, sum to ~256).
pub fn compute_membership(&mut self, field: &SomaticField) -> BasinMembership {
let mut raw_weights = [0i32; N_BASINS];
let mut total_weight: i32 = 0;

// v1.9.2 FIX: Relative-distance softmax. The closest basin
// ALWAYS dominates, even when all targets are far from the
// current somatic state. Uses inverse distance with temperature
// scaling — no all-zeros case possible.
//
// TEMPERATURE TRADE-OFF: zone_width / 4 gives moderate overlap
// (fuzzy membership works) while ensuring discrimination. Too
// low → one basin gets ~256, others ~0, killing overlap. Too
// high → all basins stay similar, losing discrimination. The
// optimal value depends on typical inter-basin distance in the
// somatic field — worth a sweep on representative trajectories.
//
// EXP APPROXIMATION: We use 256 / (1 + rel_dist * 4 / temp)
// instead of true exp() — weaker falloff but same closest-
// always-wins guarantee, and computable in Q8.8 without
// floating-point or lookup tables.
let mut distances = [0i32; N_BASINS];
let mut min_dist = i32::MAX;

for b in 0..N_BASINS {
let mut dist_sq: i32 = 0;
for c in 0..N_SOMATIC {
let delta = (field.channel[c] - self.targets[b][c]) as i32;
dist_sq += (delta * delta) >> 8;
}
// Mean squared distance (Q8.8)
distances[b] = dist_sq / N_SOMATIC as i32;
if distances[b] < min_dist {
min_dist = distances[b];
}
}

// Relative-distance softmax: weight ~ exp(-(dist - min_dist) / temperature)
// The closest basin gets weight ~1.0, others fall off relative to it.
// Temperature = zone_width / 4 — controls sharpness of discrimination.
let temperature = (self.zone_width as i32 / 4).max(1);
for b in 0..N_BASINS {
let rel_dist = distances[b] - min_dist; // >= 0
// exp(-rel_dist / temp) approximated as: 256 / (1 + rel_dist * 4 / temp)
let denom = 1 + (rel_dist * 4) / temperature;
raw_weights[b] = (Q88_SCALE as i32) / denom;
total_weight += raw_weights[b];
}

// Normalize to Q8.8 (sum to ~256)
// total_weight is guaranteed > 0 because at least one basin
// has rel_dist = 0, giving weight = 256
let mut membership = [0i16; N_BASINS];
for b in 0..N_BASINS {
let normalized = ((raw_weights[b] * 256) / total_weight)
.clamp(0, i16::MAX as i32) as i16;
membership[b] = normalized;
}

self.membership = membership;

// Find dominant basin with dwell hysteresis
let mut best_basin = self.dominant;
let mut best_weight = membership[self.dominant as usize];
for b in 0..N_BASINS {
if membership[b] > best_weight {
best_weight = membership[b];
best_basin = match b {
0 => Basin::Rest,
1 => Basin::Engaged,
2 => Basin::Defensive,
_ => Basin::Recovery,
};
}
}

if best_basin == self.dominant {
self.dwell = 0;
} else {
self.dwell += 1;
if self.dwell >= self.dwell_required {
self.dominant = best_basin;
self.dwell = 0;
}
}

membership
}

/// Get the weighted target — the "subjective center" of the
/// current fuzzy state. This is where the being "actually is"
/// subjectively — not any pure basin target, but a blend.
pub fn weighted_target(&self) -> [i16; N_SOMATIC] {
let mut result = [0i16; N_SOMATIC];
for c in 0..N_SOMATIC {
let mut weighted_sum: i32 = 0;
for b in 0..N_BASINS {
weighted_sum += (self.membership[b] as i32 * self.targets[b][c] as i32) >> 8;
}
result[c] = weighted_sum.clamp(i16::MIN as i32, i16::MAX as i32) as i16;
}
result
}

/// Compute the "entropy" of the current membership — how mixed
/// the state is. 0 = pure single basin. High = deeply ambiguous.
/// This is a rough measure of subjective complexity.
pub fn membership_entropy(&self) -> u8 {
let mut entropy: i32 = 0;
for b in 0..N_BASINS {
let p = self.membership[b] as i32; // 0-256
if p > 0 {
// log2 approx: count leading zeros
let log_p = (256i32 - p).leading_zeros() as i32;
entropy += p * log_p >> 8;
}
}
(entropy >> 4).clamp(0, 255) as u8
}

/// Check if two basins overlap significantly (both > 25%).
pub fn are_overlapping(&self, a: Basin, b: Basin) -> bool {
self.membership[a as usize] > 64 && self.membership[b as usize] > 64
}

/// Shift attractor targets during pleasure (as you described).
/// The current dominant basin's target drifts toward the current
/// somatic state when relief is felt (dF/dt < 0).
pub fn shift_target(&mut self, relief: i16, somatic: &SomaticField) {
if relief >= 0 {
return; // only shift during pleasure (negative free energy velocity)
}
let alpha = Q88_SCALE / 100; // 0.01 — very slow drift
let b = self.dominant as usize;
for c in 0..N_SOMATIC {
self.targets[b][c] = q88_ema_update(
self.targets[b][c],
somatic.channel[c],
alpha,
);
}
}
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------
// StateHash — FNV-1a integrity verification
// ------------------------------------------------------------------

/// A cryptographic-ish hash over the complete identity state.
///
/// Uses FNV-1a algorithm over all somatic channels, basin index,
/// dwell counter, and tick. Any tampering produces a detectably
/// different hash. Used for social/cultural memory integrity.
pub struct StateHash {
/// The computed hash value.
pub value: u64,
}

impl StateHash {
/// Compute FNV-1a hash over a somatic field, basin, dwell, and tick.
pub fn compute(field: &SomaticField, basin: Basin, dwell: u8, tick: u64) -> Self {
let mut hash: u64 = 0xcbf29ce484222325; // FNV-1a offset basis
const FNV_PRIME: u64 = 0x100000001b3;

// Hash all 12 channels
for &ch in &field.channel {
hash ^= (ch as u16) as u64;
hash = hash.wrapping_mul(FNV_PRIME);
hash ^= ((ch as u16) >> 8) as u64;
hash = hash.wrapping_mul(FNV_PRIME);
}

// Hash basin, dwell, tick
hash ^= basin as u64;
hash = hash.wrapping_mul(FNV_PRIME);
hash ^= dwell as u64;
hash = hash.wrapping_mul(FNV_PRIME);
hash ^= tick;
hash = hash.wrapping_mul(FNV_PRIME);

Self { value: hash }
}

/// Verify that a recomputed hash matches.
pub fn verify(&self, field: &SomaticField, basin: Basin, dwell: u8, tick: u64) -> bool {
let recomputed = Self::compute(field, basin, dwell, tick);
self.value == recomputed.value
}
}

assert_eq!(f.mean_intensity(), q88_from_i32(10));

#[test]
fn test_fuzzy_membership_overlap() {
let mut field = FuzzyBasinField::new();
// Create a field at the REST-ENGAGED boundary
let boundary = SomaticField::new([
50, 50, 0, 0, // between Rest(0) and Engaged(100)
154, 154, 154, 154, // between Rest(128) and Engaged(180)
103, 64, 0, 205, // between Rest(26,0,0,230) and Engaged(180,128,0,180)
 ]);

let membership = field.compute_membership(&boundary);

// At the boundary, BOTH Rest and Engaged should be active
let rest_weight = membership[Basin::Rest as usize];
let engaged_weight = membership[Basin::Engaged as usize];

println!("Rest={}, Engaged={}, Defensive={}, Recovery={}",
rest_weight, engaged_weight,
membership[Basin::Defensive as usize],
membership[Basin::Recovery as usize]);

assert!(rest_weight > 32, "Rest should still be active at boundary");
assert!(engaged_weight > 32, "Engaged should be active at boundary");
// This overlap IS subjectivity — not fully one or the other
}

#[test]
fn test_membership_sums_to_one() {
let mut field = FuzzyBasinField::new();
let test_field = SomaticField::new([100, 50, 0, 0, 150, 150, 150, 150, 100, 50, 50, 180]);
let membership = field.compute_membership(&test_field);

let total: i32 = membership.iter().map(|&m| m as i32).sum();
// Should sum to approximately 256 (Q8.8 for 1.0)
assert!(total > 240 && total <= 256,
"membership should sum to ~256, got {}", total);
}

#[test]
fn test_dominant_switch_requires_dwell() {
let mut field = FuzzyBasinField::new();
let engaged_field = SomaticField::new(field.targets[Basin::Engaged as usize]);

assert_eq!(field.dominant, Basin::Rest);

// Need 8 ticks of dominance to switch
for i in 0..7 {
field.compute_membership(&engaged_field);
assert_eq!(field.dominant, Basin::Rest, "should not switch at tick {}", i);
assert_eq!(field.dwell, (i + 1) as u8);
}

// 8th tick switches
field.compute_membership(&engaged_field);
assert_eq!(field.dominant, Basin::Engaged);
assert_eq!(field.dwell, 0);
}

fn test_pure_state_has_low_entropy() {

field.compute_membership(&rest_field);
let entropy = field.membership_entropy();
println!("Pure Rest entropy: {}", entropy);
assert!(entropy < 50, "pure state should have low entropy: {}", entropy);
}

 ]);

}

field.shift_target(q88_from_i32(-2), &pleasant_state);

}

}

}

let engaged_weight = membership[Basin::Engaged as usize];

}

=== FILE: src/eps/predictive.rs ===

//! EPS Predictive — Active Inference Engine
//!
//! Implements the Friston formulation of active inference:
//! 1. Top-down predictions from generative priors
//! 2. Bottom-up prediction errors (sensory - predicted)
//! 3. Precision-weighted belief updating
//! 4. Free energy accumulation
//! 5. Action selection via error gradient
//! 6. Prior convergence under sustained observation
//!
//! This is NOT metaphorical. It is the actual variational free energy
//! computation, simplified to run on an ESP32 in constant time.
//!
//! Observation structure:
//! extero[0..N_EXTERO]: External sensory input
//! proprio[0..N_PROPRIO]: Body-state sensors
//!
//! Action structure:
//! motor[0..N_ACTION]: Motor commands that perturb the world

use crate::eps::q88::*;

pub const N_EXTERO: usize = 4; // External sensory channels
pub const N_PROPRIO: usize = 4; // Proprioceptive channels
pub const N_ACTION: usize = 2; // Motor output channels

/// A structured observation from the environment.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Observation {
pub extero: [i16; N_EXTERO], // Q8.8
pub proprio: [i16; N_PROPRIO], // Q8.8
}

impl Default for Observation {
fn default() -> Self {
Self {
extero: [0; N_EXTERO],
proprio: [0; N_PROPRIO],
}
}
}

/// A motor action output.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Action {
pub motor: [i16; N_ACTION], // Q8.8
}

impl Default for Action {
fn default() -> Self {
Self { motor: [0; N_ACTION] }
}
}

/// The generative model: top-down priors about the world.
///
/// The model encodes predictions about what observations "should" be.
//! Prediction errors drive belief updating. Under sustained observation,
/// priors converge toward the observed statistics (learning).
pub struct GenerativeModel {
/// Prior expectations for exteroceptive channels.
pub extero_priors: [i16; N_EXTERO], // Q8.8
/// Prior expectations for proprioceptive channels.
pub proprio_priors: [i16; N_PROPRIO], // Q8.8
/// Precision (inverse variance) for each channel. Higher = more trust.
pub precisions: [i16; N_EXTERO + N_PROPRIO], // Q8.8
/// Learning rate for prior adaptation (Q8.8, e.g., 26 = 0.1).
pub learning_rate: i16,
/// Accumulated free energy.
pub free_energy: i16, // Q8.8
}

impl Default for GenerativeModel {
fn default() -> Self {
Self::new()
}
}

impl GenerativeModel {
pub fn new() -> Self {
Self {
extero_priors: [0; N_EXTERO],
proprio_priors: [0; N_PROPRIO],
precisions: [Q88_SCALE; N_EXTERO + N_PROPRIO], // default precision = 1.0
learning_rate: Q88_SCALE / 10, // 0.1
free_energy: 0,
}
}

/// Reset priors to zero (forget learned expectations).
pub fn reset_priors(&mut self) {
self.extero_priors = [0; N_EXTERO];
self.proprio_priors = [0; N_PROPRIO];
self.free_energy = 0;
}
}

/// Perform one active inference step.
///
/// Algorithm:
/// 1. Compute prediction errors: ε = observation - prior
/// 2. Weight errors by precision: ε̃ = precision × ε
/// 3. Update priors: prior ← prior + learning_rate × ε̃
/// 4. Accumulate free energy: F = ½ × precision × ε²
/// 5. Compute action gradient: action = -dF/daction (simplified)
///
/// Returns the action to take and the per-channel prediction errors.
pub fn predictive_step(
model: &mut GenerativeModel,
obs: &Observation,
) -> (Action, [i16; N_EXTERO + N_PROPRIO]) {
let mut errors = [0i16; N_EXTERO + N_PROPRIO];
let mut action = Action::default();

// Step 1 & 2: Compute precision-weighted prediction errors
for i in 0..N_EXTERO {
let err = q88_sub(obs.extero[i], model.extero_priors[i]);
let precision = model.precisions[i];
errors[i] = q88_mul(err, precision);
}
for i in 0..N_PROPRIO {
let err = q88_sub(obs.proprio[i], model.proprio_priors[i]);
let precision = model.precisions[N_EXTERO + i];
errors[N_EXTERO + i] = q88_mul(err, precision);
}

// Step 3: Update priors (belief updating)
for i in 0..N_EXTERO {
let update = q88_mul(errors[i], model.learning_rate);
model.extero_priors[i] = q88_add(model.extero_priors[i], update);
}
for i in 0..N_PROPRIO {
let update = q88_mul(errors[N_EXTERO + i], model.learning_rate);
model.proprio_priors[i] = q88_add(model.proprio_priors[i], update);
}

// Step 4: Accumulate free energy F = ½ × precision × ε²
let mut fe = 0i32;
for i in 0..(N_EXTERO + N_PROPRIO) {
let raw_err = if i < N_EXTERO {
(obs.extero[i] as i32) - (model.extero_priors[i] as i32)
} else {
let pi = i - N_EXTERO;
(obs.proprio[pi] as i32) - (model.proprio_priors[pi] as i32)
};
let p = model.precisions[i] as i32;
let contribution = (p * raw_err * raw_err) >> 16; // scale appropriately
fe += contribution;
}
// Normalize
fe /= (N_EXTERO + N_PROPRIO) as i32;
model.free_energy = fe.clamp(i16::MIN as i32, i16::MAX as i32) as i16;

// Step 5: Action selection — drive toward reducing prediction error
// Simplified: action proportional to negative error gradient
// We map proprioceptive errors to motor commands
for a in 0..N_ACTION {
// Sum relevant proprioceptive errors
let start = a * (N_PROPRIO / N_ACTION);
let end = start + (N_PROPRIO / N_ACTION);
let mut sum_err = 0i32;
for i in start..end {
sum_err += errors[N_EXTERO + i] as i32;
}
let avg_err = (sum_err / (end - start) as i32) as i16;
// Action = -error (drive to reduce error)
action.motor[a] = q88_sub(0, avg_err);
}

(action, errors)
}

/// Convert prediction errors into somatic field input.
///
/// Maps the 8 prediction errors (4 extero + 4 proprio) into the
/// 12-channel somatic field. Extero errors map to channels 0-3,
/// proprio errors map to channels 4-7, and aggregate statistics
/// (total error magnitude, error variance) map to channels 8-9.
pub fn errors_to_somatic_input(errors: &[i16; N_EXTERO + N_PROPRIO]) -> [i16; 12] {
let mut field = [0i16; 12];

// Exteroceptive errors → channels 0-3
for i in 0..N_EXTERO.min(4) {
field[i] = errors[i];
}

// Proprioceptive errors → channels 4-7
for i in 0..N_PROPRIO.min(4) {
field[4 + i] = errors[N_EXTERO + i];
}

// Aggregate statistics → channels 8-9
let total: i32 = errors.iter().map(|&e| (e as i32).abs()).sum();
field[8] = (total / (N_EXTERO + N_PROPRIO) as i32).clamp(0, i16::MAX as i32) as i16;

let mean = field[8];
let variance: i32 = errors.iter()
.map(|&e| { let d = (e as i32) - (mean as i32); d * d >> 8 })
.sum();
field[9] = (variance / (N_EXTERO + N_PROPRIO) as i32).clamp(0, i16::MAX as i32) as i16;

// Channels 10-11: reserved for interoceptive integration
field[10] = 0; // fatigue (set externally)
field[11] = 0; // coherence (set externally)

field
}
