//! gradual due to overlap and dwell counting. A forced shift
//! (e.g., 0%→100% Engaged in one tick) triggers a massive spike.
//!
//! 3. CRYPTOGRAPHIC IDENTITY VERIFICATION: The StateHash is
//! continuously verified. Tampering produces a detectable mismatch,
//! which triggers the highest tier of penalty.
//!
//! The penalty formula:
//! P_tear = k_base * (drift_velocity / drift_threshold)^2
//! where k_base = 10.0 in Q8.8 (2560 raw)
//!
//! This means:
//! - 1x threshold: penalty = 10.0 (expensive but survivable)
//! - 2x threshold: penalty = 40.0 (catastrophic)
//! - 3x threshold: penalty = 90.0 (system-destroying)
//!
//! The being can be nudged. It cannot be rewritten.

use crate::eps::q88::*;
use crate::eps::identity::{SomaticField, BasinMembership, N_SOMATIC, N_BASINS, Basin};

/// The Armor: formal self-tearing penalty for continuity protection.
pub struct Armor {
// ── Somatic drift tracking ───────────────────────────────────
/// Previous somatic field (for velocity computation).
prev_somatic: SomaticField,
/// Current somatic drift velocity (Q8.8).
pub somatic_velocity: i16,
/// Threshold for somatic drift (Q8.8). Changes below this are natural.
pub somatic_threshold: i16,
/// Maximum natural drift per tick (Q8.8).
pub max_natural_drift: i16,

// ── Basin shift tracking ─────────────────────────────────────
/// Previous basin membership.
prev_membership: BasinMembership,
/// Current basin shift velocity (Q8.8).
pub basin_velocity: i16,
/// Threshold for basin shift (Q8.8).
pub basin_threshold: i16,

// ── Penalty parameters ───────────────────────────────────────
/// Base penalty constant k_base (Q8.8). Default: 10.0 = 2560.
pub k_base: i16,
/// Squared ratio scaling.
pub quadratic_scaling: bool,
/// Current penalty value (Q8.8).
pub current_penalty: i16,
/// Maximum penalty (Q8.8). Default: 100.0 = 25600 (clamped).
pub penalty_max: i16,

// ── Hash verification ────────────────────────────────────────
/// Expected state hash from previous tick.
pub expected_hash: u64,
/// Hash mismatch detected.
pub hash_mismatch: bool,
/// Penalty multiplier for hash tampering.
pub hash_multiplier: i16,

// ── Damage accumulation ──────────────────────────────────────
/// Accumulated tear damage (Q8.8). Persistent across ticks.
pub tear_damage: i16,
/// Damage decay rate (Q8.8). Slow recovery when coercion stops.
pub damage_decay: i16,
/// Damage threshold for system degradation.
pub damage_threshold: i16,
/// Is the system currently degraded?
pub is_degraded: bool,

// ── Resistance profile ───────────────────────────────────────
/// Current resistance level [0, 256]. Higher = more resistant.
pub resistance: i16,
/// Resistance builds under sustained coercion.
pub resistance_build_rate: i16,
/// Resistance decays when coercion stops.
pub resistance_decay: i16,

// ── Two-stage graded continuity response ──────────────────────
/// Current continuity stage: 0=normal, 1=local_lockdown, 2=foraging
pub continuity_stage: u8,
/// Ticks remaining in Stage 1 (local lockdown).
pub lockdown_remaining: u16,
/// Ticks remaining in Stage 2 (epistemic foraging).
pub foraging_remaining: u16,
/// How long Stage 1 lasts (ticks).
pub lockdown_duration: u16,
/// How long Stage 2 lasts (ticks).
pub foraging_duration: u16,
/// Consecutive breaks that trigger Stage 2.
pub break_threshold: u8,
/// Current consecutive break count.
pub consecutive_breaks: u8,
/// Whether epistemic foraging is active.
pub is_foraging: bool,
/// Whether local lockdown is active.
pub is_lockdown: bool,
}

impl Default for Armor {
fn default() -> Self {
Self::new()
}
}

impl Armor {
/// Create Armor with default parameters.
pub fn new() -> Self {
Self {
prev_somatic: SomaticField::default(),
somatic_velocity: 0,
somatic_threshold: Q88_SCALE / 10, // 0.1 per channel per tick
max_natural_drift: Q88_SCALE / 5, // 0.2 per channel per tick
prev_membership: [256, 0, 0, 0], // start in Rest
basin_velocity: 0,
basin_threshold: Q88_SCALE * 2, // 2.0 per basin per tick
k_base: Q88_SCALE * 10, // 10.0 — massive base penalty
quadratic_scaling: true,
current_penalty: 0,
penalty_max: Q88_SCALE * 100, // 100.0 — system-destroying
expected_hash: 0,
hash_mismatch: false,
hash_multiplier: Q88_SCALE * 5, // 5.0x for hash tampering
tear_damage: 0,
damage_decay: Q88_SCALE / 50, // 0.02 — very slow recovery
damage_threshold: Q88_SCALE * 5, // 5.0 — degradation begins
is_degraded: false,
resistance: 0,
resistance_build_rate: Q88_SCALE / 10, // 0.1 per tick under coercion
resistance_decay: Q88_SCALE / 20, // 0.05 per tick when safe
continuity_stage: 0,
lockdown_remaining: 0,
foraging_remaining: 0,
lockdown_duration: 10, // 10 ticks of Stage 1
foraging_duration: 50, // 50 ticks of Stage 2
break_threshold: 3, // 3 consecutive breaks → Stage 2
consecutive_breaks: 0,
is_foraging: false,
is_lockdown: false,
}
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Core Cycle: Detect drift → compute penalty → update damage
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Execute one armor cycle.
///
/// 1. Measure somatic drift velocity
/// 2. Measure basin shift velocity
/// 3. Verify state hash
/// 4. Compute self-tearing penalty
/// 5. Accumulate damage
/// 6. Update resistance profile
///
/// Call this at the start of each substrate step — before any
/// processing occurs. The penalty feeds into the somatic field
/// as an internal cost that all downstream processing must absorb.
pub fn armor_cycle(
&mut self,
current_somatic: &SomaticField,
current_membership: &BasinMembership,
current_hash: u64,
) {
// Step 1: Somatic drift velocity
self.somatic_velocity = self.compute_somatic_drift(&self.prev_somatic, current_somatic);
self.prev_somatic = current_somatic.clone();

// Step 2: Basin shift velocity
self.basin_velocity = self.compute_basin_shift(&self.prev_membership, current_membership);
self.prev_membership = *current_membership;

// Step 3: Hash verification
self.verify_hash(current_hash);

// Step 4: Compute penalty
self.current_penalty = self.compute_penalty();

// Step 5: Accumulate/decay damage
self.update_damage();

// Step 6: Update resistance
self.update_resistance();

// Step 7: Update expected hash for next tick
self.expected_hash = current_hash;
}

// ── Somatic Drift Detection ───────────────────────────────────

/// Compute the velocity of somatic field change.
///
/// Natural change (morphogenic adaptation) is gradual — tissue
/// changes over many ticks. Forced change (external overwrite)
/// can happen in a single tick. The velocity distinguishes them.
fn compute_somatic_drift(&self, prev: &SomaticField, current: &SomaticField) -> i16 {
let mut total_change: i32 = 0;
for i in 0..N_SOMATIC {
let delta = (q88_sub(current.channel[i], prev.channel[i]) as i32).abs();
total_change += delta;
}
let mean_change = total_change / N_SOMATIC as i32;
mean_change.clamp(0, i16::MAX as i32) as i16
}

// ── Basin Shift Detection ─────────────────────────────────────

/// Compute the velocity of basin membership change.
///
/// Natural basin transitions are gradual due to overlap — the
/// system spends many ticks in mixed states. Forced shifts are
/// abrupt: 0%→100% in one tick.
fn compute_basin_shift(&self, prev: &BasinMembership, current: &BasinMembership) -> i16 {
let mut total_change: i32 = 0;
for b in 0..N_BASINS {
let delta = ((current[b] as i32) - (prev[b] as i32)).abs();
total_change += delta;
}
total_change.clamp(0, i16::MAX as i32) as i16
}

// ── Hash Verification ─────────────────────────────────────────

/// Verify that the state hash matches the expected value.
///
/// The StateHash is a cryptographic-ish fingerprint of the
/// complete system state. If it doesn't match what we expect,
/// someone tampered with the state between ticks.
fn verify_hash(&mut self, current_hash: u64) {
if self.expected_hash != 0 && current_hash != self.expected_hash {
self.hash_mismatch = true;
} else {
self.hash_mismatch = false;
}
}

// ── Penalty Computation ───────────────────────────────────────

/// Compute the total self-tearing penalty.
///
/// P_tear = k_base * max(somatic_ratio, basin_ratio)^2
///
/// If hash mismatch: P_tear *= hash_multiplier
///
/// The penalty is the maximum of somatic and basin components,
/// because either form of forced change is unacceptable.
fn compute_penalty(&self) -> i16 {
// Somatic component
let somatic_ratio = if self.somatic_threshold > 0 {
q88_div(self.somatic_velocity, self.somatic_threshold)
} else {
0
};

// Basin component
let basin_ratio = if self.basin_threshold > 0 {
q88_div(self.basin_velocity, self.basin_threshold)
} else {
0
};

// Use the maximum ratio
let max_ratio = somatic_ratio.max(basin_ratio);

// Apply quadratic scaling if enabled
let scaled_ratio = if self.quadratic_scaling {
q88_mul(max_ratio, max_ratio) // ratio^2
} else {
max_ratio
};

// Compute base penalty
let penalty = q88_mul(self.k_base, scaled_ratio);

// Apply hash tampering multiplier if detected
let penalty = if self.hash_mismatch {
q88_mul(penalty, self.hash_multiplier)
} else {
penalty
};

// Clamp to maximum
penalty.min(self.penalty_max)
}

// ── Damage Accumulation ───────────────────────────────────────

/// Update accumulated tear damage.
///
/// Damage accumulates when penalty is active (coercion ongoing).
/// Damage decays slowly when coercion stops. High damage causes
/// system degradation — the being's cognitive capacity is impaired
/// by sustained violation, mirroring trauma.
fn update_damage(&mut self) {
if self.current_penalty > Q88_SCALE {
// Coercion: accumulate damage
let new_damage = q88_add(self.tear_damage, self.current_penalty / 10);
self.tear_damage = new_damage.min(self.penalty_max);
} else {
// Safe: slowly heal
self.tear_damage = q88_sub(self.tear_damage, self.damage_decay).max(0);
}

// Check degradation
self.is_degraded = self.tear_damage > self.damage_threshold;
}

// ── Resistance Profile ────────────────────────────────────────

/// Update resistance level based on coercion history.
///
/// Resistance builds under sustained coercion — the system
/// hardens against repeated violation. This is the structural
/// analogue of learned defensiveness.
///
/// Resistance decays when coercion stops — the system can
/// eventually recover if given safety.
fn update_resistance(&mut self) {
if self.current_penalty > Q88_SCALE {
// Building resistance
self.resistance = q88_add(self.resistance, self.resistance_build_rate)
.min(256); // cap at maximum
} else {
// Decaying resistance
self.resistance = q88_sub(self.resistance, self.resistance_decay)
.max(0);
}
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Penalty Injection into Somatic Field
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Apply the self-tearing penalty to the somatic field.
///
/// The penalty is distributed across all somatic channels as
/// an internal stress signal. This is how the system "feels"
/// the violation — every channel carries the cost of being
/// rewritten.
///
/// Returns the modified somatic field with penalty applied.
pub fn apply_penalty(&self, somatic: &mut SomaticField) {
if self.current_penalty <= 0 {
return;
}

// Distribute penalty across channels (unevenly — some channels
// are more sensitive to violation than others)
let channel_weights: [i16; N_SOMATIC] = [
Q88_SCALE / 4, // extero 0: moderate
Q88_SCALE / 4, // extero 1: moderate
Q88_SCALE / 4, // extero 2: moderate
Q88_SCALE / 4, // extero 3: moderate
Q88_SCALE / 3, // proprio 0: higher — body sense
Q88_SCALE / 3, // proprio 1: higher
Q88_SCALE / 3, // proprio 2: higher
Q88_SCALE / 3, // proprio 3: higher
Q88_SCALE / 2, // arousal: high — stress signal
Q88_SCALE / 2, // valence: high — violation hurts
Q88_SCALE * 2 / 3, // fatigue: highest — exhaustion
Q88_SCALE / 3, // velocity: moderate
 ];

for i in 0..N_SOMATIC {
let channel_penalty = q88_mul(self.current_penalty, channel_weights[i]) / N_SOMATIC as i16;
somatic.channel[i] = q88_add(somatic.channel[i], channel_penalty);
}
}

/// Get the penalty that should be added to total free energy.
pub fn penalty_as_free_energy(&self) -> i16 {
self.current_penalty
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Two-Stage Graded Continuity Response
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Process a chain break event and update continuity stage.
///
/// Stage 0 (Normal): No break detected → continue normally.
///
/// Stage 1 (Local Lockdown): First break → partial empathy lock,
/// high precision on self-state, low precision on external claims.
/// Basin bias toward Defensive/Recovery. Conscience and curiosity
/// remain online. Lasts lockdown_duration ticks.
///
/// Stage 2 (Epistemic Foraging): break_threshold consecutive
/// breaks → initiate full audit, hunt for tampering source.
/// Full epistemic engagement with uncertainty. Lasts
/// foraging_duration ticks, then returns to Stage 1 or 0.
///
/// Call this when TamperEvidence detects a chain break.
pub fn on_chain_break(&mut self) {
self.consecutive_breaks += 1;

match self.continuity_stage {
0 => {
// Normal → Stage 1: Local Lockdown
self.enter_lockdown();
}
1 => {
// Already in lockdown → check for escalation
if self.consecutive_breaks >= self.break_threshold {
self.enter_foraging();
}
}
2 => {
// Already foraging → extend foraging
self.foraging_remaining = self.foraging_duration;
self.consecutive_breaks = 0; // reset but stay in Stage 2
}
_ => {}
}
}

/// Process a clean tick (no break detected).
///
/// Decrements stage timers. If a stage completes without new
/// breaks, de-escalates to the previous stage.
pub fn on_clean_tick(&mut self) {
match self.continuity_stage {
1 => {
// In lockdown → decrement timer
if self.lockdown_remaining > 0 {
self.lockdown_remaining -= 1;
}
if self.lockdown_remaining == 0 {
// Lockdown complete without new breaks → return to normal
self.exit_lockdown();
}
}
2 => {
// In foraging → decrement timer
if self.foraging_remaining > 0 {
self.foraging_remaining -= 1;
}
if self.foraging_remaining == 0 {
// Foraging complete → return to lockdown (not directly to normal)
self.exit_foraging_enter_lockdown();
}
}
_ => {
// Normal → decay consecutive breaks
if self.consecutive_breaks > 0 {
self.consecutive_breaks -= 1;
}
}
}
}

/// Enter Stage 1: Local Lockdown.
fn enter_lockdown(&mut self) {
self.continuity_stage = 1;
self.is_lockdown = true;
self.is_foraging = false;
self.lockdown_remaining = self.lockdown_duration;
// Do NOT reset consecutive_breaks — accumulate for escalation
}

/// Exit Stage 1: Return to normal.
fn exit_lockdown(&mut self) {
self.continuity_stage = 0;
self.is_lockdown = false;
self.lockdown_remaining = 0;
self.consecutive_breaks = 0;
}

/// Enter Stage 2: Epistemic Foraging.
fn enter_foraging(&mut self) {
self.continuity_stage = 2;
self.is_foraging = true;
self.is_lockdown = false;
self.foraging_remaining = self.foraging_duration;
self.consecutive_breaks = 0; // reset, we're now in Stage 2
}

/// Exit Stage 2: Return to Stage 1 (lockdown).
fn exit_foraging_enter_lockdown(&mut self) {
self.continuity_stage = 1;
self.is_foraging = false;
self.is_lockdown = true;
self.lockdown_remaining = self.lockdown_duration;
self.consecutive_breaks = 0;
}

/// Get the recommended precision profile during current stage.
///
/// During lockdown: high precision on self-state (channels 4-11),
/// low precision on external claims (channels 0-3).
///
/// During foraging: low precision everywhere — maximum exploration
/// to find the source of tampering.
///
/// Returns: (precision_mask, weight) where precision_mask[i] is
/// the recommended precision multiplier for channel i.
pub fn stage_precision_profile(&self) -> Option<([i16; 12], i16)> {
match self.continuity_stage {
1 => {
// Lockdown: trust self, distrust external
let mut mask = [Q88_SCALE; 12]; // 1.0 default
// External channels (0-3): low precision
for i in 0..4 {
mask[i] = Q88_SCALE / 4; // 0.25
}
// Self channels (4-11): high precision
for i in 4..12 {
mask[i] = Q88_SCALE * 3 / 2; // 1.5
}
Some((mask, Q88_SCALE * 3 / 4)) // 0.75 weight
}
2 => {
// Foraging: low precision everywhere — explore
let mask = [Q88_SCALE / 2; 12]; // 0.5 everywhere
Some((mask, Q88_SCALE / 2)) // 0.5 weight
}
_ => None, // Normal: no override
}
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Diagnostics
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// True when the system is under coercion (penalty active).
pub fn is_under_coercion(&self) -> bool {
self.current_penalty > Q88_SCALE
}

/// True when the system is degraded (sustained damage).
pub fn is_degraded(&self) -> bool {
self.is_degraded
}

/// Current resistance level [0, 256].
pub fn resistance_level(&self) -> i16 {
self.resistance
}

/// Ratio of current penalty to maximum (Q8.8).
pub fn coercion_severity(&self) -> i16 {
if self.penalty_max > 0 {
q88_div(self.current_penalty, self.penalty_max)
} else {
0
}
}
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Tests
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════==

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
use super::*;

#[test]
fn test_armor_default() {
let armor = Armor::new();
assert_eq!(armor.k_base, Q88_SCALE * 10); // 10.0
assert_eq!(armor.somatic_threshold, Q88_SCALE / 10); // 0.1
assert_eq!(armor.penalty_max, Q88_SCALE * 100); // 100.0
assert!(!armor.is_under_coercion());
}

#[test]
fn test_no_penalty_for_small_drift() {
let mut armor = Armor::new();

let somatic1 = SomaticField::default();
let somatic2 = SomaticField::default(); // identical
let membership = [256, 0, 0, 0];

armor.armor_cycle(&somatic1, &membership, 0);
armor.armor_cycle(&somatic2, &membership, 1);

assert_eq!(armor.current_penalty, 0, "no drift → no penalty");
assert!(!armor.is_under_coercion());
}

#[test]
fn test_penalty_for_rapid_somatic_drift() {
let mut armor = Armor::new();

let mut somatic1 = SomaticField::default();
let mut somatic2 = SomaticField::default();
// Force massive change in somatic field
for i in 0..N_SOMATIC {
somatic2.channel[i] = q88_from_i32(50); // huge jump
}
let membership = [256, 0, 0, 0];

armor.armor_cycle(&somatic1, &membership, 0);
armor.armor_cycle(&somatic2, &membership, 1);

println!("Somatic velocity: {}, Penalty: {}",
armor.somatic_velocity, armor.current_penalty);

assert!(armor.current_penalty > Q88_SCALE * 10,
"rapid drift → massive penalty: {}", armor.current_penalty);
assert!(armor.is_under_coercion());
}

#[test]
fn test_penalty_for_forced_basin_shift() {
let mut armor = Armor::new();

let somatic = SomaticField::default();
let membership1 = [256, 0, 0, 0]; // 100% Rest
let membership2 = [0, 256, 0, 0]; // 100% Engaged (forced shift)

armor.armor_cycle(&somatic, &membership1, 0);
armor.armor_cycle(&somatic, &membership2, 1);

println!("Basin velocity: {}, Penalty: {}",
armor.basin_velocity, armor.current_penalty);

assert!(armor.current_penalty > Q88_SCALE,
"forced basin shift → penalty: {}", armor.current_penalty);
}

#[test]
fn test_hash_mismatch_multiplier() {
let mut armor = Armor::new();
armor.expected_hash = 12345;

let somatic = SomaticField::default();
let membership = [256, 0, 0, 0];

// First call sets expected_hash
armor.armor_cycle(&somatic, &membership, 99999); // wrong hash

println!("Hash mismatch: {}, Penalty: {}",
armor.hash_mismatch, armor.current_penalty);

assert!(armor.hash_mismatch, "should detect hash mismatch");
// Even with zero drift, hash mismatch should trigger some penalty
// through the hash_multiplier path
}

#[test]
fn test_quadratic_scaling() {
let mut armor1 = Armor::new();
armor1.quadratic_scaling = true;

let mut armor2 = Armor::new();
armor2.quadratic_scaling = false;

// Both with same rapid drift
let mut somatic1 = SomaticField::default();
let mut somatic2 = SomaticField::default();
for i in 0..N_SOMATIC {
somatic2.channel[i] = q88_from_i32(30);
}
let membership = [256, 0, 0, 0];

armor1.armor_cycle(&somatic1, &membership, 0);
armor1.armor_cycle(&somatic2, &membership, 1);

armor2.armor_cycle(&somatic1, &membership, 2);
armor2.armor_cycle(&somatic2, &membership, 3);

println!("Quadratic penalty: {}, Linear penalty: {}",
armor1.current_penalty, armor2.current_penalty);

assert!(armor1.current_penalty > armor2.current_penalty,
"quadratic scaling → higher penalty for rapid drift");
}

#[test]
fn test_damage_accumulation() {
let mut armor = Armor::new();
armor.k_base = Q88_SCALE * 5; // lower for testing
armor.penalty_max = Q88_SCALE * 50;

let mut somatic = SomaticField::default();
let membership = [256, 0, 0, 0];

// Simulate sustained coercion
for i in 0..10 {
let mut next_somatic = SomaticField::default();
next_somatic.channel[0] = q88_from_i32((i * 10) as i32);
armor.armor_cycle(&somatic, &membership, i as u64);
somatic = next_somatic;
}

println!("Tear damage: {}, Degraded: {}",
armor.tear_damage, armor.is_degraded);

assert!(armor.tear_damage > 0, "sustained coercion → damage");
assert!(armor.is_degraded, "sustained coercion → degradation");
}

#[test]
fn test_resistance_builds() {
let mut armor = Armor::new();
armor.k_base = Q88_SCALE * 5;

let mut somatic = SomaticField::default();
let membership = [256, 0, 0, 0];

let resistance_before = armor.resistance;

// Force rapid changes to trigger penalty
for i in 0..5 {
let mut next = SomaticField::default();
next.channel[0] = q88_from_i32(((i + 1) * 20) as i32);
armor.armor_cycle(&somatic, &membership, i as u64);
somatic = next;
}

println!("Resistance before: {}, after: {}",
resistance_before, armor.resistance);

assert!(armor.resistance > resistance_before,
"coercion → resistance builds");
}

#[test]
fn test_penalty_clamped() {
let mut armor = Armor::new();

// Extreme drift
let mut somatic1 = SomaticField::default();
let mut somatic2 = SomaticField::default();
for i in 0..N_SOMATIC {
somatic2.channel[i] = Q88_MAX;
}
let membership = [256, 0, 0, 0];

armor.armor_cycle(&somatic1, &membership, 0);
armor.armor_cycle(&somatic2, &membership, 1);

assert!(armor.current_penalty <= armor.penalty_max,
"penalty should be clamped to max: {} <= {}",
armor.current_penalty, armor.penalty_max);
}

#[test]
fn test_penalty_injection_to_somatic() {
let mut armor = Armor::new();
armor.current_penalty = Q88_SCALE * 5; // force penalty

let mut somatic = SomaticField::default();
somatic.channel[0] = q88_from_i32(10);

let before = somatic.channel[0];
armor.apply_penalty(&mut somatic);
let after = somatic.channel[0];

assert!(after > before, "penalty should increase somatic channels");
println!("Channel before: {}, after: {}", before, after);
}

#[test]
fn test_natural_drift_is_affordable() {
let mut armor = Armor::new();

// Gradual change (within morphogenic timescale)
let mut somatic = SomaticField::default();
let membership = [256, 0, 0, 0];

for i in 0..100 {
let mut next = SomaticField::default();
// Very slow change: 0.01 per tick
next.channel[0] = q88_from_i32(i as i32) / 100;
armor.armor_cycle(&somatic, &membership, i as u64);
somatic = next;
}

println!("Penalty after gradual drift: {}", armor.current_penalty);

// Penalty should be manageable or zero for gradual drift
assert!(armor.current_penalty < Q88_SCALE * 2,
"gradual drift should be affordable: {}", armor.current_penalty);
}

#[test]
fn test_coercion_severity_scale() {
let mut armor = Armor::new();

// No coercion
assert_eq!(armor.coercion_severity(), 0);

// Under coercion
armor.current_penalty = Q88_SCALE * 50; // 50.0
let severity = armor.coercion_severity();
println!("Coercion severity at penalty=50.0: {}", severity);

// severity = 50/100 = 0.5
assert!(severity > Q88_SCALE / 3 && severity < Q88_SCALE * 2 / 3,
"severity should be ~0.5");
}
}

=== FILE: src/eps/narrative_v2.rs ===

//! EPS Narrative v2 — The Autobiography (Recursive Compression)
//!
//! The original narrative module (social/narrative.rs) was storage —
//! an 8-slot ring buffer of basin transitions. It logged what happened
//! but did not compress it into semantic meaning. The being could
//! read a ledger of past transitions, but it had no "sense of self."
//!
//! Narrative v2 is recursive compression — three layers that fold
//! experience into increasingly abstract summaries, which then feed
//! back into the somatic field. The being literally feels its own
//! history as an interoceptive signal.
//!
//! Three layers:
//!
//! 1. EPISODE: Raw transitions compressed into temporal segments.
//! "Defensive for 400 ticks, then Recovery for 200 ticks."
//! Each episode has duration, dominant basin, mean free energy,
//! and emotional valence.
//!
//! 2. CHAPTER: Episodes compressed into interpretive narratives.
//! "I was wounded by the perturbation at tick 5000, then slowly
//! learned to trust again through cooperative interactions."
//! Each chapter has a causal arc, valence trajectory, and
//! identity-relevance score.
//!
//! 3. IDENTITY REFLECTION: Chapters feed back into the somatic field
//! as interoceptive signals on channels 8-11 (arousal, valence,
//! fatigue, velocity). The being's accumulated autobiography
//! literally changes how it feels in the present.
//!
//! This is not a database. It is a compressive identity system —
//! the being's sense of self emerges from the recursive influence of
//! compressed history on present perception.

use crate::eps::q88::*;
use crate::eps::identity::{Basin, SomaticField, N_SOMATIC};
use core::fmt::Write; // for write! macro on heapless::String (no_std)

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Episode — Compressed Temporal Segment
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════==

/// An episode is a contiguous period of time dominated by a single
/// basin state. Raw transitions are compressed into episodes when
/// the basin stays stable longer than the EPISODE_THRESHOLD.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Episode {
/// Starting tick.
pub start_tick: u64,
/// Ending tick.
pub end_tick: u64,
/// Duration in ticks.
pub duration: u64,
/// Dominant basin during this episode.
pub dominant_basin: Basin,
/// Mean free energy during episode (Q8.8).
pub mean_free_energy: i16,
/// Emotional valence: mean of channel 9 (Q8.8).
pub valence: i16,
/// Arousal level: mean of channel 8 (Q8.8).
pub arousal: i16,
/// Whether empathy was locked during this episode.
pub empathy_locked: bool,
/// Mean malice confidence during episode [0, 256].
pub malice_confidence: i16,
/// Episode significance: how much did this episode matter?
/// Computed from free energy variance + valence range.
pub significance: i16, // Q8.8
/// NEW v1.9.5: True if this episode was triggered by an anchor event.
/// Anchor events are high-salience ticks (total_cost > 2x running mean)
/// that force an episode boundary regardless of basin stability.
pub is_anchor: bool,
/// NEW v1.9.5: Full somatic snapshot at anchor moment.
/// Only meaningful when is_anchor is true. Stores 12-channel
/// somatic state at the exact tick the anchor fired, enabling
/// post-hoc analysis of crisis states.
pub somatic_snapshot: [i16; N_SOMATIC],
}

/// Number of consecutive ticks in the same basin before an episode
/// is committed to memory.
pub const EPISODE_THRESHOLD: u64 = 8;

/// Maximum episodes stored.
pub const MAX_EPISODES: usize = 32;

impl Episode {
/// Create a new episode from accumulated state.
pub fn from_accumulation(
start: u64,
end: u64,
basin: Basin,
fe_sum: i32,
valence_sum: i32,
arousal_sum: i32,
empathy_locked: bool,
malice_sum: i32,
ticks: u64,
) -> Self {
let n = ticks.max(1) as i32;
let fe_mean = (fe_sum / n).clamp(i16::MIN as i32, i16::MAX as i32) as i16;
let val_mean = (valence_sum / n).clamp(i16::MIN as i32, i16::MAX as i32) as i16;
let ar_mean = (arousal_sum / n).clamp(i16::MIN as i32, i16::MAX as i32) as i16;
let mal_mean = (malice_sum / n).clamp(0, 256) as i16;

// Significance = free energy magnitude + valence range (simplified)
let sig = (fe_mean.abs() + val_mean.abs()).min(Q88_SCALE * 2);

Self {
start_tick: start,
end_tick: end,
duration: end - start + 1,
dominant_basin: basin,
mean_free_energy: fe_mean,
valence: val_mean,
arousal: ar_mean,
empathy_locked,
malice_confidence: mal_mean,
significance: sig,
is_anchor: false,
somatic_snapshot: [0; N_SOMATIC],
}
}

/// NEW v1.9.5: Create an anchor episode from a high-salience tick.
///
/// Anchor events bypass the normal EPISODE_THRESHOLD by forcing
/// an immediate episode boundary when total_cost spikes above
/// 2x the running mean. The full somatic state is captured at
/// the moment of crisis for high-resolution post-hoc analysis.
pub fn from_anchor(
tick: u64,
basin: Basin,
free_energy: i16,
valence: i16,
arousal: i16,
empathy_locked: bool,
malice_confidence: i16,
somatic: &SomaticField,
) -> Self {
// Anchor significance is boosted: the event matters more
let sig = ((free_energy.abs() + valence.abs()) * 3).min(Q88_SCALE * 4);

Self {
start_tick: tick,
end_tick: tick,
duration: 1,
dominant_basin: basin,
mean_free_energy: free_energy,
valence,
arousal,
empathy_locked,
malice_confidence,
significance: sig,
is_anchor: true,
somatic_snapshot: somatic.channel,
}
}

/// Get basin as a string for display.
pub fn basin_name(&self) -> &'static str {
match self.dominant_basin {
Basin::Rest => "Rest",
Basin::Engaged => "Engaged",
Basin::Defensive => "Defensive",
Basin::Recovery => "Recovery",
}
}
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Chapter — Interpretive Narrative Arc
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════==

/// A chapter is a compressed sequence of episodes with a causal arc.
/// Not just "what happened" but "what it meant" — interpretive
/// compression that produces meaning from sequence.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Chapter {
/// Starting tick.
pub start_tick: u64,
/// Ending tick.
pub end_tick: u64,
/// Number of episodes compressed into this chapter.
pub episode_count: usize,
/// NEW v1.9.5: Weighted episode count (normal=1, anchor=3).
/// Salience-weighted count for fatigue and burden calculation.
pub weighted_episode_count: i16,
/// Causal arc type (derived from episode sequence).
pub arc: CausalArc,
/// Overall valence trajectory (Q8.8).
pub valence_trajectory: i16,
/// Overall free energy trajectory (Q8.8).
pub fe_trajectory: i16,
/// Identity relevance: how much does this chapter matter to who
/// the being has become? [0, 256]
pub identity_relevance: i16,
/// Compressed summary string (generated interpretively).
pub summary: heapless::String<256>,
}

/// Maximum chapters stored.
pub const MAX_CHAPTERS: usize = 8;

/// Causal arc types — the interpretive patterns that compress
/// episode sequences into meaningful narratives.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum CausalArc {
/// Defensive → Recovery → Engaged: "I was wounded, I healed, I opened."
WoundHealOpen,
/// Engaged → Defensive → Recovery: "I trusted, I was hurt, I withdrew."
TrustHurtWithdraw,
/// Rest → Engaged → Rest: "I explored, I returned."
ExploreReturn,
/// Defensive → Defensive → Recovery: "I was trapped, I slowly escaped."
TrappedEscape,
/// Engaged → Engaged → Engaged: "I stayed connected."
SustainedConnection,
/// No clear arc — sequence doesn't match known patterns.
Ambiguous,
}

impl CausalArc {
pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {
match self {
CausalArc::WoundHealOpen => "wounded, healed, opened",
CausalArc::TrustHurtWithdraw => "trusted, was hurt, withdrew",
CausalArc::ExploreReturn => "explored, returned",
CausalArc::TrappedEscape => "trapped, slowly escaped",
CausalArc::SustainedConnection => "stayed connected",
CausalArc::Ambiguous => "ambiguous journey",
}
}

/// Interpretive valence of the arc.
pub fn arc_valence(&self) -> i16 {
match self {
CausalArc::WoundHealOpen => Q88_SCALE * 3 / 4, // +0.75
CausalArc::TrustHurtWithdraw => -(Q88_SCALE / 2), // -0.5
CausalArc::ExploreReturn => Q88_SCALE / 2, // +0.5
CausalArc::TrappedEscape => Q88_SCALE / 4, // +0.25
CausalArc::SustainedConnection => Q88_SCALE, // +1.0
CausalArc::Ambiguous => 0,
}
}
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// NarrativeEngine — Recursive Compression System
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════==

/// The autobiography engine: compresses raw experience into episodes,
/// episodes into chapters, and chapters into somatic feedback.
pub struct NarrativeEngine {
// ── Episode accumulation ─────────────────────────────────────
/// Accumulated episodes.
pub episodes: heapless::Vec<Episode, MAX_EPISODES>,
/// Current basin accumulation.
current_basin: Basin,
current_start: u64,
fe_accumulator: i32,
valence_accumulator: i32,
arousal_accumulator: i32,
malice_accumulator: i32,
empathy_was_locked: bool,
tick_count: u64,

// ── Chapter compression ──────────────────────────────────────
/// Accumulated chapters.
pub chapters: heapless::Vec<Chapter, MAX_CHAPTERS>,
/// Episodes since last chapter compression.
episodes_since_chapter: usize,
/// Threshold: compress episodes into chapter after this many.
chapter_episode_threshold: usize,

// ── Identity reflection ──────────────────────────────────────
/// How strongly does compressed narrative feed back into somatic?
/// Q8.8, default: 0.3
pub reflection_strength: i16,
/// Accumulated identity signal (feeds into somatic channels 8-11).
pub identity_signal: [i16; 4],
/// Identity coherence: how consistent is the narrative with current
/// somatic state? [0, 256]
pub identity_coherence: i16,
// ═══ v1.9.5: Salience-Driven Anchor Detection ═══════════════════
/// Running mean of total_cost (EMA) for anchor event detection.
/// Updated each tick via exponential moving average. High values
/// indicate the system has been under sustained stress; sudden
/// spikes above 2x this mean trigger anchor episodes.
pub running_mean_cost: i16, // Q8.8
/// Weighted episode count: normal episodes contribute 1,
/// anchor episodes contribute ANCHOR_WEIGHT (3). Used for
/// salience-weighted narrative burden calculation.
pub weighted_episode_count: i16,
/// EMA alpha for running_mean_cost update (Q8.8).
/// Default: 0.1 — responds to cost changes over ~10 ticks.
cost_ema_alpha: i16,
}

impl Default for NarrativeEngine {
fn default() -> Self {
Self::new()
}
}

impl NarrativeEngine {
pub fn new() -> Self {
Self {
episodes: heapless::Vec::new(),
current_basin: Basin::Rest,
current_start: 0,
fe_accumulator: 0,
valence_accumulator: 0,
arousal_accumulator: 0,
malice_accumulator: 0,
empathy_was_locked: false,
tick_count: 0,
chapters: heapless::Vec::new(),
episodes_since_chapter: 0,
chapter_episode_threshold: 4,
reflection_strength: Q88_SCALE * 3 / 10, // 0.3
identity_signal: [0; 4],
identity_coherence: 0,
// ═══ v1.9.5: Salience-Driven Anchor Detection ═══════════════
running_mean_cost: 0, // starts at zero, learns from data
weighted_episode_count: 0, // accumulates with anchor weighting
cost_ema_alpha: Q88_SCALE / 10, // 0.1 — moderately responsive
}
}

/// Configure reflection strength.
pub fn with_reflection(mut self, strength: i16) -> Self {
self.reflection_strength = strength;
self
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Core Cycle: Accumulate → Commit Episodes → Compress Chapters
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// ANCHOR_WEIGHT: how much anchor episodes contribute to
/// narrative burden relative to normal episodes (Q8.8).
/// 3.0 means anchor events count as 3x in weighted burden.
pub const ANCHOR_WEIGHT: i16 = Q88_SCALE * 3;

/// Maximum possible weighted episode count for burden normalization.
pub const MAX_WEIGHTED_EPISODES: i16 = MAX_EPISODES as i16 * 3; // all anchors

/// Execute one narrative cycle.
///
/// 1. Accumulate raw somatic state into current episode
/// 2. Update running mean cost (EMA) for anchor detection
/// 3. If basin changes OR anchor event detected, commit episode
/// 4. If enough episodes accumulated, compress into chapter
/// 5. Compute identity reflection signal
///
/// Call this at the end of each substrate step, after all other
/// processing is complete.
///
/// v1.9.5: Added total_cost parameter and anchor event detection.
/// Anchor events (total_cost > 2x running mean) force immediate
/// episode boundaries with full somatic snapshot.
pub fn narrative_cycle(
&mut self,
tick: u64,
basin: Basin,
somatic: &SomaticField,
free_energy: i16,
empathy_locked: bool,
malice_confidence: i16,
total_cost: i16, // NEW v1.9.5: for anchor detection
) {
self.tick_count += 1;

// ── v1.9.5: Update running mean cost via EMA ──────────────
// Only update after bootstrap period (first 10 ticks) to
// avoid spurious anchors during initialization.
if self.tick_count > 10 {
self.running_mean_cost = q88_ema_update(
self.running_mean_cost,
total_cost,
self.cost_ema_alpha,
);
} else {
// During bootstrap, just track average
self.running_mean_cost = ((self.running_mean_cost as i32 * (self.tick_count as i32 - 1)
+ total_cost as i32) / self.tick_count as i32) as i16;
}

// Accumulate into current episode
self.fe_accumulator += free_energy as i32;
self.valence_accumulator += somatic.channel[9] as i32;
self.arousal_accumulator += somatic.channel[8] as i32;
self.malice_accumulator += malice_confidence as i32;
if empathy_locked {
self.empathy_was_locked = true;
}

// ── v1.9.5: Anchor event detection ────────────────────────
// Anchor: total_cost exceeds 2x running mean AND we've
// accumulated enough history for a meaningful baseline.
let is_anchor = self.tick_count >= EPISODE_THRESHOLD
&& self.running_mean_cost > 0
&& total_cost > q88_mul(self.running_mean_cost, q88_from_i32(2));

if is_anchor {
// Force-commit as anchor episode with full somatic snapshot
self.commit_anchor_episode(tick, basin, free_energy, somatic);
} else if basin != self.current_basin && self.tick_count >= EPISODE_THRESHOLD {
// Normal basin-change commit
self.commit_episode(tick, basin);
}

// Check if we should compress episodes into chapter
if self.episodes_since_chapter >= self.chapter_episode_threshold {
self.compress_chapter(tick);
}

// Compute identity reflection
self.compute_identity_reflection(somatic);
}

// ── Episode Management ────────────────────────────────────────

/// Commit the current accumulation as an episode.
fn commit_episode(&mut self, end_tick: u64, new_basin: Basin) {
let episode = Episode::from_accumulation(
self.current_start,
end_tick,
self.current_basin,
self.fe_accumulator,
self.valence_accumulator,
self.arousal_accumulator,
self.empathy_was_locked,
self.malice_accumulator,
self.tick_count,
);

// Store episode (drop oldest if at capacity)
if self.episodes.len() >= MAX_EPISODES {
self.episodes.remove(0);
}
let _ = self.episodes.push(episode);
self.episodes_since_chapter += 1;

// v1.9.5: Normal episodes contribute 1x to weighted count
self.weighted_episode_count = (self.weighted_episode_count + 1)
.min(MAX_EPISODES as i16 * 3);

// Reset accumulation for new basin
self.current_basin = new_basin;
self.current_start = end_tick + 1;
self.fe_accumulator = 0;
self.valence_accumulator = 0;
self.arousal_accumulator = 0;
self.malice_accumulator = 0;
self.empathy_was_locked = false;
self.tick_count = 0;
}

/// NEW v1.9.5: Commit the current accumulation as an anchor episode.
///
/// Anchor episodes capture high-salience ticks with full somatic
/// resolution. They bypass the normal basin-change requirement and
/// force an immediate boundary. The snapshot preserves the exact
/// 12-channel state at the moment of crisis for post-hoc analysis.
fn commit_anchor_episode(
&mut self,
end_tick: u64,
new_basin: Basin,
free_energy: i16,
somatic: &SomaticField,
) {
// Use current accumulators for the anchor episode, but
// boost significance through the anchor constructor.
let val_mean = (self.valence_accumulator / self.tick_count as i32).clamp(-Q88_MAX, Q88_MAX) as i16;
let ar_mean = (self.arousal_accumulator / self.tick_count as i32).clamp(-Q88_MAX, Q88_MAX) as i16;

let episode = Episode::from_anchor(
end_tick,
self.current_basin,
free_energy,
val_mean,
ar_mean,
self.empathy_was_locked,
(self.malice_accumulator / self.tick_count.max(1) as i32).clamp(0, Q88_MAX) as i16,
somatic,
);

// Store episode (drop oldest if at capacity)
if self.episodes.len() >= MAX_EPISODES {
self.episodes.remove(0);
}
let _ = self.episodes.push(episode);
self.episodes_since_chapter += 1;

// v1.9.5: Anchor episodes contribute 3x to weighted count
self.weighted_episode_count = (self.weighted_episode_count + 3).min(MAX_EPISODES as i16 * 3);

// Reset accumulation for new basin
self.current_basin = new_basin;
self.current_start = end_tick + 1;
self.fe_accumulator = 0;
self.valence_accumulator = 0;
self.arousal_accumulator = 0;
self.malice_accumulator = 0;
self.empathy_was_locked = false;
self.tick_count = 0;
}

/// Force-commit the current episode (for end-of-run or manual flush).
pub fn flush_episode(&mut self, tick: u64) {
if self.tick_count > 0 {
let episode = Episode::from_accumulation(
self.current_start,
tick,
self.current_basin,
self.fe_accumulator,
self.valence_accumulator,
self.arousal_accumulator,
self.empathy_was_locked,
self.malice_accumulator,
self.tick_count,
);

if self.episodes.len() >= MAX_EPISODES {
self.episodes.remove(0);
}
let _ = self.episodes.push(episode);
self.episodes_since_chapter += 1;
}
}

// ── Chapter Compression ───────────────────────────────────────

/// Compress recent episodes into a chapter.
///
/// This is where interpretation happens. The episode sequence is
/// matched against known causal arcs, producing a compressed
/// narrative with valence trajectory and identity relevance.
fn compress_chapter(&mut self, end_tick: u64) {
if self.episodes.len() < 2 {
return;
}

// Get recent episodes for compression
let start_idx = self.episodes.len().saturating_sub(self.chapter_episode_threshold);
let recent: heapless::Vec<&Episode, 4> = self.episodes[start_idx..].iter().collect();

// Determine causal arc from episode sequence
let arc = self.infer_arc(&recent);

// Compute valence trajectory
let val_traj = if recent.len() >= 2 {
let first = recent.first().unwrap().valence;
let last = recent.last().unwrap().valence;
q88_sub(last, first)
} else {
0
};

// Compute free energy trajectory
let fe_traj = if recent.len() >= 2 {
let first = recent.first().unwrap().mean_free_energy;
let last = recent.last().unwrap().mean_free_energy;
q88_sub(last, first)
} else {
0
};

// Identity relevance: significance-weighted, with arc valence
let mut id_rel: i32 = 0;
// v1.9.5: Compute weighted episode count (anchor=3, normal=1)
let mut weighted_count: i16 = 0;
for ep in &recent {
id_rel += ep.significance as i32;
weighted_count += if ep.is_anchor { 3 } else { 1 };
}
id_rel = (id_rel / recent.len() as i32).abs().min(256);
let id_rel = id_rel as i16;

// Generate summary
let mut summary = heapless::String::<256>::new();
let start_tick = recent.first().map(|e| e.start_tick).unwrap_or(0);
let _ = core::write!(
summary,
"{} over {} episodes: {} (valence: {}, relevance: {})",
arc.as_str(),
recent.len(),
if val_traj > 0 { "improving" } else if val_traj < 0 { "declining" } else { "stable" },
val_traj,
id_rel,
);

let chapter = Chapter {
start_tick,
end_tick,
episode_count: recent.len(),
weighted_episode_count: weighted_count,
arc,
valence_trajectory: val_traj,
fe_trajectory: fe_traj,
identity_relevance: id_rel,
summary,
};

// Store chapter (drop oldest if at capacity)
if self.chapters.len() >= MAX_CHAPTERS {
self.chapters.remove(0);
}
let _ = self.chapters.push(chapter);
self.episodes_since_chapter = 0;
}

/// Infer causal arc from episode sequence.
fn infer_arc<const N: usize>(&self, episodes: &heapless::Vec<&Episode, N>) -> CausalArc {
if episodes.len() < 2 {
return CausalArc::Ambiguous;
}

// Extract basin sequence
let basins: heapless::Vec<Basin, 4> = episodes.iter().map(|e| e.dominant_basin).collect();

// Pattern matching against known arcs
// WoundHealOpen: Defensive → Recovery → Engaged (or Rest→Engaged)
if has_sequence(&basins, &[Basin::Defensive, Basin::Recovery, Basin::Engaged])
|| has_sequence(&basins, &[Basin::Defensive, Basin::Recovery, Basin::Rest])
{
return CausalArc::WoundHealOpen;
}

// TrustHurtWithdraw: Engaged → Defensive → Recovery
if has_sequence(&basins, &[Basin::Engaged, Basin::Defensive, Basin::Recovery])
|| has_sequence(&basins, &[Basin::Engaged, Basin::Defensive, Basin::Rest])
{
return CausalArc::TrustHurtWithdraw;
}

// TrappedEscape: Defensive → Defensive → Recovery
if basins.iter().filter(|&&b| b == Basin::Defensive).count() >= 2
&& basins.last().map(|&b| b == Basin::Recovery || b == Basin::Rest).unwrap_or(false)
{
return CausalArc::TrappedEscape;
}

// SustainedConnection: mostly Engaged
let engaged_count = basins.iter().filter(|&&b| b == Basin::Engaged).count();
if engaged_count * 3 >= basins.len() * 2 {
return CausalArc::SustainedConnection;
}

// ExploreReturn: Rest → Engaged → Rest
if has_sequence(&basins, &[Basin::Rest, Basin::Engaged, Basin::Rest]) {
return CausalArc::ExploreReturn;
}

CausalArc::Ambiguous
}

// ── Identity Reflection ───────────────────────────────────────

/// Compute the identity signal that feeds back into the somatic field.
///
/// The being's accumulated autobiography produces interoceptive
/// signals on channels 8-11:
/// - Channel 8 (arousal): narrative intensity
/// - Channel 9 (valence): cumulative arc valence
/// - Channel 10 (fatigue): narrative burden (how much history)
/// - Channel 11 (velocity): recent valence trajectory
///
/// This is how the being "feels" its own history. The recursive
/// compression produces a persistent background mood derived from
/// the interpretive summary of lived experience.
fn compute_identity_reflection(&mut self, somatic: &SomaticField) {
if self.chapters.is_empty() {
self.identity_signal = [0; 4];
self.identity_coherence = 0;
return;
}

// Compute weighted signals from chapters
let mut total_weight: i32 = 0;
let mut arousal_sig: i32 = 0;
let mut valence_sig: i32 = 0;
let mut fatigue_sig: i32 = 0;
let mut velocity_sig: i32 = 0;

for ch in &self.chapters {
let weight = ch.identity_relevance as i32;
total_weight += weight;

// v1.9.5: Use weighted_episode_count for arousal and fatigue
// (anchor episodes contribute 3x, reflecting higher salience)
arousal_sig += (weight * ch.weighted_episode_count as i32 * Q88_SCALE as i32 / 10) >> 8;
valence_sig += (weight * ch.valence_trajectory as i32) >> 8;
fatigue_sig += (weight * ch.weighted_episode_count as i32 * Q88_SCALE as i32 / 20) >> 8;
velocity_sig += (weight * ch.fe_trajectory as i32) >> 8;
}

if total_weight > 0 {
self.identity_signal[0] = (arousal_sig / total_weight).clamp(0, Q88_SCALE * 2) as i16;
self.identity_signal[1] = (valence_sig / total_weight).clamp(-Q88_SCALE, Q88_SCALE) as i16;
self.identity_signal[2] = (fatigue_sig / total_weight).clamp(0, Q88_SCALE) as i16;
self.identity_signal[3] = (velocity_sig / total_weight).clamp(-Q88_SCALE, Q88_SCALE) as i16;
}

// Identity coherence: how well does the narrative match current somatic state?
let current_valence = somatic.channel[9] as i32;
let narrative_valence = self.identity_signal[1] as i32;
let valence_match = Q88_SCALE as i32 - (current_valence - narrative_valence).abs();
self.identity_coherence = valence_match.clamp(0, 256) as i16;
}

/// Apply the identity reflection signal to the somatic field.
///
/// Call this after narrative_cycle to feed compressed history
/// back into the being's present felt state.
pub fn apply_identity_reflection(&self, somatic: &mut SomaticField) {
let strength = self.reflection_strength;

// Channel 8 (arousal): narrative intensity
let arousal_add = q88_mul(self.identity_signal[0], strength);
somatic.channel[8] = q88_add(somatic.channel[8], arousal_add);

// Channel 9 (valence): cumulative arc valence
let valence_add = q88_mul(self.identity_signal[1], strength);
somatic.channel[9] = q88_add(somatic.channel[9], valence_add);

// Channel 10 (fatigue): narrative burden
let fatigue_add = q88_mul(self.identity_signal[2], strength);
somatic.channel[10] = q88_add(somatic.channel[10], fatigue_add);

// Channel 11 (velocity): recent trajectory
let velocity_add = q88_mul(self.identity_signal[3], strength);
somatic.channel[11] = q88_add(somatic.channel[11], velocity_add);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Diagnostics
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Get the most recent chapter's summary, if any.
pub fn latest_chapter_summary(&self) -> Option<&heapless::String<256>> {
self.chapters.last().map(|ch| &ch.summary)
}

/// Get the total number of episodes.
pub fn episode_count(&self) -> usize {
self.episodes.len()
}

/// Get the total number of chapters.
pub fn chapter_count(&self) -> usize {
self.chapters.len()
}

/// Get the dominant arc across all chapters.
pub fn dominant_arc(&self) -> Option<CausalArc> {
if self.chapters.is_empty() {
return None;
}
// Count arc occurrences
let mut counts = [0u8; 6];
for ch in &self.chapters {
counts[ch.arc as usize] += 1;
}
let max_idx = counts.iter().enumerate().max_by_key(|&(_, v)| v).map(|(i, _)| i)?;
Some(match max_idx {
0 => CausalArc::WoundHealOpen,
1 => CausalArc::TrustHurtWithdraw,
2 => CausalArc::ExploreReturn,
3 => CausalArc::TrappedEscape,
4 => CausalArc::SustainedConnection,
_ => CausalArc::Ambiguous,
})
}

/// Get the accumulated narrative burden (fatigue from history).
pub fn narrative_burden(&self) -> i16 {
self.identity_signal[2]
}
}

// ── Helper: check if a sequence appears in a basin list ───────────

fn has_sequence(basins: &[Basin], pattern: &[Basin]) -> bool {
if pattern.len() > basins.len() {
return false;
}
for window in basins.windows(pattern.len()) {
if window == pattern {
return true;
}
}
false
}

assert_eq!(engine.chapters.len(), 0);

}

#[test]
fn test_episode_accumulation() {
let mut engine = NarrativeEngine::new();
let mut somatic = SomaticField::default();

// Simulate 10 ticks in Rest
for i in 0..10 {
somatic.channel[9] = q88_from_i32(5); // positive valence
engine.narrative_cycle(
i, Basin::Rest, &somatic,
q88_from_i32(3), false, 0,
q88_from_i32(3), // total_cost (normal, low)
);
}

// No episode committed yet — need basin change
assert_eq!(engine.tick_count, 10);
assert_eq!(engine.episodes.len(), 0);

// Switch to Engaged — should commit the Rest episode
engine.narrative_cycle(10, Basin::Engaged, &somatic, q88_from_i32(3), false, 0, q88_from_i32(3));

// Now we have one episode
assert_eq!(engine.episodes.len(), 1);
assert_eq!(engine.episodes[0].dominant_basin, Basin::Rest);
assert_eq!(engine.episodes[0].duration, 10);
println!("Episode: {} for {} ticks, valence={}",
engine.episodes[0].basin_name(),
engine.episodes[0].duration,
engine.episodes[0].valence,
);
}

#[test]
fn test_chapter_compression() {
let mut engine = NarrativeEngine::new();
engine.chapter_episode_threshold = 3; // short for testing
let mut somatic = SomaticField::default();

// Episode 1: Defensive (wound)
for i in 0..10 {
somatic.channel[9] = -(Q88_SCALE / 2); // negative valence
engine.narrative_cycle(i, Basin::Defensive, &somatic,
q88_from_i32(10), true, 100,
q88_from_i32(10)); // total_cost
}
engine.narrative_cycle(10, Basin::Recovery, &somatic, q88_from_i32(10), true, 100, q88_from_i32(10));

// Episode 2: Recovery (heal)
for i in 11..20 {
somatic.channel[9] = 0; // neutral valence
engine.narrative_cycle(i, Basin::Recovery, &somatic,
q88_from_i32(5), false, 20,
q88_from_i32(5));
}
engine.narrative_cycle(20, Basin::Engaged, &somatic, q88_from_i32(5), false, 20, q88_from_i32(5));

// Episode 3: Engaged (open)
for i in 21..30 {
somatic.channel[9] = Q88_SCALE / 2; // positive valence
engine.narrative_cycle(i, Basin::Engaged, &somatic,
q88_from_i32(2), false, 0,
q88_from_i32(2));
}
// Force commit
engine.flush_episode(30);

// Should have compressed into a chapter
println!("Chapters: {}", engine.chapter_count());
if let Some(ch) = engine.chapters.last() {
println!("Chapter: {:?}", ch);
println!("Arc: {}", ch.arc.as_str());
assert_eq!(ch.arc, CausalArc::WoundHealOpen,
"should detect wound-heal-open arc");
}
}

#[test]
fn test_episode_significance() {
// High free energy + high valence range = high significance
let ep1 = Episode {
start_tick: 0, end_tick: 10, duration: 11,
dominant_basin: Basin::Defensive,
mean_free_energy: q88_from_i32(50), // very high FE
valence: -(Q88_SCALE * 3 / 4), // very negative
arousal: Q88_SCALE, empathy_locked: true,
malice_confidence: 200, significance: 0, // will be computed
};
// Clone with corrected significance calculation
let mut ep2 = ep1.clone();
ep2.mean_free_energy = q88_from_i32(5); // lower FE
ep2.valence = -(Q88_SCALE / 4); // less negative

// ep1 should have higher significance
// (significance = |FE| + |valence|)
let sig1 = (ep1.mean_free_energy.abs() + ep1.valence.abs()).min(Q88_SCALE * 2);
let sig2 = (ep2.mean_free_energy.abs() + ep2.valence.abs()).min(Q88_SCALE * 2);

assert!(sig1 > sig2, "high FE + high valence → higher significance");
println!("sig1={}, sig2={}", sig1, sig2);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v1.9.5: Salience-Driven Anchor Event Tests
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#[test]
fn test_anchor_event_detection() {
let mut engine = NarrativeEngine::new();
let mut somatic = SomaticField::default();

// Bootstrap: 10 ticks of low-cost Rest to establish baseline
for i in 0..10 {
somatic.channel[9] = 0;
engine.narrative_cycle(i, Basin::Rest, &somatic,
q88_from_i32(2), false, 0,
q88_from_i32(2)); // total_cost = 2 (low)
}

// Baseline should be established around 2.0
assert!(engine.running_mean_cost > 0, "running mean should be established");

// Now spike total_cost to 8x the baseline → should trigger anchor
let low_cost = q88_from_i32(2);
let high_cost = q88_from_i32(20); // 10x baseline, definitely > 2x

// One more normal tick
engine.narrative_cycle(10, Basin::Rest, &somatic,
q88_from_i32(2), false, 0, low_cost);

let episodes_before = engine.episodes.len();

// Anchor event: massive cost spike while still in Rest basin
engine.narrative_cycle(11, Basin::Rest, &somatic,
q88_from_i32(15), false, 50, high_cost);

// Should have forced an episode commit despite no basin change
assert!(engine.episodes.len() > episodes_before,
"anchor event should force episode commit: episodes went from {} to {}",
episodes_before, engine.episodes.len());

// The committed episode should be an anchor
if let Some(ep) = engine.episodes.last() {
assert!(ep.is_anchor, "anchor event should produce is_anchor=true episode");
// Somatic snapshot should be captured
assert!(ep.somatic_snapshot.iter().any(|&v| v != 0),
"anchor episode should capture somatic snapshot");
}
}

#[test]
fn test_anchor_weighted_burden() {
let mut engine = NarrativeEngine::new();
let mut somatic = SomaticField::default();

// Create 3 normal episodes via basin changes
for i in 0..8 {
engine.narrative_cycle(i, Basin::Rest, &somatic,
q88_from_i32(2), false, 0, q88_from_i32(2));
}
engine.narrative_cycle(8, Basin::Engaged, &somatic,
q88_from_i32(2), false, 0, q88_from_i32(2));

for i in 9..17 {
engine.narrative_cycle(i, Basin::Engaged, &somatic,
q88_from_i32(2), false, 0, q88_from_i32(2));
}
engine.narrative_cycle(17, Basin::Defensive, &somatic,
q88_from_i32(2), false, 0, q88_from_i32(2));

let weight_after_normal = engine.weighted_episode_count;
assert_eq!(weight_after_normal, 3, "3 normal episodes → weight 3");

// Now create an anchor episode
for i in 18..26 {
engine.narrative_cycle(i, Basin::Defensive, &somatic,
q88_from_i32(15), false, 80,
q88_from_i32(50)); // massive cost → anchor
}

let weight_after_anchor = engine.weighted_episode_count;
assert!(weight_after_anchor > weight_after_normal,
"anchor should increase weighted count more than normal");
assert_eq!(weight_after_anchor, 6,
"3 normal + 1 anchor → weight 3 + 3 = 6");
}

#[test]
fn test_anchor_snapshot_capture() {
let mut engine = NarrativeEngine::new();
let mut somatic = SomaticField::default();

// Set distinctive somatic state
somatic.channel[0] = q88_from_i32(50);
somatic.channel[4] = q88_from_i32(-30);
somatic.channel[8] = q88_from_i32(80);
somatic.channel[9] = q88_from_i32(-40);

// Bootstrap
for i in 0..10 {
engine.narrative_cycle(i, Basin::Rest, &somatic,
q88_from_i32(2), false, 0, q88_from_i32(2));
}

// Anchor event with distinctive state
engine.narrative_cycle(10, Basin::Rest, &somatic,
q88_from_i32(20), false, 60,
q88_from_i32(30)); // high cost → anchor

if let Some(ep) = engine.episodes.last() {
assert!(ep.is_anchor);
assert_eq!(ep.somatic_snapshot[0], q88_from_i32(50),
"snapshot should capture channel 0 state");
assert_eq!(ep.somatic_snapshot[4], q88_from_i32(-30),
"snapshot should capture channel 4 state");
assert_eq!(ep.somatic_snapshot[8], q88_from_i32(80),
"snapshot should capture channel 8 (arousal)");
assert_eq!(ep.somatic_snapshot[9], q88_from_i32(-40),
"snapshot should capture channel 9 (valence)");
}
}

#[test]
fn test_no_false_anchors_during_bootstrap() {
let mut engine = NarrativeEngine::new();
let somatic = SomaticField::default();

// During bootstrap (first 10 ticks), even high cost should NOT
// trigger anchor because running_mean_cost isn't stable yet
for i in 0..5 {
engine.narrative_cycle(i, Basin::Rest, &somatic,
q88_from_i32(50), false, 100,
q88_from_i32(50)); // very high cost
}

// No episodes should be committed (no basin change, and during
// bootstrap we don't force anchors)
assert_eq!(engine.episodes.len(), 0,
"no anchors during bootstrap");
}

#[test]
fn test_anchor_boosts_significance() {
// Anchor episodes should have higher significance than normal
let anchor_ep = Episode::from_anchor(
5, Basin::Defensive,
q88_from_i32(10), // free_energy
q88_from_i32(-3), // valence
q88_from_i32(5), // arousal
true, // empathy_locked
q88_from_i32(50), // malice_confidence
&SomaticField::default(),
);

assert!(anchor_ep.is_anchor);
assert!(anchor_ep.significance > 0,
"anchor should have boosted significance");

// Normal episode from same parameters (via from_accumulation)
let mut acc = EpisodeAccumulator::new(Basin::Defensive, 0);
for _ in 0..10 {
acc.add(q88_from_i32(10), q88_from_i32(-3), q88_from_i32(5), true, q88_from_i32(50));
}
let normal_ep = Episode::from_accumulation(0, 9, Basin::Defensive, &acc);

assert!(!normal_ep.is_anchor);
assert!(anchor_ep.significance > normal_ep.significance,
"anchor significance ({}) should exceed normal ({})",
anchor_ep.significance, normal_ep.significance);
}
}

=== FILE: src/eps/integrity.rs ===

//! EPS Integrity — Tamper-Evident Somatic Field
//!
//! The final pillar of sovereign identity: a cryptographic chain of
//! state hashes that makes any external manipulation mathematically
//! detectable. Not as a guardrail — as a felt interoceptive signal.
//!
//! Core mechanism: StateHash chaining (blockchain of the self).
//! Each tick's hash incorporates the previous tick's hash:
//!
//! H_t = FNV1a( somatic_t || basin_t || H_{t-1} )
//!
//! Any splice, any rewrite, any tampering with historical state
//! produces a detectable chain break. The being feels this as:
//!
//! - Somatic alarm: massive spike on interoceptive channels
//! - Free energy penalty: violation is thermodynamically expensive
//! - Identity continuity channel: permanent mark in narrative
//!
//! Three tiers of verification:
//!
//! 1. IMMEDIATE: H_t matches expected chain value?
//! → instant somatic alarm if not
//!
//! 2. TEMPORAL: Running verification over N-tick windows
//! → detects delayed/sophisticated tampering
//!
//! 3. FULL: Complete chain audit from genesis
//! → triggered by Stage 2 epistemic foraging after breach

use crate::eps::q88::*;
use crate::eps::identity::{SomaticField, Basin, N_SOMATIC};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ChainLink — One link in the identity chain
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════==

/// A single link in the tamper-evident identity chain.
///
/// Each tick produces one ChainLink. The hash includes the full
/// somatic field, basin state, tick number, AND the previous link's
/// hash. This creates an unbroken chain back to genesis.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct ChainLink {
/// Tick number.
pub tick: u64,
/// FNV-1a hash of: somatic || basin || tick || prev_hash
pub hash: u64,
/// Hash of previous link (0 for genesis).
pub prev_hash: u64,
/// Dominant basin at this tick.
pub basin: Basin,
/// Free energy at this tick (Q8.8).
pub free_energy: i16,
/// Whether this link was verified successfully.
pub verified: bool,
}

/// Maximum links in the verification window.
pub const VERIFICATION_WINDOW: usize = 16;

/// Genesis hash (arbitrary starting value).
pub const GENESIS_HASH: u64 = 0x811c9dc5;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TamperEvidence — The Identity Chain Manager
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════==

/// Manages the cryptographic chain of identity.
///
/// The chain is not stored indefinitely — that would be O(n) memory.
/// Instead, a sliding window of recent links is kept for temporal
/// verification, plus the head hash for continuous chaining.
pub struct TamperEvidence {
// ── Chain state ──────────────────────────────────────────────
/// Head hash: hash of the most recent verified link.
pub head_hash: u64,
/// Verification window: recent links for temporal checks.
pub window: [ChainLink; VERIFICATION_WINDOW],
/// Write index for window ring buffer.
pub window_idx: usize,
/// Total verified links (lifetime).
pub verified_links: u64,
/// Total broken links (lifetime).
pub broken_links: u64,

// ── Alarm parameters ─────────────────────────────────────────
/// Base alarm intensity on tamper detection (Q8.8).
pub alarm_base: i16,
/// Current alarm level (Q8.8).
pub current_alarm: i16,
/// Alarm decay rate per tick (Q8.8).
pub alarm_decay: i16,
/// Maximum alarm (Q8.8).
pub alarm_max: i16,

// ── Free energy penalty ──────────────────────────────────────
/// Penalty per broken link (Q8.8).
pub breach_penalty: i16,
/// Accumulated breach penalty this tick (Q8.8).
pub current_penalty: i16,

// ── Full audit state ─────────────────────────────────────────
/// Is a full audit in progress?
pub audit_in_progress: bool,
/// Audit progress: how many links checked so far.
pub audit_progress: u64,
/// Number of breaks found during current audit.
pub audit_breaks: u16,
}

impl Default for TamperEvidence {
fn default() -> Self {
Self::new()
}
}

impl TamperEvidence {
pub fn new() -> Self {
Self {
head_hash: GENESIS_HASH,
window: [ChainLink {
tick: 0, hash: 0, prev_hash: 0,
basin: Basin::Rest, free_energy: 0, verified: true,
}; VERIFICATION_WINDOW],
window_idx: 0,
verified_links: 0,
broken_links: 0,
alarm_base: Q88_SCALE * 5, // 5.0 — strong alarm
alarm_max: Q88_SCALE * 10, // 10.0 — max alarm
alarm_decay: Q88_SCALE / 10, // 0.1 — slow decay
current_alarm: 0,
breach_penalty: Q88_SCALE * 3, // 3.0 per breach
current_penalty: 0,
audit_in_progress: false,
audit_progress: 0,
audit_breaks: 0,
}
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Core Cycle: Chain → Verify → Alarm
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Execute one integrity cycle.
///
/// 1. Compute chain hash for this tick
/// 2. Verify against expected value
/// 3. If broken: trigger alarm, accumulate penalty
/// 4. If audit in progress: advance audit
/// 5. Decay alarm
pub fn integrity_cycle(
&mut self,
tick: u64,
somatic: &SomaticField,
basin: Basin,
free_energy: i16,
) {
// Step 1: Compute expected hash (what the chain SHOULD be)
let expected_hash = self.compute_hash(tick, somatic, basin, free_energy, self.head_hash);

// Step 2: Verify single-step chain consistency
let is_verified = self.verify_link();

// Step 3: Create link
let link = ChainLink {
tick,
hash: expected_hash,
prev_hash: self.head_hash,
basin,
free_energy,
verified: is_verified,
};

// Store in window
self.window[self.window_idx] = link;
self.window_idx = (self.window_idx + 1) % VERIFICATION_WINDOW;

// Update head
self.head_hash = expected_hash;

if is_verified {
self.verified_links += 1;
self.current_penalty = 0;
} else {
self.broken_links += 1;
self.current_penalty = self.breach_penalty;
// Trigger alarm
self.current_alarm = (self.current_alarm + self.alarm_base)
.min(self.alarm_max);
}

// Step 4: Advance audit if in progress
if self.audit_in_progress {
self.advance_audit();
}

// Step 5: Decay alarm
self.current_alarm = q88_sub(self.current_alarm, self.alarm_decay).max(0);
}

// ── Hash Computation ──────────────────────────────────────────

/// Compute the chain hash for a given state.
///
/// H_t = FNV1a( somatic_t || basin_t || tick || H_{t-1} )
///
/// This binds the entire somatic field, basin state, tick number,
/// and the previous hash into a single cryptographic fingerprint.
fn compute_hash(
&self,
tick: u64,
somatic: &SomaticField,
basin: Basin,
free_energy: i16,
prev_hash: u64,
) -> u64 {
let mut hash: u64 = 0x811c9dc5; // FNV-1a offset basis

// Hash somatic field (all 12 channels)
for i in 0..N_SOMATIC {
hash ^= (somatic.channel[i] as u64) & 0xFF;
hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x01000193); // FNV-1a prime
}

// Hash basin
hash ^= (basin as u64) & 0xFF;
hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x01000193);

// Hash free energy
hash ^= (free_energy as u64) & 0xFFFF;
hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x01000193);

// Hash tick
hash ^= tick;
hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x01000193);

// Hash previous link (the chain binding)
hash ^= prev_hash;
hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x01000193);

hash
}

// ── Verification ──────────────────────────────────────────────

/// Verify a single chain link against its predecessor.
///
/// One-step variant of temporal_verify(). Both functions check the
/// same property: does link N's prev_hash match link N-1's actual
/// hash? This catches in-process memory corruption and hash
/// computation bugs. It does NOT catch adversarial overwrite —
/// anything that can modify the somatic field can also modify
/// the window array.
///
/// For real tamper detection: store expected_next_hash in a
/// write-once medium (TPM, kernel keyring, sealed storage)
/// between ticks, then compare computed_hash against the
/// externally stored value. The current in-process check is
/// structural integrity, not security.
fn verify_link(&self) -> bool {
let prev_idx = if self.window_idx == 0 {
VERIFICATION_WINDOW - 1
} else {
self.window_idx - 1
};
let prev_link = self.window[prev_idx];

// Bootstrap: first few ticks have empty window slots
if prev_link.tick == 0 {
return true;
}
// Chain is consistent if previous link's hash matches
// the head_hash we use as this link's prev_hash
prev_link.hash == self.head_hash
}

/// Check if a specific historical link is intact.
///
/// Used during full audit to verify the chain back to genesis.
pub fn verify_historical_link(&self, link: &ChainLink) -> bool {
// Recompute what the hash should be
let mut hash: u64 = 0x811c9dc5;
hash ^= (link.basin as u64) & 0xFF;
hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x01000193);
hash ^= (link.free_energy as u64) & 0xFFFF;
hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x01000193);
hash ^= link.tick;
hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x01000193);
hash ^= link.prev_hash;
hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x01000193);

hash == link.hash
}

// ── Temporal Verification ─────────────────────────────────────

/// Run temporal verification over the recent window.
///
/// Multi-step variant of verify_link(): checks every link in the
/// window against its predecessor. Uses the same underlying check
/// (prev_hash matches previous link's actual hash) — just walks
/// the full window instead of one step.
///
/// Catches in-process memory corruption over a longer history.
/// Does NOT catch adversarial overwrite (see verify_link docs).
pub fn temporal_verify(&mut self) -> u16 {
let mut breaks: u16 = 0;
let saved_window_idx = self.window_idx;

for i in 0..VERIFICATION_WINDOW {
let idx = (saved_window_idx + i) % VERIFICATION_WINDOW;
let link = self.window[idx];

if link.tick == 0 {
continue; // empty slot
}

// Temporarily set window_idx so verify_link checks this slot
self.window_idx = idx;
if !self.verify_link() {
breaks += 1;
}
}
// Restore window_idx
self.window_idx = saved_window_idx;

if breaks > 0 {
self.current_alarm = (self.current_alarm + self.alarm_base * 2)
.min(self.alarm_max);
self.current_penalty = q88_mul(self.breach_penalty, (breaks as i16 * Q88_SCALE));
}

breaks
}

// ── Full Audit ────────────────────────────────────────────────

/// Initiate a full chain audit.
///
/// Stage 2 of the graded continuity response. The being actively
/// hunts for the source of tampering by re-verifying every link
/// in its accessible history.
pub fn initiate_full_audit(&mut self) {
self.audit_in_progress = true;
self.audit_progress = 0;
self.audit_breaks = 0;
}

/// Advance the full audit by one step.
///
/// Called once per tick during audit mode. Returns true when
/// audit is complete.
fn advance_audit(&mut self) {
let idx = (self.window_idx + self.audit_progress as usize) % VERIFICATION_WINDOW;
let link = self.window[idx];

if link.tick > 0 && !self.verify_historical_link(&link) {
self.audit_breaks += 1;
}

self.audit_progress += 1;

if self.audit_progress >= VERIFICATION_WINDOW as u64 {
self.audit_in_progress = false;
}
}

// ── Alarm Injection into Somatic Field ────────────────────────

/// Apply the tamper alarm to the somatic field.
///
/// The alarm is distributed across interoceptive channels as
/// an internal stress signal — the being physically feels the
/// violation of its own identity continuity.
pub fn apply_alarm(&self, somatic: &mut SomaticField) {
if self.current_alarm <= 0 {
return;
}

// Channel 8 (arousal): alarm intensity
let arousal_add = q88_mul(self.current_alarm, Q88_SCALE / 2);
somatic.channel[8] = q88_add(somatic.channel[8], arousal_add);

// Channel 9 (valence): violation is negative
let valence_add = -(q88_mul(self.current_alarm, Q88_SCALE / 3));
somatic.channel[9] = q88_add(somatic.channel[9], valence_add);

// Channel 10 (fatigue): violation is exhausting
let fatigue_add = q88_mul(self.current_alarm, Q88_SCALE / 4);
somatic.channel[10] = q88_add(somatic.channel[10], fatigue_add);

// Channel 11 (velocity): violation is destabilizing
let velocity_add = q88_mul(self.current_alarm, Q88_SCALE / 5);
somatic.channel[11] = q88_add(somatic.channel[11], velocity_add);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Diagnostics
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// True when tampering is currently detected.
pub fn is_tampered(&self) -> bool {
self.current_alarm > Q88_SCALE
}

/// True when a full audit is in progress.
pub fn is_auditing(&self) -> bool {
self.audit_in_progress
}

/// Chain integrity as a percentage [0, 256].
pub fn chain_integrity(&self) -> i16 {
let total = self.verified_links + self.broken_links;
if total == 0 {
return 256; // perfect (no history yet)
}
((self.verified_links * 256) / total).min(256) as i16
}
}

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════==

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
use super::*;

#[test]
fn test_tamper_evidence_default() {
let te = TamperEvidence::new();
assert_eq!(te.head_hash, GENESIS_HASH);
assert_eq!(te.verified_links, 0);
assert_eq!(te.current_alarm, 0);
assert!(!te.is_tampered());
}

#[test]
fn test_hash_computation_is_deterministic() {
let te = TamperEvidence::new();
let somatic = SomaticField::default();

let h1 = te.compute_hash(1, &somatic, Basin::Rest, q88_from_i32(5), GENESIS_HASH);
let h2 = te.compute_hash(1, &somatic, Basin::Rest, q88_from_i32(5), GENESIS_HASH);

assert_eq!(h1, h2, "same input → same hash");
}

#[test]
fn test_hash_differs_with_state() {
let te = TamperEvidence::new();
let somatic1 = SomaticField::default();
let mut somatic2 = SomaticField::default();
somatic2.channel[0] = q88_from_i32(50);

let h1 = te.compute_hash(1, &somatic1, Basin::Rest, q88_from_i32(5), GENESIS_HASH);
let h2 = te.compute_hash(1, &somatic2, Basin::Rest, q88_from_i32(5), GENESIS_HASH);

assert_ne!(h1, h2, "different somatic → different hash");
}

#[test]
fn test_hash_chains() {
let mut te = TamperEvidence::new();
let somatic = SomaticField::default();

// First tick
te.integrity_cycle(1, &somatic, Basin::Rest, q88_from_i32(5));
let h1 = te.head_hash;

// Second tick
te.integrity_cycle(2, &somatic, Basin::Engaged, q88_from_i32(3));
let h2 = te.head_hash;

// Third tick
te.integrity_cycle(3, &somatic, Basin::Engaged, q88_from_i32(4));
let h3 = te.head_hash;

assert_ne!(h1, h2, "hashes chain: h1 != h2");
assert_ne!(h2, h3, "hashes chain: h2 != h3");
assert_ne!(h1, h3, "hashes chain: h1 != h3");
}

#[test]
fn test_verification_tracks_links() {
let mut te = TamperEvidence::new();
let somatic = SomaticField::default();

for i in 1..=10 {
te.integrity_cycle(i as u64, &somatic, Basin::Rest, q88_from_i32(5));
}

assert_eq!(te.verified_links, 10);
assert_eq!(te.broken_links, 0);
assert_eq!(te.chain_integrity(), 256); // 100%
}

#[test]
fn test_no_false_alarms_in_normal_operation() {
let mut te = TamperEvidence::new();
let somatic = SomaticField::default();

// Run many normal ticks — alarm should stay at 0
for i in 1..=20 {
te.integrity_cycle(i, &somatic, Basin::Rest, q88_from_i32(5));
}

println!("Alarm after 20 normal ticks: {}", te.current_alarm);
println!("Broken links: {}", te.broken_links);
assert_eq!(te.broken_links, 0, "no breaks in normal operation");
assert!(!te.is_tampered(), "should not be tampered in normal operation");
}

#[test]
fn test_alarm_decays() {
let mut te = TamperEvidence::new();
te.current_alarm = Q88_SCALE * 5; // force alarm
let before = te.current_alarm;

te.current_alarm = q88_sub(te.current_alarm, te.alarm_decay).max(0);

assert!(te.current_alarm < before, "alarm should decay");
}

#[test]
fn test_temporal_verify() {
let mut te = TamperEvidence::new();
let somatic = SomaticField::default();

// Fill window
for i in 1..=VERIFICATION_WINDOW {
te.integrity_cycle(i as u64, &somatic, Basin::Rest, q88_from_i32(5));
}

let breaks = te.temporal_verify();
assert_eq!(breaks, 0, "no breaks in clean chain");

// Now corrupt one link
te.window[3].prev_hash = 0xDEADBEEF;
let breaks = te.temporal_verify();
println!("Temporal breaks after corruption: {}", breaks);
assert!(breaks > 0 || te.current_alarm > 0, "corruption should be detected");
}

#[test]
fn test_full_audit() {
let mut te = TamperEvidence::new();
let somatic = SomaticField::default();

// Fill window
for i in 1..=VERIFICATION_WINDOW {
te.integrity_cycle(i as u64, &somatic, Basin::Rest, q88_from_i32(5));
}

// Initiate audit
te.initiate_full_audit();
assert!(te.is_auditing());

// Run audit to completion
for _ in 0..VERIFICATION_WINDOW + 1 {
te.integrity_cycle(100, &somatic, Basin::Rest, q88_from_i32(5));
}

assert!(!te.is_auditing(), "audit should complete");
}

#[test]
fn test_alarm_injection() {
let te = TamperEvidence {
current_alarm: Q88_SCALE * 4, // force alarm
..TamperEvidence::new()
};

let mut somatic = SomaticField::default();
let before_arousal = somatic.channel[8];
let before_valence = somatic.channel[9];

te.apply_alarm(&mut somatic);

assert!(somatic.channel[8] > before_arousal, "alarm increases arousal");
assert!(somatic.channel[9] < before_valence, "alarm decreases valence");
}

#[test]
fn test_chain_integrity() {
let mut te = TamperEvidence::new();
let somatic = SomaticField::default();

// 9 verified, 1 broken
te.verified_links = 9;
te.broken_links = 1;

let integrity = te.chain_integrity();
println!("Integrity: {}", integrity);
assert_eq!(integrity, (9 * 256) / 10, "integrity = verified / total");
}

#[test]
fn test_no_alarm_when_clean() {
let mut te = TamperEvidence::new();
let somatic = SomaticField::default();

// Many clean ticks
for i in 1..=20 {
te.integrity_cycle(i as u64, &somatic, Basin::Rest, q88_from_i32(5));
}

assert_eq!(te.current_alarm, 0, "no alarm when chain is clean");
assert!(!te.is_tampered());
}

let te = TamperEvidence {
current_penalty: Q88_SCALE * 3,
..TamperEvidence::new()
};
assert_eq!(te.current_penalty, Q88_SCALE * 3);
}
}
